===== PROC: TM_AlisSatisAylar =====


-- exec TM_AlisSatisAylar '2022','2023'
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_AlisSatisAylar](@BASTAR VARCHAR(4), @BITTAR VARCHAR(4))
AS
BEGIN 

DECLARE @BASTAR1 DATETIME, @BITTAR1 DATETIME
SET @BASTAR1 = @BASTAR
SET @BITTAR1 = @BITTAR
declare @TopTutar money

SET LANGUAGE Turkish
SELECT 
AY,
AYADI,    
[Alýþ]=SUM(TOPLAM_GIREN), 
[Satýþ]=SUM(TOPLAM_CIKAN)  
FROM (
SELECT   
AY=MONTH(FATURATARIH) , 
AYADI= DATENAME(month, FATURATARIH),

TOPLAM_GIREN=CASE WHEN TUR IN(11,12) THEN SUM(FATURA_MATRAHI) ELSE 0 END,
TOPLAM_CIKAN=CASE WHEN TUR IN(15,16) THEN SUM(FATURA_MATRAHI) ELSE 0 END
FROM  FATBASLIK
where year(FATURATARIH)= @BASTAR
group by TUR, MONTH(FATURATARIH), DATENAME(month, FATURATARIH)
 ) AS ASD
 GROUP BY 
 AY,
AYADI
 order by 1
SET LANGUAGE us_english


end


===== PROC: TM_AlisSatisYillar =====


-- exec TM_AlisSatisYillar '2020','2023'
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_AlisSatisYillar](@BASTAR VARCHAR(4), @BITTAR VARCHAR(4))
AS
BEGIN 
DECLARE @BASTAR1 DATETIME, @BITTAR1 DATETIME
SET @BASTAR1 = @BASTAR+'-01-01 00:00:00'
SET @BITTAR1 = @BITTAR+'-12-31 23:59:59'
declare @TopTutar money


select YIL, 
[Alýþ]=SUM(TOPLAM_GIREN), 
[Satýþ]=SUM(TOPLAM_CIKAN)  
from(
SELECT   
YIL=YEAR(FATURATARIH) , 
TOPLAM_GIREN=CASE WHEN TUR IN(11,12,13) THEN SUM(FATURA_MATRAHI) ELSE 0 END,
TOPLAM_CIKAN=CASE WHEN TUR IN(15,16,17) THEN SUM(FATURA_MATRAHI) ELSE 0 END
FROM  FATBASLIK
where 
FATURATARIH between @BASTAR1 and @BITTAR1
group by TUR, year(FATURATARIH)
) as YILLAR
group by YIL
 order by 1

end


===== PROC: TM_CariExtre =====


--SELECT * FROM GOREVYORUM WHERE ID>14153

--SELECT * FROM STOKFIYAT
--SELECT * FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Fiyat Liste Adý' AND  RB.YER_ID=R.ID



--  exec [TM_Rehber] '120.01.041','X',''

--	exec TM_CariExtre '1102','2024-01-01','2024-12-31','Detay'

CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_CariExtre](@REHBERID VARCHAR(50), @BASTARIH DATE, @BITTARIH DATE, @DetayOzet VARCHAR(10)= NULL)
AS
BEGIN 
IF @REHBERID LIKE 'KOD%'
BEGIN
	SELECT TOP 1 @REHBERID=CONVERT(VARCHAR(50),ID) FROM REHBER WHERE KOD=SUBSTRING(@REHBERID,4,LEN(@REHBERID))
END


IF @DetayOzet='Ozet'
begin
SELECT 
	KUR,
	TARIH, 
	TURAD=CASE WHEN ISNULL(TURAD,'')='' and ISNULL(KUR,'')='' THEN 'Genel Toplam :' 
			   WHEN ISNULL(TURAD,'')='' and ISNULL(KUR,'')<>'' THEN 'Toplam :' else TURAD end, 
	RESIM,
	CEKID,
	HESAPADI='',
	BORC,
	ALACAK,
	BORCBAKIYE=CASE WHEN (BORCBAKIYE IS NULL) AND (BORC-ALACAK>0) THEN BORC-ALACAK 
					WHEN (BORCBAKIYE IS NULL) AND (BORC-ALACAK<0) THEN 0 ELSE BORCBAKIYE END ,
	ALACAKBAKIYE=CASE WHEN (ALACAKBAKIYE IS NULL) AND (BORC-ALACAK<0) THEN BORC-ALACAK 
					  WHEN (ALACAKBAKIYE IS NULL) AND (BORC-ALACAK>0) THEN 0 ELSE ALACAKBAKIYE END  
FROM (
SELECT KUR,TARIH, TURAD, RESIM=CASE WHEN TUR IN (11,12,15,16) THEN 32 ELSE -1 END,CEKID,
		BORC=SUM(BORC),
		ALACAK=SUM(ALACAK), 
		BORCBAKIYE,--=SUM(BORCBAKIYE),
		ALACAKBAKIYE--=SUM(ALACAKBAKIYE) 
FROM (
 select 
	SIRANO,
	CEKID,
	TARIH=CONVERT(DATE,TARIH),
	AKSIYONTARIH,
	NO,
	TUR,
	BASLIK,
	TURAD,
	REHBERID,
	KOD,
	AD,
	--ACIKLAMA,
--	HESAPID,
--	HESAPKODU,
--	HESAPADI,
	DURUM,
	BORC,  
	ALACAK,
	KUR,
	YERELKUR,
	MASRAFID,
	MASRAFKOD,
	MASRAFAD,
	BORCBAKIYE ,
	ALACAKBAKIYE ,
	ABS(YERELTUTAR) AS YERELTUTAR,
	YERELBAKIYE,
	SUBEID,
	VADETARIHI,
	ADET,
	BIRIM,
	BIRIMFIYAT 
from  dbo.fn_Cari_Ekstre (@REHBERID,CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTARIH)+' 00:00:00',CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTARIH)+' 23:59:59',0
)
) DDD --order by KUR,TARIH
 
GROUP BY ROLLUP(KUR,TARIH, TURAD,TUR,CEKID,
	BORCBAKIYE ,
	ALACAKBAKIYE)) D1
WHERE
 (ALACAKBAKIYE IS NOT NULL OR ( TARIH IS NULL)) AND KUR IS NOT NULL
 
end else
begin
SELECT * FROM (
SELECT SIRANO=ISNULL(SIRANO,99),
	KUR,
	TARIH,
	CEKID,
	TURAD=CASE WHEN ISNULL(ACIKLAMA,'')<>'' THEN ACIKLAMA 
			   WHEN ISNULL(ACIKLAMA,'')='' AND ISNULL(HESAPADI,'')<>''  THEN HESAPADI 
			   WHEN ISNULL(KUR,'')<>'' AND ISNULL(TARIH,'')='' THEN 'Genel Toplam :' 
			   WHEN ISNULL(KUR,'')<>'' AND ISNULL(TARIH,'')<>'' AND ISNULL(CEKID,'')<>''  and ISNULL(TURAD,'')='' THEN 'Ara Toplam :' 
			   WHEN ISNULL(KUR,'')<>'' AND ISNULL(TARIH,'')<>'' and ISNULL(CEKID,'')='' THEN 'Toplam :' 
			   else TURAD end  , 
	RESIM,
	BORC,
	ALACAK,
	BORCBAKIYE=CASE WHEN (BORCBAKIYE IS NULL) AND (BORC-ALACAK>0) THEN BORC-ALACAK 
					WHEN (BORCBAKIYE IS NULL) AND (BORC-ALACAK<0) THEN 0 ELSE BORCBAKIYE END ,
	ALACAKBAKIYE=CASE WHEN (ALACAKBAKIYE IS NULL) AND (BORC-ALACAK<0) THEN BORC-ALACAK 
					  WHEN (ALACAKBAKIYE IS NULL) AND (BORC-ALACAK>0) THEN 0 ELSE ALACAKBAKIYE END  ,
	HESAPADI, ACIKLAMA, DETAYACIKLAMA
FROM (
SELECT SIRANO,KUR,TARIH, TURAD, RESIM=CASE WHEN TUR IN (11,12,15,16) THEN 32 ELSE -1 END,CEKID,HESAPADI,ACIKLAMA,DETAYACIKLAMA=ISNULL(CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),TARIH,104),'')+'         '+ISNULL(NO,'')+'         '+ISNULL(TURAD,''),
		BORC=SUM(BORC),
		ALACAK=SUM(ALACAK), 
		BORCBAKIYE,--=SUM(BORCBAKIYE),
		ALACAKBAKIYE--=SUM(ALACAKBAKIYE) 
FROM (
  select 
	SIRANO,CEKID,
	TARIH=CONVERT(DATE,TARIH),
	AKSIYONTARIH,NO,TUR,
	BASLIK,
	TURAD,--=ISNULL(TURAD,'')+' / '  +ISNULL(HESAPKODU,'')+' / '+ISNULL(HESAPADI,''),
	REHBERID,
	KOD,AD,ACIKLAMA,
	HESAPID,HESAPKODU,
	HESAPADI,DURUM,BORC,  
	ALACAK,KUR,YERELKUR,
	MASRAFID,MASRAFKOD,
	MASRAFAD,BORCBAKIYE,
	ALACAKBAKIYE,
	ABS(YERELTUTAR) AS YERELTUTAR,
	YERELBAKIYE,SUBEID,
	VADETARIHI,ADET,
	BIRIM,BIRIMFIYAT 
  from  
  dbo.fn_Cari_Detayli_Ekstre (@REHBERID,CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTARIH)+' 00:00:00',CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTARIH)+' 23:59:59') 
) DDD --order by KUR,TARIH
 
GROUP BY ROLLUP(KUR,TARIH,CEKID, TURAD,TUR,HESAPADI,ACIKLAMA,NO,SIRANO,
	BORCBAKIYE ,
	ALACAKBAKIYE)) D1
WHERE
((ALACAKBAKIYE IS NOT NULL OR TARIH IS NULL or CEKID IS NULL) AND (KUR IS NOT NULL)) 
  ) DDD WHERE TURAD<>'Toplam :' 
  ORDER BY KUR,  SIRANO
end


end


--	exec TM_CariExtre '1344','2024-01-01','2024-12-31'

===== PROC: TM_CariExtreDetay =====
 
--SELECT * FROM GOREVYORUM WHERE ID>14153

--SELECT * FROM STOKFIYAT
--SELECT * FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Fiyat Liste Adý' AND  RB.YER_ID=R.ID

 
--  exec [TM_Rehber] '120.01.041','X',''

--	exec [TM_CariExtreDetay] '47934' 

CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_CariExtreDetay](@ID INT)
AS
BEGIN 
SELECT
       ISNULL(S.KOD,MG.KOD) AS [Kod],
       ISNULL(S.STOKADI,MG.AD) AS [Ad],
       F.ACIKLAMA AS [Açýklama],
       F.ADET AS [Adet],
       G.ANAHTAR AS [Birim],
       F.BIRIMFIYAT AS [Birim Fiyat],
       F.ISKONTO AS [Ýsk1%],
       F.ISKONTO2 AS [Ýsk2%],
       F.KDV,
       ROUND(F.KDVDAHILFIYAT - F.TUTAR,2) AS [KDV Tutarý],
       F.TUTAR AS [Tutar],
	   F.KDVDAHILFIYAT AS [Tutar (KDV Dahil)],
       F.KUR AS [P.Birimi],
       F.DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT AS [Döviz Birim Fiyat],
       F.DOVIZ_KURU AS [Döviz Cinsi],	
	   ROUND((CASE 
				WHEN KDVDURUM ='Dahil' THEN ((DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT*ADET)*(100.0-ROUND(ISKONTO,G.DEGER))/100)-(((DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT*ADET)*(100.0-ROUND(ISKONTO,G.DEGER))/100)*(100.0/(100.0+F.KDV))) 
				WHEN F.OTVMIKTAR>0.0 then (((case when F.DOVIZ_KURU=FB.RAPORDOVIZ then F.DOVIZ_TUTARI else F.TUTAR/FB.DOVIZKUR end)+ ((CASE WHEN F.OTVYUZDE =0 THEN ADET*F.OTVMIKTAR ELSE (F.OTVMIKTAR*(((case when F.DOVIZ_KURU=FB.RAPORDOVIZ then F.DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT else F.BIRIMFIYAT/FB.DOVIZKUR end)*F.ADET)/100.0))  END)))*F.KDV/100.0)
				ELSE CAST((F.KDV*((case when F.DOVIZ_KURU=FB.RAPORDOVIZ then ((DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT*ADET)*(100.0-ROUND(ISKONTO,G.DEGER))/100) else F.TUTAR/FB.DOVIZKUR end)/100.0)) AS decimal(18,6))  
			 END),2) AS [Döviz KDV Tutarý],	   
       F.DOVIZ_TUTARI AS [Döviz Tutarý],
	   F.DOVIZ_TUTARI + 	   ROUND((CASE 
				WHEN KDVDURUM ='Dahil' THEN ((DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT*ADET)*(100.0-ROUND(ISKONTO,G.DEGER))/100)-(((DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT*ADET)*(100.0-ROUND(ISKONTO,G.DEGER))/100)*(100.0/(100.0+F.KDV))) 
				WHEN F.OTVMIKTAR>0.0 then (((case when F.DOVIZ_KURU=FB.RAPORDOVIZ then F.DOVIZ_TUTARI else F.TUTAR/FB.DOVIZKUR end)+ ((CASE WHEN F.OTVYUZDE =0 THEN ADET*F.OTVMIKTAR ELSE (F.OTVMIKTAR*(((case when F.DOVIZ_KURU=FB.RAPORDOVIZ then F.DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT else F.BIRIMFIYAT/FB.DOVIZKUR end)*F.ADET)/100.0))  END)))*F.KDV/100.0)
				ELSE CAST((F.KDV*((case when F.DOVIZ_KURU=FB.RAPORDOVIZ then ((DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT*ADET)*(100.0-ROUND(ISKONTO,G.DEGER))/100) else F.TUTAR/FB.DOVIZKUR end)/100.0)) AS decimal(18,6))  
			 END),2) AS [Döviz Tutarý (KDV Dahil)],
       F.DOVIZKURDEGERI AS [Döviz Kuru]
FROM
	FATURA F
		INNER JOIN FATBASLIK FB ON FB.ID = F.FATBASID
		LEFT JOIN STOKLAR S ON S.ID = F.URUNID AND F.TUR = 1
		LEFT JOIN MASRAFGELIR MG ON MG.ID = F.URUNID AND F.TUR = 0
		LEFT JOIN GENINI G ON G.BOLUM = -2702 AND G.DEGER = F.BIRIM
WHERE
       F.FATBASID = @ID

 
end



--	exec TM_CariExtre '1344','2024-01-01','2024-12-31'


===== PROC: TM_CiroRaporu =====


-- exec TM_SatisFirsatiPerformansRaporu '2023-01-01','2023-12-31'


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_CiroRaporu](@BASTAR DATETIME, @BITTAR DATETIME)
AS
BEGIN 


DECLARE 
	@DonemBasi DATETIME,
	@DonemSonu DATETIME  
SET @DonemBasi =  @BASTAR+' 00:00:00'
SET @DonemSonu =   @BITTAR+' 23:59:59'
SELECT
	SUBE,
	CAST(FATURA AS FLOAT) AS FATURA,
 	CAST(SGK AS FLOAT) AS SGK,
	CAST(FIS AS FLOAT) AS FIS,
	CAST(IADE AS FLOAT) AS IADE,
	CAST(TOPLAM AS FLOAT) AS TOPLAM
FROM(
	SELECT
		'Merkez' AS SUBE,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TUR = 15 AND FB.TIPI <> 2 AND FB.FATURASERI NOT IN ('FTH','USK','IZM') AND FB.BASLIK != 'SOSYAL GÜVENLÝK KURUMU BAÞKANLIÐI' THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) AS FATURA,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TUR = 15 AND FB.TIPI <> 2 AND FB.FATURASERI NOT IN ('FTH','USK','IZM') AND FB.BASLIK = 'SOSYAL GÜVENLÝK KURUMU BAÞKANLIÐI' THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) AS SGK,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TUR = 16 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) AS FIS,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TUR = 15 AND FB.TIPI = 2 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) AS IADE,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.FATURASERI NOT IN ('FTH','USK','IZM') AND FB.TIPI <> 2 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) AS TOPLAM
	FROM    
		FATBASLIK FB INNER JOIN FATURA F ON FB.ID=F.FATBASID       				   
	WHERE   
		FB.FATURATARIH BETWEEN @DonemBasi AND @DonemSonu AND FB.TUR IN (15,16) AND ADET <> 0.0
/*	UNION ALL
	SELECT
		'Bornova' AS SUBE,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TUR = 15 AND FB.TIPI <> 2 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) AS FATURA,
		ISNULL((SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN FB1.TUR = 15 AND FB1.TIPI <> 2 AND FB1.FATURASERI IN ('IZM') THEN CASE WHEN FB1.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F1.TUTAR ELSE F1.TUTAR/(1+(F1.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) FROM FATBASLIK FB1 INNER JOIN FATURA F1 ON FB1.ID = F1.FATBASID WHERE FB1.FATURATARIH BETWEEN @DonemBasi AND @DonemSonu AND FB1.TUR IN (15,16) AND F1.ADET <> 0.0),0) AS SGK,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TUR = 16 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) AS FIS,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TUR = 15 AND FB.TIPI = 2 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) AS IADE,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TIPI <> 2 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END)+
		ISNULL((SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN FB1.TUR = 15 AND FB1.TIPI <> 2 AND FB1.FATURASERI IN ('IZM') THEN CASE WHEN FB1.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F1.TUTAR ELSE F1.TUTAR/(1+(F1.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) FROM FATBASLIK FB1 INNER JOIN FATURA F1 ON FB1.ID = F1.FATBASID WHERE FB1.FATURATARIH BETWEEN @DonemBasi AND @DonemSonu AND FB1.TUR IN (15,16) AND F1.ADET <> 0.0),0) AS TOPLAM
	FROM    
		Venmed_Bornova.dbo.FATBASLIK FB INNER JOIN Venmed_Bornova.dbo.FATURA F ON FB.ID=F.FATBASID       
	--				 INNER JOIN Venmed_Bornova.dbo.STOKLAR S ON S.ID = F.URUNID AND F.TUR IN (1,11)  
	WHERE   
		FB.FATURATARIH BETWEEN @DonemBasi AND @DonemSonu AND FB.TUR IN (15,16)  AND ADET <> 0.0
	UNION ALL
	SELECT
		'Fatih' AS SUBE,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TUR = 15 AND FB.TIPI <> 2 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) AS FATURA,
		ISNULL((SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN FB1.TUR = 15 AND FB1.TIPI <> 2 AND FB1.FATURASERI IN ('FTH') THEN CASE WHEN FB1.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F1.TUTAR ELSE F1.TUTAR/(1+(F1.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) FROM FATBASLIK FB1 INNER JOIN FATURA F1 ON FB1.ID = F1.FATBASID WHERE FB1.FATURATARIH BETWEEN @DonemBasi AND @DonemSonu AND FB1.TUR IN (15,16) AND F1.ADET <> 0.0),0) AS SGK,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TUR = 16 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) AS FIS,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TUR = 15 AND FB.TIPI = 2 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) AS IADE,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TIPI <> 2 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END)+
		ISNULL((SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN FB1.TUR = 15 AND FB1.TIPI <> 2 AND FB1.FATURASERI IN ('FTH') THEN CASE WHEN FB1.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F1.TUTAR ELSE F1.TUTAR/(1+(F1.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) FROM FATBASLIK FB1 INNER JOIN FATURA F1 ON FB1.ID = F1.FATBASID WHERE FB1.FATURATARIH BETWEEN @DonemBasi AND @DonemSonu AND FB1.TUR IN (15,16) AND F1.ADET <> 0.0),0) AS TOPLAM
	FROM    
		Venmed_Fatih.dbo.FATBASLIK FB INNER JOIN Venmed_Fatih.dbo.FATURA F ON FB.ID=F.FATBASID       
	--				 INNER JOIN Venmed_Fatih.dbo.STOKLAR S ON S.ID = F.URUNID AND F.TUR IN (1,11)  
	WHERE   
		FB.FATURATARIH BETWEEN @DonemBasi AND @DonemSonu AND FB.TUR IN (15) AND ADET <> 0.0
	UNION ALL
	SELECT
		'Üsküdar' AS SUBE,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TUR = 15 AND FB.TIPI <> 2 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) AS FATURA,
		ISNULL((SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN FB1.TUR = 15 AND FB1.TIPI <> 2 AND FB1.FATURASERI IN ('USK') THEN CASE WHEN FB1.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F1.TUTAR ELSE F1.TUTAR/(1+(F1.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) FROM FATBASLIK FB1 INNER JOIN FATURA F1 ON FB1.ID = F1.FATBASID WHERE FB1.FATURATARIH BETWEEN @DonemBasi AND @DonemSonu AND FB1.TUR IN (15,16) AND F1.ADET <> 0.0),0) AS SGK,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TUR = 16 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) AS FIS,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TUR = 15 AND FB.TIPI = 2 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) AS IADE,
		SUM(CASE WHEN FB.TIPI <> 2 THEN CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F.TUTAR ELSE F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END)+
		ISNULL((SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN FB1.TUR = 15 AND FB1.TIPI <> 2 AND FB1.FATURASERI IN ('USK') THEN CASE WHEN FB1.KDVDURUM='Hariç' THEN F1.TUTAR ELSE F1.TUTAR/(1+(F1.KDV/100.0)) END ELSE 0 END) FROM FATBASLIK FB1 INNER JOIN FATURA F1 ON FB1.ID = F1.FATBASID WHERE FB1.FATURATARIH BETWEEN @DonemBasi AND @DonemSonu AND FB1.TUR IN (15,16) AND F1.ADET <> 0.0),0) AS TOPLAM
	FROM    
		Venmed_Uskudar.dbo.FATBASLIK FB INNER JOIN Venmed_Uskudar.dbo.FATURA F ON FB.ID=F.FATBASID       
	--				 INNER JOIN Venmed_Uskudar.dbo.STOKLAR S ON S.ID = F.URUNID AND F.TUR IN (1,11)  
	WHERE   
		FB.FATURATARIH BETWEEN @DonemBasi AND @DonemSonu AND FB.TUR IN (15) AND ADET <> 0.0
		*/
) AS X
end


===== PROC: TM_CiroYuzdeDagilim =====


-- exec TM_CiroYuzdeDagilim '2023-01-01','2023-12-31','100'
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_CiroYuzdeDagilim](@BASTAR DATETIME, @BITTAR DATETIME,@Yuzde float=100)
AS
BEGIN 

SET @BASTAR = @BASTAR+' 00:00:00'
SET @BITTAR = @BITTAR+' 23:59:59'
declare @TopTutar money


SELECT @TopTutar=SUM(FATURA_MATRAHI) 
FROM FATBASLIK FB INNER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID=FB.REHBERID AND TUR=15
WHERE FATURATARIH>=@BASTAR
AND FATURATARIH<=@BITTAR
having SUM(FATURA_MATRAHI)>0

SELECT FIRMA,TUTAR,ORAN,KUMULATIF FROM (
SELECT R.FIRMA, SUM(FATURA_MATRAHI) AS TUTAR,@TopTutar AS TOPLAMTUTAR,
ORAN=100.0*SUM(FATURA_MATRAHI)/@TopTutar,
KUMULATIF= sum(100.0*SUM(FATURA_MATRAHI)/@TopTutar) over(order by SUM(FATURA_MATRAHI)DESC) 
FROM FATBASLIK FB  INNER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID=FB.REHBERID AND TUR=15
WHERE FATURATARIH>=@BASTAR
AND FATURATARIH<=@BITTAR
GROUP BY R.FIRMA
having SUM(FATURA_MATRAHI)>0
)AS XX where KUMULATIF <=@Yuzde
ORDER BY 2 DESC
end


===== PROC: TM_CrmIstatistik =====


-- exec TM_CrmIstatistik  
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_CrmIstatistik]
AS
BEGIN 
select   
	AY= case DATENAME(MM,R.EKLEMETARIHI) WHEN 'January' THEN '1-Ocak'
        WHEN 'February' THEN '2-Þubat'
        WHEN 'March' THEN '3-Mart'
        WHEN 'April' THEN '4-Nisan'
        WHEN 'May' THEN '5-Mayýs'
        WHEN 'June' THEN '6-Haziran'
        WHEN 'July' THEN '7-Temmuz'
        WHEN 'August' THEN '8-Aðustos'
        WHEN 'September' THEN '9-Eylül'
        WHEN 'October' THEN '10-Ekim'
        WHEN 'November' THEN '11-Kasým'
        WHEN 'December' THEN '12-Aralýk'	end,
	G.ANAHTAR,
	SAYI=COUNT(R.ID)
from REHBER R INNER JOIN GENINI G ON BOLUM = -2200 AND DEGER=R.GRUP
where 		
	DATEPART(YEAR,R.EKLEMETARIHI)=DATEPART(YEAR,GETDATE())
GROUP BY DATENAME(MM,R.EKLEMETARIHI), G.ANAHTAR

UNION ALL


SELECT 
	AY=case DATENAME(MM,R.EKLEMETARIHI)  WHEN 'January' THEN '1-Ocak'
        WHEN 'February' THEN '2-Þubat'
        WHEN 'March' THEN '3-Mart'
        WHEN 'April' THEN '4-Nisan'
        WHEN 'May' THEN '5-Mayýs'
        WHEN 'June' THEN '6-Haziran'
        WHEN 'July' THEN '7-Temmuz'
        WHEN 'August' THEN '8-Aðustos'
        WHEN 'September' THEN '9-Eylül'
        WHEN 'October' THEN '10-Ekim'
        WHEN 'November' THEN '11-Kasým'
        WHEN 'December' THEN '12-Aralýk'		end	,
	ANAHTAR='Görevler',
	SAYI=COUNT(R.ID)
FROM GOREVLER R
where 		
	DATEPART(YEAR,R.EKLEMETARIHI)=DATEPART(YEAR,GETDATE())
GROUP BY DATENAME(MM,R.EKLEMETARIHI)
ORDER BY 2, 1



end


===== PROC: TM_DashBoard =====







--  exec [TM_DashBoard]


create procedure [dbo].[TM_DashBoard]
AS
BEGIN 
DECLARE @BUGUN DATEtime , @RehberId FLOAT=2, @gunsay int=-90


set @Bugun=convert(datetime,convert(varchar(10), getdate(), 120))

select distinct ID,TUR,KATEGORI,DUYURUAD=CONVERT(VARCHAR(5),ID)+'-'+DUYURUAD, DURURUIMAGE=CASE WHEN DUYURUAD='Doðum Günü' THEN 0 
															WHEN DUYURUAD='Çek Tahsilatý' THEN 1 ELSE -1 END,
KONU,
OLAYZAMANI, 
ZAMAN=(select case 
when convert(float,OLAYZAMANI-@Bugun)<-1 then   cast(abs(convert(int,OLAYZAMANI-@Bugun)) as varchar(8))+' gün önce'
when convert(float,OLAYZAMANI-@Bugun)=-1 then 'Dün'
when convert(float,OLAYZAMANI-@Bugun) between 0.0 and 0.9999  then 'Bugün'
when convert(float,OLAYZAMANI-@Bugun)=1 then 'Yarýn'
when convert(float,OLAYZAMANI-@Bugun)>1 then cast(convert(int,OLAYZAMANI-@Bugun) as varchar(8))+' gün sonra'
else
''
end) ,
GUN=  convert(smallint,OLAYZAMANI-@Bugun),REHBERID,EKLEYEN, YER=isnull(YER,0), YER_ID=isnull(YER_ID,0)
--Data kaynaðý burasý
from ( 
select   D.ID,D.TUR,KATEGORI,DUYURUAD=U.ACIKLAMA,D.KONU,OLAYZAMANI, 
REHBERID=null,D.EKLEYEN, D.YER, D.YER_ID 
from DUYURU D 
	 left join UYARIAYAR U on D.SABLONID=U.SABLONDUYURUID
	 left join DUYURUKULLANICI DK on DK.TUR=0 and D.TUR=2  and DK.DUYURUID=D.ID

where
OKUNMATARIHI is null
and DK.ALICIID= @RehberId 
--and (D.KONU like @Konu or U.ACIKLAMA like @Konu)
--and D.GECERLILIKTARIHI < getdate()  
--and OLAYZAMANI-@Bugun>-10

UNION ALL

select   ID,TUR=-1,KATEGORI=LISTEID,DUYURUAD='Görev',KONU=KONUSU,OLAYZAMANI=BITISTARIHI, 
REHBERID,EKLEYEN, YER=33, YER_ID=ID
FROM [dbo].[fn_prg_IsListesiBanaAtananlar] (@RehberId,0,9999)

--UNION ALL

--select   ID,TUR=-10,KATEGORI=T.ID,DUYURUAD='Teklif Onay',
--KONU=(SELECT FIRMA FROM REHBER R WHERE R.ID=T.REHBERID),
--OLAYZAMANI=DURUMTARIHI, 
--REHBERID,EKLEYEN
--FROM TEKLIF T
--WHERE isnull(T.ONAYLAYAN,0)=0
--and T.ONAYLAYACAK=@RehberId

--UNION ALL

--select ID,TUR=-10,KATEGORI=T.ID,DUYURUAD='Teklif SKT',
--	Konu='Bu teklifin geçerliliði '+ 
--	Convert(varchar(10),ABS(case when ISNULL(T.GECERLILIK_SURESI,0)=0 then 0 else DATEDIFF(day,GETDATE(),T.TARIH+GECERLILIK_SURESI) end))+
--	' gün önce sona erdi. Durumunu deðiþtirin!',
--	OLAYZAMANI=T.TARIH+GECERLILIK_SURESI,
--	REHBERID,EKLEYEN

--from TEKLIF T
--where T.EKLEYEN=@RehberId and T.DURUM=(Select min(DEGER) from GENINI where BOLUM=-2902) and
--	0>case when ISNULL(T.GECERLILIK_SURESI,0)=0 then 0  else DATEDIFF(day,GETDATE(),T.TARIH+GECERLILIK_SURESI) end

UNION ALL

select   S.ID,TUR=-20,KATEGORI=S.ID,DUYURUAD='Servis - '+(select ANAHTAR from GENINI where BOLUM=-3007 and DEGER=S.DURUM),
KONU=S.KONUSU+' - '+(SELECT FIRMA FROM REHBER R WHERE R.ID=S.REHBERID),
OLAYZAMANI=S.EKLEMETARIHI, 
REHBERID,S.EKLEYEN, YER=83, YER_ID=S.ID
FROM 
	SERVIS S inner join 
	(select * from (
		select * ,SIRANO=Row_Number() over(partition by SH.SERVISID order by SH.ID desc)
		from SERVISHAREKET SH )as AA
	where SIRANO=1) as BB on S.ID=BB.SERVISID
WHERE BB.PERSONEL=@RehberId and S.ACKAPA=0

--UNION ALL

--select   S.ID,TUR=-20,KATEGORI=S.ID,DUYURUAD='Servis - '+(select ANAHTAR from GENINI where BOLUM=-3007 and DEGER=S.DURUM),
--KONU=S.KONUSU+' - '+(SELECT FIRMA FROM REHBER R WHERE R.ID=S.REHBERID),
--OLAYZAMANI=S.EKLEMETARIHI, 
--REHBERID,S.EKLEYEN,YER=83, YER_ID=S.ID
--FROM 
--	SERVIS S inner join  SERVISHAREKET SH  on S.ID=SH.SERVISID
--WHERE SH.PERSONEL=@RehberId and S.ACKAPA=0 and SH.BITIS is null

) as Liste

where
   convert(smallint,OLAYZAMANI-@Bugun) > @GunSay

ORDER BY convert(smallint,OLAYZAMANI-@Bugun)
end


===== PROC: TM_DokumanGuncelleListele =====



-- EXEC TM_DokumanGuncelleListele 1,-7
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_DokumanGuncelleListele](@TIP VARCHAR(20), @ID INT, @TARIH DATE, @DURUM VARCHAR(50), @YON VARCHAR(50), @ADI VARCHAR(250), @KONUSU VARCHAR(150), @KATEGORI VARCHAR(150), @AITKURUM VARCHAR(50), @AITDEMIRBAS VARCHAR(150), @AITDEPARTMAN VARCHAR(150), @ORJLOKASYON VARCHAR(150), @ARSIVSURE INT, @ARSIVSURETIP VARCHAR(50), @GIZLILIK VARCHAR(50), @ANAHTARKELIME VARCHAR(250), @SORUMLU VARCHAR(50), @EKLEYEN VARCHAR(50), @KLASOR VARCHAR(10), @BELGE VARCHAR(MAX), @BELGETURU VARCHAR(50))
AS
DECLARE @IMAJID INT, @BELGEB VARBINARY(MAX)
SET @BELGEB=CONVERT(varbinary(MAX),@BELGE)
BEGIN 
IF @TIP='Listele'
BEGIN
	SELECT 
		D.ID,
		D.BELGENO,	
		D.TARIH,
		I.SURUM,
		DURUM=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -1001 AND DEGER=D.DURUM),
		YON=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -3205 AND DEGER=D.YON),
		D.AD,
		D.KONU,
		KATEGORI=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -3204 AND DEGER=D.KATEGORI),
		AITOLDUGUKURUM=(SELECT TOP 1 FIRMA FROM REHBER R WHERE R.ID=D.REHBERID),
		D.REHBERID,
		AITOLDUGUDEMIRBAS=(SELECT TOP 1 DB.DEMIRBASADI FROM DEMIRBAS DB WHERE DB.ID=D.DEMIRBASID),
		D.DEMIRBASID,
		AITOLDUGUDEPARTMAN=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -2251 AND DEGER=D.BOLUM),
		LOKASYONADI=(SELECT TOP 1 L.ACIKLAMA FROM LOKASYON L WHERE TUR=2  AND D.LOKASYON=L.ID),
		LOKASYON,
		ARSIVSURESI,
		ARSIVSURETIPI=CASE WHEN ISNULL(ARSIVSURETIPI,0)=365 THEN 'Yýl'
							WHEN ISNULL(ARSIVSURETIPI,0)=30 THEN 'Ay'		
							WHEN ISNULL(ARSIVSURETIPI,0)=1 THEN 'Gün' else '' end,
		GIZLILIKDERECESI=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -3206 AND DEGER=D.GIZLILIKDERECESI),
		D.ANAHTAR,
		SORUMLU=(SELECT TOP 1 FIRMA FROM REHBER R WHERE R.ID=I.REHBERID),
		SORUMLUID=I.REHBERID,
		KLASOR
	FROM DOKUMAN D LEFT OUTER JOIN IMAJ I ON D.ID=I.YER_ID
	WHERE D.ID=@ID
END

IF @TIP='Guncelle'
BEGIN
IF @ID<>0
begin
	update D SET  				
		D.DURUM=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -1001 AND ANAHTAR=@DURUM),
		D.YON=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -3205 AND ANAHTAR=@YON),
		D.AD=@ADI,
		D.KONU=@KONUSU,
		KATEGORI=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -3204 AND ANAHTAR=@KATEGORI),
		D.REHBERID=@AITKURUM,				
		D.DEMIRBASID=@AITDEMIRBAS,
		D.BOLUM=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -2251 AND ANAHTAR=@AITDEPARTMAN),		
		LOKASYON=@ORJLOKASYON,
		ARSIVSURESI=@ARSIVSURE,
		ARSIVSURETIPI=CASE WHEN @ARSIVSURETIP='Yýl' THEN 365
							WHEN @ARSIVSURETIP='Ay' THEN 30
							WHEN @ARSIVSURETIP='Gün' THEN 1 else 0 end,
		GIZLILIKDERECESI=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -3206 AND ANAHTAR=@GIZLILIK),
		D.ANAHTAR=@ANAHTARKELIME		,
		TARIH=@TARIH,
		KLASOR=@KLASOR,
		DEGISTIREN=@EKLEYEN,
		DEGISTIRMETARIHI=GETDATE()
	FROM DOKUMAN D 
	WHERE D.ID=@ID
	update I SET  		
		I.REHBERID=@SORUMLU
	FROM DOKUMAN D INNER JOIN IMAJ I ON D.ID=I.YER_ID
	WHERE D.ID=@ID
end else
begin
INSERT INTO DOKUMAN(DURUM, YON, AD, KONU, KATEGORI, REHBERID, DEMIRBASID, BOLUM, LOKASYON, ARSIVSURESI, ARSIVSURETIPI, GIZLILIKDERECESI, ANAHTAR, TARIH, EKLEYEN, EKLEMETARIHI, KLASOR, SUBEID, MODUL) 				
SELECT DURUM=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -1001 AND ANAHTAR=@DURUM),
		YON=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -3205 AND ANAHTAR=@YON),
		AD=@ADI,
		KONU=@KONUSU,
		KATEGORI=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -3204 AND ANAHTAR=@KATEGORI),
		REHBERID=@AITKURUM,				
		DEMIRBASID=@AITDEMIRBAS,
		BOLUM=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -2251 AND ANAHTAR=@AITDEPARTMAN),		
		LOKASYON=@ORJLOKASYON,
		ARSIVSURESI=@ARSIVSURE,
		ARSIVSURETIPI=CASE WHEN @ARSIVSURETIP='Yýl' THEN 365
							WHEN @ARSIVSURETIP='Ay' THEN 30
							WHEN @ARSIVSURETIP='Gün' THEN 1 else 0 end,
		GIZLILIKDERECESI=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -3206 AND ANAHTAR=@GIZLILIK),
		ANAHTAR=@ANAHTARKELIME		,
		TARIH=@TARIH,
		EKLEYEN=@EKLEYEN,
		EKLEMETARIHI=GETDATE(),
		KLASOR=@KLASOR,
		SUBEID=-1,
		MODUL=210
		SET @ID = (SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY())		

		INSERT INTO IMAJ(YER_ID, REHBERID, YERI, BELGE, EKLEMETARIHI, EKLEYEN, BELGETURU, ICDIS)
		SELECT 	@ID,REHBERID=@SORUMLU,YERI=1, CONVERT(varbinary(MAX),@BELGE), GETDATE(),@EKLEYEN,REPLACE(@BELGETURU,'.',''),1
		SET @IMAJID = (SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY())		
		
		SET @BELGEB  = (SELECT TOP 1   BELGE FROM IMAJ WHERE ID=@IMAJID) 

		EXEC sp_Imaj_Kaydetme @IMAJID, @BELGEB  



		DECLARE  				
				@DOSYAADI	VARCHAR(255),
				@DIZIN		VARCHAR(255)				
		SET @DOSYAADI =SUBSTRING('000000',1,6-LEN(@IMAJID))+CONVERT(varchar(10),@IMAJID)+'.OBJ'
		SET @DIZIN    =ISNULL((SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-10006),'')+''+
							  (SELECT CONVERT(varchar(10),(DATEPART (YY,EKLEMETARIHI))) FROM IMAJ WHERE ID=@IMAJID)+'-'+
							  (SELECT SUBSTRING('0',1,2-LEN(DATEPART(MM,EKLEMETARIHI))) FROM IMAJ WHERE ID=@IMAJID)+
							  (SELECT CONVERT(varchar(10),(DATEPART (MM,EKLEMETARIHI))) FROM IMAJ WHERE ID=@IMAJID)
		SET @DIZIN=@DIZIN+'\'
		SET @DIZIN=@DIZIN+@DOSYAADI




		DECLARE @cmd nvarchar(4000)
		SET @cmd = 'powershell.exe -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File C:\TMServis\ConvertToBase64.ps1 -base64FilePath "'+@DIZIN+'" -binaryFilePath "'+@DIZIN+'"'

		EXEC xp_cmdshell @cmd
		UPDATE IMAJ SET BELGE=NULL WHERE ID=@IMAJID
		
end

	exec [TM_DokumanGuncelleListele] 'Listele',@ID,'','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','',''
END
 
end



===== PROC: TM_DokumanKlasor =====


--exec [TM_DokumanKlasor]

CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_DokumanKlasor] 
AS
BEGIN 
SELECT SIRA=convert(float,replace(CONCAT(IsNulL(dk.ustID,0),Dk.Id),'-','')) ,
	SIRA1=convert(float,substring(replace(CONCAT(IsNulL(dk.ustID,0),Dk.Id),'-',''),1,3)) ,
	dk.ID, 
	dk.USTID, 
	dk.TUR, 
	dk.RESIM, 
	dk.AD, 
	dk.DURUM
	
FROM DOKUMANKLASOR Dk --LEFT OUTeR JoIN DOKUMANKLASOR DKUST ON Dk.ID = DKUST.USTID and DKUST.DURUM =1
WHERE 
dk.DURUM=1 --AND SUBEID=0 

--and ISNULL(dkust.Id,'')=''
ORDER BY USTID ,ad  end



===== PROC: TM_DokumanYetki =====




-- EXEC [TM_DokumanYetki] 1,-7
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_DokumanYetki](@REHBERID INT, @KLASORID INT)
AS
BEGIN 

WITH Dizin AS
(
    SELECT ID,USTID,AD=CAST(AD AS NVARCHAR(260))
    FROM DOKUMANKLASOR
    WHERE USTID=0
    UNION ALL
    SELECT A.ID
    , A.USTID,
    AD=CAST(V.AD+'\'+A.AD AS NVARCHAR(260))
    FROM DOKUMANKLASOR A
    INNER JOIN Dizin V ON
        V.ID = A.USTID
)

select distinct
	D.*,
	DTIP=1,
	KISAYOLID=0,
	Firma.FIRMA as KURUM,
	Lokasyon.ACIKLAMA as LOKASYONAD,
	Sorumlu.FIRMA as SORUMLUAD,
	EXT='.'+I.BELGETURU,
	I.SURUM,
	I.BOYUT,
	IMAJID=I.ID,
	KLASORAD=(SELECT AD FROM Dizin where ID=D.KLASOR),
	ONAYLAYACAKAD=(SELECT FIRMA FROM REHBER where ID=I.ONAYLAYACAK),
	ONAYLAYANAD=(SELECT FIRMA FROM REHBER where ID=I.ONAY),
	EKLEYENAD=(SELECT FIRMA FROM REHBER where ID=D.EKLEYEN),
	DEGISTIRENAD=(SELECT FIRMA FROM REHBER where ID=D.DEGISTIREN)
from DOKUMAN D
 INNER JOIN IMAJ I ON I.ID = (select top 1 ID from IMAJ where YERI=1 AND YER_ID=D.ID order by ID desc)
 LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER Firma on Firma.ID=D.REHBERID
 LEFT OUTER JOIN LOKASYON AS Lokasyon ON Lokasyon.ID=D.LOKASYON  
 LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER AS Sorumlu ON Sorumlu.ID = I.REHBERID
 LEFT OUTER JOIN DOKUMANYETKI DY ON DY.YERI=321 AND DY.YERID = D.ID 
Where D.KLASOR =@KLASORID 

 union all 

select 
  distinct   D.*,DTIP=0,KISAYOLID=DK.ID,Firma.FIRMA as KURUM,Lokasyon.ACIKLAMA as LOKASYONAD,Sorumlu.FIRMA as 
SORUMLUAD,
EXT=case when D.AD like '%.%' then '.'+REVERSE( SUBSTRING(REVERSE(isnull(D.AD,'.')),1,CHARINDEX('.',REVERSE(isnull
(D.AD,'.')),1)-1)) else '' end,I.SURUM,
I.BOYUT,
IMAJID=I.ID,
KLASORAD=(SELECT AD FROM Dizin where ID=D.KLASOR),
ONAYLAYACAKAD=(SELECT FIRMA FROM REHBER where ID=I.ONAYLAYACAK),
ONAYLAYANAD=(SELECT FIRMA FROM REHBER where ID=I.ONAY),
EKLEYENAD=(SELECT FIRMA FROM REHBER where ID=D.EKLEYEN),
DEGISTIRENAD=(SELECT FIRMA FROM REHBER where ID=D.DEGISTIREN)
from 
	DOKUMANKISAYOL DK 
	inner join DOKUMAN D on DK.DOKUMANID=D.ID
	INNER JOIN IMAJ I ON I.ID = (select top 1 ID from IMAJ where YERI=1 AND YER_ID=D.ID order by ID desc)
 	LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER Firma on Firma.ID=D.REHBERID
 	LEFT OUTER JOIN LOKASYON AS Lokasyon ON  Lokasyon.ID=D.LOKASYON  
 	LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER AS Sorumlu ON Sorumlu.ID = I.REHBERID
 Where DK.YER=321 and DK.YER_ID =@KLASORID
 
end



===== PROC: TM_Dokumler =====


-- exec TM_MizanToplu '2023-01-01','2023-12-31'
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_Dokumler](@BASTAR varchar(10), @BITTAR varchar(10), @RaporId INT)
AS
BEGIN 

IF @RaporId=1 BEGIN EXEC TM_SatisFirsatiPerformansRaporu @BASTAR, @BITTAR END
IF @RaporId=2 BEGIN EXEC TM_MizanToplu @BASTAR, @BITTAR END
IF @RaporId=3 BEGIN EXEC TM_KdvOzet @BASTAR, @BITTAR END
IF @RaporId=4 BEGIN EXEC TM_IsListesiPerformansRaporu @BASTAR, @BITTAR END
IF @RaporId=5 BEGIN EXEC TM_CiroYuzdeDagilim @BASTAR, @BITTAR END
IF @RaporId=6 BEGIN EXEC TM_AlisSatisYillar @BASTAR, @BITTAR END
IF @RaporId=7 BEGIN EXEC TM_AlisSatisAylar @BASTAR, @BITTAR END
IF @RaporId=8 BEGIN EXEC TM_CiroRaporu @BASTAR, @BITTAR END

end


===== PROC: TM_DonusumListeleri =====





--sue=sipariþ üretim emri
--suf=sipariþ üretim fiþi
--igk=Ýrsaliye => Gelen Konsinye
--ivs=Ýrsaliye => Verilen Sipariþ
--uel=üretim emri listesi (5 ci buton olacak)

--  exec [TM_DonusumListeleri] '2024-04-01', '2025-05-30','siparis',0,0


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_DonusumListeleri](@BasTarihi VARCHAR(10), @BitTarihi VARCHAR(10), @ekran varchar(10), @gizlenen int, @kalmayan int, @ekleyen varchar(50)=null)
AS
BEGIN 
DECLARE @SQL VARCHAR(MAX)

if @ekran like 'Alis%'
begin
	exec TM_DonusumListeleriAlis @BasTarihi , @BitTarihi , @ekran , @gizlenen , @kalmayan 
end
if @ekran like 'Satis%'
begin
	exec TM_DonusumListeleriSatis @BasTarihi , @BitTarihi , @ekran , @gizlenen , @kalmayan 
end

if @ekran='uel'
begin
set @sql='
select 
	Durum=(select ANAHTAR from GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -1001 and DIL = -1 AND DEGER=U.DURUM),
	U.EMIRNO, 
	U.ID,
	UDID=UD.ID,
	EMIRTURU=(select ANAHTAR from GENINI where BOLUM<>0 and BOLUM = -2488 and DIL = -1 AND DEGER=U.EMIRTURU),
	FIRMAAD = R.FIRMA,
	U.SIPARIS_NO,
	SP.SIPARISTARIH,
	SPD.TESLIMTARIHI,
	EMIRTARIHI=u.TALEPTARIHI,
	BASLAMA=U.BASTAR,
	BITIS=U.BITTAR,
	STOKKODU=S.KOD,
	S.URUNNO,
	S.STOKADI,  
	ANAKAYNAKAD = L.ACIKLAMA,
	TALEPEDEN=(select R.FIRMA from REHBER R where TALEPEDEN=R.ID),
	ONAYLAYACAK=(select R.FIRMA from REHBER R where U.ONAYLAYACAK=R.ID),
	ONAY=CASE WHEN ISNULL(U.ONAY,0)=0 THEN ''Onaysýz'' else ''Onaylý'' end,
	ONAYLAYAN=(select R.FIRMA from REHBER R where U.ONAYLAYAN=R.ID),
	U.ADET,
	BIRIM=(SELECT top 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2702 AND DEGER=U.BIRIM),
	ACIKLAMA=U.ACIKLAMA,	
	PROJEKODU = P.PROJEKODU,
	S.OZELKOD,
	DISKAYNAKOLAY=CASE WHEN UF.OLAY=0 THEN ''Çýkýþ'' WHEN UF.OLAY=1 THEN  ''Giriþ'' else '''' end,
	DISKAYNAKTARIH=UF.TARIH,
	DISKAYNAKCARI=R1.FIRMA,
	DISKAYNAKMIKTAR=CASE WHEN ISNULL(UF.OLAY,0)=0 THEN UF.CIKAN else UF.GIREN end,
	YORUMMEDYATARIH=GY.EKLEMETARIHI,
	YORUMMEDYAYORUM=TRIM(char(10) from GY.YORUM),
	YORUMMEDYAEKLEYEN=(SELECT TOP 1 R.FIRMA FROM REHBER R WHERE R.ID = GY.EKLEYEN),
	DETAYBUTON=23,
	GOREVID=GY.GOREVID,
	FASONBUTON=23,
	YORUMSAY=(SELECT COUNT(GY1.ID) FROM GOREVYORUM GY1 LEFT OUTER JOIN URETIMOPERASYON UOP on GY1.GOREVID=UOP.ID WHERE UOP.URETIMEMRIID=U.ID AND GY1.TUR=142),
	FASONSAY=(SELECT COUNT(ID) FROM URETIMOPERASYONFASON UF1 WHERE UF1.URETIMEMRIID=U.ID ),
	FASONTIPI=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2799 AND DEGER=UF.FASONTIPI)
from 
	URETIMEMRI U
		left join URETIMEMRIDETAY UD ON UD.URETIMEMRIID = U.ID AND U.STOKID = UD.URUNID
		left join STOKLAR S on S.ID=U.STOKID
		left join REHBER R ON R.ID = U.REHBERID
		left join LOKASYON L ON L.ID = U.ANAKAYNAK
		left join SIPARIS SP ON SP.SIPARISNO = U.SIPARIS_NO
		left join SIPARISDETAY SPD ON SPD.SIPARISID = SP.ID AND SPD.URUNID = U.STOKID AND SPD.ID = UD.YERID AND UD.YERI = 93
		left join PROJELER P ON P.ID = SPD.PROJEID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN URETIMOPERASYONFASON UF ON UF.URETIMEMRIID=U.ID AND UF.ID=(SELECT TOP 1 ID FROM URETIMOPERASYONFASON UF1 WHERE UF1.URETIMEMRIID=U.ID ORDER BY TARIH DESC)
		LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R1 ON R1.ID = UF.REHBERID		
		LEFT OUTER JOIN GOREVYORUM GY ON GY.TUR=142 and GY.ID=(SELECT TOP 1 GY1.ID FROM GOREVYORUM GY1 LEFT OUTER JOIN URETIMOPERASYON UOP on GY1.GOREVID=UOP.ID WHERE UOP.URETIMEMRIID=U.ID AND GY1.TUR=142   ORDER BY GY1.EKLEMETARIHI DESC)	
		
where 
	1=1 
	and convert(date,U.BASTAR ,102)  between convert(date,'''+@BasTarihi+''',102) and convert(date,'''+@BitTarihi+''',102)
	
	'
	if @kalmayan=0 begin set @sql=@sql+'and U.DURUM > 0' end 
	EXEC (@SQL)
end


if @ekran='siparis'
begin
set @sql='
SELECT
	S.ID,
	S.SIPARISTARIH,
	SD.ACIKLAMA,
	FIRMA=SUBSTRING(R.FIRMA,1,10),
	ST.URUNNO,
	ST.OZELKOD,
	SD.POZNO,
	ST.KOD,
	STOKADI=SUBSTRING(ST.STOKADI,1,50),
	S.SIPARISNO,
	SD.TESLIMTARIHI,
	SIPARIS=SD.ADET,
	DONUSEN=(ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1 where F1.YERI=409 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)) + ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERID=SD.ID AND F1.URUNID = SD.URUNID),0.0))),
	KALAN=SD.ADET-(ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1 where F1.YERI=409 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)) + ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERID=SD.ID AND F1.URUNID = SD.URUNID),0.0))),
	US=CASE WHEN P.PROJEADI = ''STOKTAN'' THEN ''S'' WHEN P.PROJEADI = ''ÜRETÝLECEK'' THEN ''Ü'' ELSE '''' END,
	[NOT]=S.ACIKLAMA,
	OZELKODU1=S.OZELKOD,
	YORUMMEDYATARIH=GY.EKLEMETARIHI,
	YORUMMEDYAYORUM=TRIM(char(10) from GY.YORUM),
	YORUMMEDYAEKLEYEN=(SELECT TOP 1 R.FIRMA FROM REHBER R WHERE R.ID = GY.EKLEYEN),
	DETAYBUTON=23,
	GOREVID=GY.GOREVID,
	GIZLI=CASE WHEN ISNULL((select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=S.TUR and HEDEFTUR=14 and KAYNAKID=SD.ID),'''')='''' THEN '''' ELSE ''Gizli'' end,
	FASONBUTON=23,
	YORUMMEDYATARIHSATIR=GY1.EKLEMETARIHI,
	YORUMMEDYAYORUMSATIR=TRIM(char(10) from GY1.YORUM),
	YORUMMEDYAEKLEYENSATIR=(SELECT TOP 1 R.FIRMA FROM REHBER R WHERE R.ID = GY1.EKLEYEN),
	DETAYBUTONSATIR=23,
	SIPARISDETAYID=SD.ID,
	YORUMSAY=(SELECT COUNT(ID) FROM GOREVYORUM GY1 WHERE GY1.TUR=91 AND GY1.GOREVID=S.ID ),
	YORUMSAYSATIR=(SELECT COUNT(ID) FROM GOREVYORUM GY2 WHERE GY2.TUR=93 AND GY2.GOREVID=SD.ID )
FROM 
	SIPARIS S 
	INNER JOIN SIPARISDETAY SD on S.ID=SD.SIPARISID 
    INNER JOIN STOKLAR ST on SD.TUR=1 and SD.URUNID=ST.ID 
    INNER JOIN REHBER R on R.ID=S.REHBERID
	LEFT JOIN PROJELER P ON P.ID = SD.PROJEID
	LEFT OUTER JOIN GOREVYORUM GY ON GY.TUR=91 AND GY.GOREVID=S.ID AND GY.ID=(SELECT TOP 1 ID FROM GOREVYORUM GY1 WHERE GY1.TUR=91 AND GY1.GOREVID=S.ID ORDER BY EKLEMETARIHI DESC)
	LEFT OUTER JOIN GOREVYORUM GY1 ON GY1.TUR=93 AND GY1.GOREVID=SD.ID AND GY1.ID=(SELECT TOP 1 ID FROM GOREVYORUM GY2 WHERE GY2.TUR=93 AND GY2.GOREVID=SD.ID ORDER BY EKLEMETARIHI DESC)
WHERE   
		SD.TESLIMTARIHI between convert(date,'''+@BasTarihi+''',102) and convert(date,'''+@BitTarihi+''',102)
		AND S.TUR = 19
        AND S.DURUM <> 6
	
		--AND SD.ADET > ABS(isnull((select sum(S1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY S1 where S1.YERI=409 and S1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)) + ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=409 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0))
		--AND NOT EXISTS(select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=S.TUR and HEDEFTUR=14 and KAYNAKID=SD.ID)

--ORDER BY R.FIRMA , SD.TESLIMTARIHI'

		if @kalmayan=0
		begin
			set @sql=@sql+' AND SD.ADET > ABS(isnull((select sum(S1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY S1 where S1.YERI=409 and S1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)) + ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=409 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0))'
		end
		if @gizlenen=0
		begin	
			set @sql=@sql+' AND NOT EXISTS(select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=S.TUR and HEDEFTUR=14 and KAYNAKID=SD.ID)'
		end
		EXEC (@SQL)
end





end




===== PROC: TM_DonusumListeleriAlis =====





--sue=sipariþ üretim emri
--suf=sipariþ üretim fiþi
--igk=Ýrsaliye => Gelen Konsinye
--ivs=Ýrsaliye => Verilen Sipariþ
--uel=üretim emri listesi (5 ci buton olacak)

--  exec [TM_DonusumListeleri] '2024-03-11', '2024-03-31','siparis',1,0


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_DonusumListeleriAlis](@BasTarihi VARCHAR(10), @BitTarihi VARCHAR(10), @ekran varchar(10), @gizlenen int, @kalmayan int)
AS
BEGIN 
DECLARE @SQL VARCHAR(MAX)

if @ekran='Alis1'
begin
select
	BASLIKID=S.ID,
	SATIRID=SD.ID,
	STOKID=ST.ID,
	REHBERID=R.ID, 
	R.FIRMA,
	S.TARIH,
	BELGENO=S.TEKLIFNO,
	ST.KOD,
	ST.STOKADI,
	ST.ANABIRIM,
	SD.ACIKLAMA,
	ST.IZLEME,
	ST.OZELKOD,
	ST.OZELKOD2, 
	ST.MUHKODU,
	SD.KDV,
	SD.KUR,
	SD.DOVIZ_KURU,
	SD.ADET,
	SD.MIKTAR,
	BIRIM=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-2702 and DIL=-1 and DEGER = SD.BIRIM),
	--SD.BIRIM,
	SD.BIRIMFIYAT,
	SD.ISKONTO,
	SD.ISKONTO2,
	SD.TUTAR,
	SD.DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT,
	SD.DOVIZ_KURU,
	SD.DOVIZKURDEGERI,
	SD.DOVIZ_TUTARI,
	SD.MASRAFID,
	SD.MERKEZID,
	SD.KAMPANYAID,
	DONUSEN=isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1  where F1.YERI=428 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0),
	IADE=0.0,
	KALAN=ADET-isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1 where F1.YERI=428 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)
	-isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1 where F1.YERI=416 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0),
	SD.TESLIMTARIHI, 
	SATICI=RK.FIRMA,
	--SATICI=S.HAZIRLAYAN,
	SD.PROJEID, 
	SD.POZNO, 
	ST.URUNNO, 
	DETAY_OZELKOD = SD.OZELKOD, 
	DETAY_OZELKOD2 = SD.OZELKOD2,
	PROJEKODU=(Select top 1 P.PROJEKODU from PROJELER P Where P.ID=SD.PROJEID), 
	GIZLE= case when exists(select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=99 and HEDEFTUR=9 and KAYNAKID=SD.ID) then 1 else 0 end 
from 
	TEKLIF S 
		inner join TEKLIFDETAY SD on S.ID=SD.TEKLIFID 
		inner join STOKLAR ST on SD.TUR=1 and SD.URUNID=ST.ID inner join REHBER R on R.ID=S.REHBERID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER RK ON RK.ID=S.HAZIRLAYAN
where  
	convert(date,S.TARIH,102) between convert(date,@BasTarihi,102) and convert(date,@BitTarihi,102)
	and (@kalmayan = 1 OR SD.ADET > isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1 where F1.YERI=428 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0))
	and (@gizlenen = 1 OR not exists (select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=99 and HEDEFTUR=9 and KAYNAKID=SD.ID) )

end

if @ekran='Alis2'
begin
select
	BASLIKID=S.ID,
	SATIRID=SD.ID,
	STOKID=ST.ID, 
	REHBERID=R.ID, 
	R.FIRMA,
	TARIH=S.SIPARISTARIH,
	BELGENO=S.SIPARISNO,
	ST.KOD,ST.STOKADI,
	ST.ANABIRIM,
	SD.ACIKLAMA,
	ST.IZLEME,
	ST.OZELKOD,
	ST.OZELKOD2,
	ST.MUHKODU,
	SD.KDV,
	SD.KUR,
	SD.DOVIZ_KURU,
	SD.ADET,
	SD.MIKTAR,
	BIRIM=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-2702 and DIL=-1 and DEGER = SD.BIRIM),
	--SD.BIRIM,
	SD.BIRIMFIYAT,
	SD.ISKONTO,
	SD.ISKONTO2,
	SD.TUTAR,
	SD.DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT,
	SD.DOVIZ_KURU,
	SD.DOVIZKURDEGERI,
	SD.DOVIZ_TUTARI,
	SD.MASRAFID,
	SD.MERKEZID,
	SD.KAMPANYAID,
	DONUSEN=isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1 where F1.YERI=428 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)         + isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.URUNID=SD.URUNID and F1.YERI=428 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0),
	IADE=0.0,
	KALAN=ADET-(ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1 where F1.YERI=428 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0))  + ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.URUNID=SD.URUNID and F1.YERI=428 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0))),
	SD.TESLIMTARIHI, 
	S.REHBERILETID, 
	SEVK = (SELECT AD FROM  REHBERILETISIM WHERE ID=S.REHBERILETID),
	SATICI=RK.FIRMA,
	--SATICI=S.SATICIKODU, 
	SD.PROJEID, 
	SD.POZNO, 
	ST.URUNNO, 
	PROJEKODU=(Select top 1 P.PROJEKODU from PROJELER P Where P.ID=SD.PROJEID), 
	GIZLE= case when exists(select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=S.TUR and HEDEFTUR=9 and KAYNAKID=SD.ID) then 1 else 0 end,
	DETAY_OZELKOD = SD.OZELKOD, 
	DETAY_OZELKOD2 = SD.OZELKOD2
from 
	SIPARIS S 
	inner join SIPARISDETAY SD on S.ID=SD.SIPARISID 
	inner join STOKLAR ST on SD.TUR=1 and SD.URUNID=ST.ID inner join REHBER R on R.ID=S.REHBERID
	LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER RK ON RK.ID=S.SATICIKODU
where 
	convert(date,S.SIPARISTARIH,102) between convert(date,@BasTarihi,102) and convert(date,@BitTarihi,102)
	and S.TUR=101
	and (@kalmayan = 1 OR SD.ADET > ABS(isnull((select sum(S1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY S1 where S1.YERI=428 and S1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)) + ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=428 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)))
	and (@gizlenen = 1 OR not exists (select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=S.TUR and HEDEFTUR=9 and KAYNAKID=SD.ID))
end
if @ekran='Alis3'
begin

select
	BASLIKID=FB.ID,
	SATIRID=F.ID,
	STOKID=ST.ID,
	REHBERID=R.ID,
	R.FIRMA,
	TARIH=FB.FATURATARIH,
	BELGENO=FB.FATURANO,
	ST.KOD,
	ST.STOKADI,
	ST.ANABIRIM,
	F.ACIKLAMA,
	ST.IZLEME,
	ST.OZELKOD,
	ST.OZELKOD2,
	ST.MUHKODU,
	F.KDV,
	F.KUR,
	F.DOVIZ_KURU,
	F.ADET,
	F.MIKTAR,
	BIRIM=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-2702 and DIL=-1 and DEGER = F.BIRIM),
	--F.BIRIM,
	F.BIRIMFIYAT,
	F.ISKONTO,
	F.ISKONTO2,
	F.TUTAR,
	F.DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT,
	F.DOVIZ_KURU,
	F.DOVIZKURDEGERI,
	F.DOVIZ_TUTARI,
	F.MASRAFID,
	F.MERKEZID,
	F.KAMPANYAID,
	DONUSEN=isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=469 and F1.YERID=F.ID ),0.0),
	IADE=isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=416 and F1.YERID=F.ID ),0.0),
	KALAN=ADET-isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=469 and F1.YERID=F.ID ),0.0)-isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=416 and F1.YERID=F.ID ),0.0),
	TESLIMTARIHI=FB.FATURATARIH, 
	SATICI=RK.FIRMA,
	--SATICI=FB.SATICIKODU, 
	FB.GIRISDEPO, 
	FB.CIKISDEPO,
	F.PROJEID,
	F.POZNO,
	ST.URUNNO, 
	PROJEKODU=(Select top 1 P.PROJEKODU from PROJELER P Where P.ID=F.PROJEID), 
	GIZLE= case when exists(select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=FB.TUR and HEDEFTUR=10 and KAYNAKID=F.ID) then 1 else 0 end, 
	DETAY_OZELKOD=F.OZELKOD, 
	DETAY_OZELKOD2=F.OZELKOD2 
 from 
	FATBASLIK FB 
		inner join FATURA F on FB.ID=F.FATBASID 
		inner join STOKLAR ST on F.TUR=1 and F.URUNID=ST.ID 
		inner join REHBER R on R.ID=FB.REHBERID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER RK ON RK.ID=FB.SATICIKODU
 where 
	convert(date,FB.FATURATARIH,102) between convert(date,@BasTarihi,102) and convert(date,@BitTarihi,102)
	and FB.TUR=109
	and (@kalmayan = 1 OR ADET > isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=469 and F1.YERID=F.ID ),0.0))
	and (@gizlenen = 1 OR not exists (select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=FB.TUR and HEDEFTUR=10 and KAYNAKID=F.ID))
end
if @ekran='Alis4'
begin

select
	BASLIKID=S.ID,
	SATIRID=SD.ID,
	STOKID=ST.ID, 
	REHBERID=R.ID, 
	R.FIRMA,
	TARIH=S.SIPARISTARIH,
	BELGENO=S.SIPARISNO,
	ST.KOD,
	ST.STOKADI,
	ST.ANABIRIM,
	SD.ACIKLAMA,
	ST.IZLEME,
	ST.OZELKOD,
	ST.OZELKOD2,
	ST.MUHKODU,
	SD.KDV,
	SD.KUR,
	SD.DOVIZ_KURU,
	SD.ADET,
	SD.MIKTAR,
	BIRIM=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-2702 and DIL=-1 and DEGER = SD.BIRIM),
	--SD.BIRIM,
	SD.BIRIMFIYAT,
	SD.ISKONTO,
	SD.ISKONTO2,
	SD.TUTAR,
	SD.DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT,
	SD.DOVIZ_KURU,
	SD.DOVIZKURDEGERI,
	SD.DOVIZ_TUTARI,
	SD.MASRAFID,
	SD.MERKEZID,
	SD.KAMPANYAID,
	DONUSEN=isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1 where F1.YERI=406 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)         + isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.URUNID=SD.URUNID and F1.YERI=406 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0),
	IADE=0.0,
	KALAN=ADET-(ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1 where F1.YERI=406 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0))  + ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.URUNID=SD.URUNID and F1.YERI=406 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0))),
	SD.TESLIMTARIHI, 
	S.REHBERILETID, 
	SEVK = (SELECT AD FROM  REHBERILETISIM WHERE ID=S.REHBERILETID),
	SATICI=RK.FIRMA,
	--SATICI=S.SATICIKODU, 
	SD.PROJEID, 
	SD.POZNO, 
	ST.URUNNO,
	PROJEKODU=(Select top 1 P.PROJEKODU from PROJELER P Where P.ID=SD.PROJEID),
	GIZLE= case when exists(select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=S.TUR and HEDEFTUR=10 and KAYNAKID=SD.ID) then 1 else 0 end, 
	DETAY_OZELKOD = SD.OZELKOD, 
	DETAY_OZELKOD2 = SD.OZELKOD2
from 
	SIPARIS S 
		inner join SIPARISDETAY SD on S.ID=SD.SIPARISID 
		inner join STOKLAR ST on SD.TUR=1 and SD.URUNID=ST.ID 
		inner join REHBER R on R.ID=S.REHBERID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER RK ON RK.ID=S.SATICIKODU
where 
	convert(date,S.SIPARISTARIH,102) between convert(date,@BasTarihi,102) and convert(date,@BitTarihi,102)
	and S.TUR=9
	and (@kalmayan = 1 OR SD.ADET > ABS(isnull((select sum(S1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY S1 where S1.YERI=406 and S1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)) + ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=406 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)))
	and (@gizlenen = 1 OR not exists (select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=S.TUR and HEDEFTUR=10 and KAYNAKID=SD.ID))
end
if @ekran='Alis5'
begin

select 
	BASLIKID=FB.ID,
	SATIRID=F.ID,
	STOKID=ST.ID,
	REHBERID=R.ID,
	R.FIRMA,
	TARIH=FB.FATURATARIH,
	BELGENO=FB.FATURANO,
	ST.KOD,
	ST.STOKADI,
	ST.ANABIRIM,
	F.ACIKLAMA,
	ST.IZLEME,
	ST.OZELKOD,
	ST.OZELKOD2,
	ST.MUHKODU,
	F.KDV,
	F.KUR,
	F.DOVIZ_KURU,
	F.ADET,
	F.MIKTAR,
	BIRIM=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-2702 and DIL=-1 and DEGER = F.BIRIM),
	--F.BIRIM,
	F.BIRIMFIYAT,
	F.ISKONTO,
	F.ISKONTO2,
	F.TUTAR,
	F.DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT,
	F.DOVIZ_KURU,
	F.DOVIZKURDEGERI,
	F.DOVIZ_TUTARI,
	F.MASRAFID,
	F.MERKEZID,
	F.KAMPANYAID,
	DONUSEN=isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=408 and F1.YERID=F.ID ),0.0),
	IADE=isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=416 and F1.YERID=F.ID ),0.0),
	KALAN=ADET-isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=408 and F1.YERID=F.ID ),0.0)-isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=416 and F1.YERID=F.ID ),0.0),
	TESLIMTARIHI=FB.FATURATARIH,
	SATICI=RK.FIRMA,
	--SATICI=FB.SATICIKODU, 
	FB.GIRISDEPO, 
	FB.CIKISDEPO,
	F.PROJEID, 
	F.POZNO, 
	ST.URUNNO,
	PROJEKODU=(Select top 1 P.PROJEKODU from PROJELER P Where P.ID=F.PROJEID),
	GIZLE= case when exists(select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=FB.TUR and HEDEFTUR=11 and KAYNAKID=F.ID) then 1 else 0 end,
	DETAY_OZELKOD=F.OZELKOD, 
	DETAY_OZELKOD2=F.OZELKOD2 
from 
	FATBASLIK FB 
		inner join FATURA F on FB.ID=F.FATBASID 
		inner join STOKLAR ST on F.TUR=1 and F.URUNID=ST.ID 
		inner join REHBER R on R.ID=FB.REHBERID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER RK ON RK.ID=FB.SATICIKODU
where 
	convert(date,FB.FATURATARIH,102) between convert(date,@BasTarihi,102) and convert(date,@BitTarihi,102)
	and FB.TUR=10
	and (@kalmayan = 1 OR ADET > isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=408 and F1.YERID=F.ID ),0.0))
	and (@gizlenen = 1 OR not exists (select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=FB.TUR and HEDEFTUR=11 and KAYNAKID=F.ID))
end
if @ekran='Alis6'
begin

select 
	BASLIKID=S.ID,
	SATIRID=SD.ID,
	STOKID=ST.ID, 
	REHBERID=R.ID, 
	R.FIRMA,
	TARIH=S.SIPARISTARIH,
	BELGENO=S.SIPARISNO,
	ST.KOD,
	ST.STOKADI,
	ST.ANABIRIM,
	SD.ACIKLAMA,
	ST.IZLEME,
	ST.OZELKOD,
	ST.OZELKOD2,
	ST.MUHKODU,
	SD.KDV,
	SD.KUR,
	SD.DOVIZ_KURU,
	SD.ADET,
	SD.MIKTAR,
	BIRIM=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-2702 and DIL=-1 and DEGER = SD.BIRIM),
	--SD.BIRIM,
	SD.BIRIMFIYAT,
	SD.ISKONTO,
	SD.ISKONTO2,
	SD.TUTAR,
	SD.DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT,
	SD.DOVIZ_KURU,
	SD.DOVIZKURDEGERI,
	SD.DOVIZ_TUTARI,
	SD.MASRAFID,
	SD.MERKEZID,
	SD.KAMPANYAID,
	DONUSEN=isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1 where F1.YERI=407 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)         + isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.URUNID=SD.URUNID and F1.YERI=407 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0),
	IADE=0.0,
	KALAN=ADET-(ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1 where F1.YERI=407 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0))  + ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.URUNID=SD.URUNID and F1.YERI=407 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0))),
	SD.TESLIMTARIHI, 
	S.REHBERILETID, 
	SEVK = (SELECT AD FROM  REHBERILETISIM WHERE ID=S.REHBERILETID),
	SATICI=RK.FIRMA,
	--SATICI=S.SATICIKODU, 
	SD.PROJEID, 
	SD.POZNO, 
	ST.URUNNO,
	PROJEKODU=(Select top 1 P.PROJEKODU from PROJELER P Where P.ID=SD.PROJEID),
	GIZLE= case when exists(select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=S.TUR and HEDEFTUR=11 and KAYNAKID=SD.ID) then 1 else 0 end, 
	DETAY_OZELKOD = SD.OZELKOD, 
	DETAY_OZELKOD2 = SD.OZELKOD2 
from 
	SIPARIS S 
		inner join SIPARISDETAY SD on S.ID=SD.SIPARISID 
		inner join STOKLAR ST on SD.TUR=1 and SD.URUNID=ST.ID 
		inner join REHBER R on R.ID=S.REHBERID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER RK ON RK.ID=S.SATICIKODU
where 
	convert(date,S.SIPARISTARIH,102) between convert(date,@BasTarihi,102) and convert(date,@BitTarihi,102)	
	and S.TUR=9
	and (@kalmayan = 1 OR SD.ADET > ABS(isnull((select sum(S1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY S1 where S1.YERI=407 and S1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)) + ABS(isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from FATURA F1 where F1.YERI=407 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)))
	and (@gizlenen = 1 OR not exists (select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=S.TUR and HEDEFTUR=11 and KAYNAKID=SD.ID))
end
end



===== PROC: TM_DonusumListeleriSatis =====





--sue=sipariþ üretim emri
--suf=sipariþ üretim fiþi
--igk=Ýrsaliye => Gelen Konsinye
--ivs=Ýrsaliye => Verilen Sipariþ
--uel=üretim emri listesi (5 ci buton olacak)

--  exec [TM_DonusumListeleri] '2024-03-11', '2024-03-31','siparis',1,0


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_DonusumListeleriSatis](@BasTarihi VARCHAR(10), @BitTarihi VARCHAR(10), @ekran varchar(10), @gizlenen int, @kalmayan int)
AS
BEGIN 
DECLARE @SQL VARCHAR(MAX)

if @ekran='Satis1'
begin
SELECT 
    BASLIKID = S.ID,
    SATIRID = SD.ID,
    STOKID = ST.ID,
	REHBERID = R.ID,	
    R.FIRMA,
    S.TARIH,
    BELGENO = S.TEKLIFNO,
    ST.KOD,
    ST.STOKADI,
    ST.ANABIRIM,
    SD.ACIKLAMA,
    ST.IZLEME,
    ST.OZELKOD,
    ST.OZELKOD2,
    ST.MUHKODU,
    SD.KDV,
    SD.KUR,
    SD.DOVIZ_KURU,
    SD.ADET,
    SD.MIKTAR,
    BIRIM=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-2702 and DIL=-1 and DEGER = SD.BIRIM),
	--SD.BIRIM,
    SD.BIRIMFIYAT,
    SD.ISKONTO,
    SD.ISKONTO2,
    SD.TUTAR,
    SD.DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT,
    SD.DOVIZ_KURU,
    SD.DOVIZKURDEGERI,
    SD.DOVIZ_TUTARI,
    SD.MASRAFID,
    SD.MERKEZID,
    SD.KAMPANYAID,
    DONUSEN = isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1  where F1.YERI=428 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0),
    IADE = 0.0,
    KALAN = ADET-isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1 where F1.YERI=413 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0)-isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1 where F1.YERI=416 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0),
    SD.TESLIMTARIHI,
    SATICI=RK.FIRMA,
	--SATICI = S.HAZIRLAYAN,
    SD.PROJEID, 
    SD.POZNO, 
    ST.URUNNO, 
    DETAY_OZELKOD = SD.OZELKOD, 
    DETAY_OZELKOD2 = SD.OZELKOD2,
    PROJEKODU = (Select top 1 P.PROJEKODU from PROJELER P Where P.ID=SD.PROJEID),
    GIZLE = case when exists(select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=99 and HEDEFTUR=19 and KAYNAKID=SD.ID) then 1 else 0 end
from 
	TEKLIF S 
		inner join TEKLIFDETAY SD on S.ID=SD.TEKLIFID
		inner join STOKLAR ST on SD.TUR=1 and SD.URUNID=ST.ID 
		inner join REHBER R on R.ID=S.REHBERID
		LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER RK ON RK.ID=S.HAZIRLAYAN
where  
	convert(date,S.TARIH,102) between convert(date,@BasTarihi,102) and convert(date,@BitTarihi,102)
	and (@kalmayan = 1 OR SD.ADET > isnull((select sum(F1.ADET) from SIPARISDETAY F1 where F1.YERI=413 and F1.YERID=SD.ID ),0.0))
	and (@gizlenen = 1 OR not exists (select ID from DONUSUMBILGISIGIZLE where KAYNAKTUR=99 and HEDEFTUR=19 and KAYNAKID=SD.ID))
end

begin

===== PROC: TM_EkAlanCalistir =====

--  exec TM_EkAlanCalistir '173','ALO'
--select ID,FIRMA FROM REHBER WHERE GRUP IN (120) and FIRMA like '%<ara>%'

CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_EkAlanCalistir](@ID VARCHAR(50), @KOSUL VARCHAR(50))
AS
BEGIN 
DECLARE @SQL VARCHAR(500)
Select 
	@SQL=REPLACE(REPLACE(ALANLAR.SQL,'<ara>',@KOSUL),'SELECT','SELECT TOP 100 ')
from ALANLAR WHERE ID=@ID 
EXEC (@SQL)
end





--select ID,FIRMA FROM REHBER WHERE SINIF=84 and FIRMA like '%<ara>%'
--select ID,FIRMA FROM REHBER WHERE GRUP IN (120) and FIRMA like '%<ara>%'

===== PROC: TM_EPostaHesaplari =====







--  exec [TM_IniOku]
--SELECT * FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2702
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_EPostaHesaplari]
AS
BEGIN 
SELECT * FROM EPOSTAHESAPLARI WHERE VARSAYILAN=1
END



===== PROC: TM_FacebookEnt =====




CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_FacebookEnt](@JSON NVARCHAR(MAX))
AS
BEGIN 


--INSERT INTO TMLOG(JSONM) VALUES(@JSON)


INSERT INTO META_Collect(Contact_ID, Contact_Name, Contact_Phone, Contact_Phone2, Contatc_eMail, Last_Update, Contact_Address, Contact_City, Contact_Town, Contact_Country, Contact_Gender, NOT1, FB_Support, Meta_Class, Durum)
SELECT 
    Contact_ID=j.id,
	Contact_Name=MAX(CASE WHEN (f.name = 'adý_soyadý' or f.name = 'FULL_NAME') THEN v.value END),
	Contact_Phone=MAX(CASE WHEN f.name = 'telefon_numarasý' THEN v.value END),   
	Contact_Phone2=MAX(CASE WHEN f.name = 'diger_telefon_numarasý' THEN v.value END),
	Contatc_eMail=MAX(CASE WHEN f.name = 'e-posta' THEN v.value END),    
    Last_Update=CONVERT(datetime, replace(j.created_time,'+0000',' ')),
    Contact_Address= MAX(CASE WHEN f.name = 'STREET_ADDRESS' THEN v.value END) ,
    Contact_City=MAX(CASE WHEN f.name = 'þehir' THEN v.value END),    
	Contact_Town='',
	Contact_Country=MAX(CASE WHEN f.name = 'ülke' THEN v.value END),
	Contact_Gender=MAX(CASE WHEN f.name = 'cinsiyet' THEN CASE WHEN isnull(v.value,'')='female' or isnull(v.value,'')='Kadýn'  then 'K'
												WHEN isnull(v.value,'')='male' or isnull(v.value,'')='Erkek'  then 'E' END END),
    NOT1=MAX(CASE WHEN f.name = 'NOT1' THEN v.value END),
    FB_Support=1, Meta_Class=2, Durum=1
    
FROM OPENJSON(@json)
WITH (
    id NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.id',
    created_time NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.created_time',
    field_data NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.field_data' AS JSON
) AS j
CROSS APPLY OPENJSON(j.field_data)
WITH (
    name NVARCHAR(255) '$.name',
    [values] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.values' AS JSON
) AS f
OUTER APPLY OPENJSON(f.[values]) WITH (
    value NVARCHAR(255) '$' 
) AS v
GROUP BY j.id, j.created_time;




end


===== PROC: TM_FATBASLIK =====

 
-- exec [TM_FATBASLIK] '2026-01-01','2026-09-11','',0,-1,'14','3584'

CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_FATBASLIK](@BasTarihi VARCHAR(10), @BitTarihi VARCHAR(10), @ID INT, @TAMAMLANAN INT, @BAYIID INT, @TUR VARCHAR(10), @KULLANICIKODU VARCHAR(20))
AS
 
BEGIN 

DECLARE @ILGILIARAMA INT, @GORMEYETKISI INT, @SUBEID INT

 
SET @ILGILIARAMA = 0
SET @GORMEYETKISI = 0
SET @SUBEID = 0

SELECT 
		@GORMEYETKISI=CASE WHEN ISNULL(RO.TY,0)=0 THEN BILGI ELSE '100' end, 
		@SUBEID=r.SUBEID 
FROM REHBER R INNER JOIN KULLANICI K ON K.REHBERID=R.ID INNER JOIN YETKIEK YE ON YE.ROLID=K.ROLID AND R.ID=@KULLANICIKODU AND MODULID= '2201'
															LEFT OUTER JOIN ROLLER RO ON RO.ID=K.ROLID

PRINT @GORMEYETKISI
PRINT @SUBEID
PRINT @KULLANICIKODU
IF @BAYIID=-1
BEGIN
SELECT distinct 
	F.ID,
	DURUM=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2403 AND DEGER=F.DURUM),
	F.ODEMEPLANI,
	FATURATARIH=CONVERT(DATE,FATURATARIH,102),
	FATURATARIHT=CONVERT(VARCHAR(5),FATURATARIH,108),
	FATURANO=FATURANO,
	FATURASERI=F.FATURASERI,
	F.TIPI,
	F.REHBERID,
	F.TUR,
	F.SUBEID,
	F.BASLIK, 
	FATURA_MATRAHI=FATURA_TUTARI-KDV_TUTARI, 
	KDV_TUTARI,
	FATURA_TUTARI=FATURA_TUTARI,
	F.KUR,
	KURFATURA_MALIYETI_ORT=0.0,
	ORTKARORAN=0.0, 
	ORTKAR=0.0  ,
	F.ACIKLAMA,
	F.OZELKOD,
	F.OZELKOD2,
	CARIKOD=R.KOD,
	CARIAD=R.FIRMA,
	F.DOVIZ_CINSI,
	F.DOVIZKUR,
	DOVIZ_TUTARI=(FATURA_TUTARI/nullif(DOVIZKUR,0.0)),  
	DOVIZ_FATURA_MATRAHI=((FATURA_TUTARI-KDV_TUTARI)/nullif(DOVIZKUR,0.0)),
	DOVIZ_KDV_TUTARI=(convert(float,KDV_TUTARI)/nullif(convert(float,DOVIZKUR),0.0))  ,
	GIRISDEPO=(SELECT DEPOADI FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.ID=F.GIRISDEPO),
	CIKISDEPO=(SELECT DEPOADI FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.ID=F.CIKISDEPO),
	F.IRSALIYENO , 
	F.SATICIKODU,
	F.DETAYBOLUMU, 
	SATICIADI = SATICIBILGI.FIRMA, 
	F.VADE, 
	VADETARIH=FATURATARIH + F.VADE,   
	DURUMNEREDEN = case     when (F.TUR=9)and(412 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Teklifden'     
							when (F.TUR=19)and(413 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Teklifden'    
							when (F.TUR=9)and(83 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Servisden'     
							when (F.TUR=19)and(83 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Servisden'    
							when (F.TUR=9)and(428 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Talepten'    
							when (F.TUR=101)and(465 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Üretimden'    else '' end,   
	DURUMNEREYE = case  	when (F.TUR=9)and(407 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Faturaya'     	
							when (F.TUR=9)and(406 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Ýrsaliyeye'   	
							when (F.TUR=101)and(428 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Sipariþe'   	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(410 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Faturaya'    	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(409 in(select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Ýrsaliyeye'  	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(429 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Konsinyeye'  	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(415 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Üretim Fiþine'  	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(420 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Üretim Fiþine'  	else '' end,     
	TESLIMTARIHI =(Select Min(TESLIMTARIHI) from SIPARISDETAY Where SIPARISID=F.ID ), 
	FATURA_GON_TARIHI=null,  
	ZARFID=null,
	ZARF=null,
	ISEMRIDURUM=isnull((select I.DURUM from ISEMRI I where I.YERI=F.TUR and I.YERID=F.ID),-1),
	F.YAZDIRILDI,  
	ONAYLAYACAK='',
	F.ONAYLAYAN,
	EFATURADURUM=0,
	REHBERILETID,
	REHBERILETAD=ISNULL(R1.AD,''),
	FIYAT_LISTESI=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -1007 AND DEGER=F.FIYAT_LISTESI),
	ODEME='',
	TESLIM_SEKLI='',
	F.RAPORDOVIZ,
	FATBASLIK=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Fatura Baþlýðý' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID),
	FATURASATIRVAR=CASE WHEN ISNULL(SD.ID,0)<>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END ,
	YORUM=(SELECT TOP 1 TRIM(char(10) from GY1.YORUM) FROM GOREVYORUM GY1 WHERE GY1.GOREVID=F.ID AND GY1.TUR=105 ORDER BY ID DESC),
	YORUMSAY=(SELECT COUNT(GY1.ID) FROM GOREVYORUM GY1 WHERE GY1.GOREVID=F.ID AND GY1.TUR=105),
	YORUMDETAY=23
from FATBASLIK F   inner join REHBER R on R.ID = F.REHBERID  
						LEFT OUTER JOIN FATURA SD ON F.ID = SD.FATBASID 
						LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER SATICIBILGI ON F.SATICIKODU = SATICIBILGI.ID 
						LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERILETISIM R1 ON R1.REHBERID=f.REHBERID AND R1.ID=F.REHBERILETID  and R1.AKTIF=1 --and R1.VARSAYILAN=1
 where 	
	((
		1=1 and 
		F.TUR =@TUR and
		convert(date,FATURATARIH,102) between convert(date,@BasTarihi,102) and convert(date,@BitTarihi,102)
	)  
	or F.ID=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ID,'')<>'' THEN @ID ELSE 0 END )	and
	(ISNULL(F.DURUM,0) NOT LIKE CASE WHEN @TAMAMLANAN=0 THEN '9' ELSE '-1%' END)
	and ISNULL(F.SATICIKODU,'') LIKE case when @GORMEYETKISI=1 THEN @KULLANICIKODU ELSE '%' END AND
	ISNULL(R.SUBEID,-1) LIKE case when @GORMEYETKISI=10 THEN @SUBEID ELSE -1 END  

 ORDER BY FATURANO desc,F.TUR,F.KUR 
 


end
ELSE
BEGIN

SELECT distinct 
	F.ID,
	DURUM=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2403 AND DEGER=F.DURUM),
	F.ODEMEPLANI,
	FATURATARIH=CONVERT(DATE,FATURATARIH,102),
	FATURATARIHT=CONVERT(VARCHAR(5),FATURATARIH,108),
	FATURANO=FATURANO,
	FATURASERI=F.FATURASERI,
	F.TIPI,
	F.REHBERID,
	F.TUR,
	F.SUBEID,
	F.BASLIK, 
	FATURA_MATRAHI=FATURA_TUTARI-KDV_TUTARI, 
	KDV_TUTARI,
	FATURA_TUTARI=FATURA_TUTARI,
	F.KUR,
	KURFATURA_MALIYETI_ORT=0.0,
	ORTKARORAN=0.0, 
	ORTKAR=0.0  ,
	F.ACIKLAMA,
	F.OZELKOD,
	F.OZELKOD2,
	CARIKOD=R.KOD,
	CARIAD=R.FIRMA,
	F.DOVIZ_CINSI,
	F.DOVIZKUR,
	DOVIZ_TUTARI=(FATURA_TUTARI/nullif(DOVIZKUR,0.0)),  
	DOVIZ_FATURA_MATRAHI=((FATURA_TUTARI-KDV_TUTARI)/nullif(DOVIZKUR,0.0)),
	DOVIZ_KDV_TUTARI=(convert(float,KDV_TUTARI)/nullif(convert(float,DOVIZKUR),0.0))  ,
	GIRISDEPO=(SELECT DEPOADI FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.ID=F.GIRISDEPO),
	CIKISDEPO=(SELECT DEPOADI FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.ID=F.CIKISDEPO),
	F.IRSALIYENO , 
	F.SATICIKODU,
	F.DETAYBOLUMU, 
	SATICIADI = SATICIBILGI.FIRMA, 
	F.VADE, 
	VADETARIH=FATURATARIH + F.VADE,   
	DURUMNEREDEN = case     when (F.TUR=9)and(412 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Teklifden'     
							when (F.TUR=19)and(413 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Teklifden'    
							when (F.TUR=9)and(83 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Servisden'     
							when (F.TUR=19)and(83 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Servisden'    
							when (F.TUR=9)and(428 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Talepten'    
							when (F.TUR=101)and(465 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Üretimden'    else '' end,   
	DURUMNEREYE = case  	when (F.TUR=9)and(407 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Faturaya'     	
							when (F.TUR=9)and(406 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Ýrsaliyeye'   	
							when (F.TUR=101)and(428 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Sipariþe'   	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(410 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Faturaya'    	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(409 in(select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Ýrsaliyeye'  	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(429 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Konsinyeye'  	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(415 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Üretim Fiþine'  	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(420 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Üretim Fiþine'  	else '' end,     
	TESLIMTARIHI =(Select Min(TESLIMTARIHI) from SIPARISDETAY Where SIPARISID=F.ID ), 
	FATURA_GON_TARIHI=null,  
	ZARFID=null,
	ZARF=null,
	ISEMRIDURUM=isnull((select I.DURUM from ISEMRI I where I.YERI=F.TUR and I.YERID=F.ID),-1),
	F.YAZDIRILDI,  
	ONAYLAYACAK='',
	F.ONAYLAYAN,
	EFATURADURUM=0,
	REHBERILETID,
	REHBERILETAD=ISNULL(R1.AD,''),
	FIYAT_LISTESI=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -1007 AND DEGER=F.FIYAT_LISTESI),
	ODEME='',
	TESLIM_SEKLI='',
	F.RAPORDOVIZ,
	FATBASLIK=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Fatura Baþlýðý' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID)  ,
	YORUM=(SELECT TOP 1 TRIM(char(10) from GY1.YORUM) FROM GOREVYORUM GY1 WHERE GY1.GOREVID=F.ID AND GY1.TUR=105 ORDER BY ID DESC),
	YORUMSAY=(SELECT COUNT(GY1.ID) FROM GOREVYORUM GY1 WHERE GY1.GOREVID=F.ID AND GY1.TUR=105),
	YORUMDETAY=23
from FATBASLIK F  inner join REHBER R on R.ID = F.REHBERID  
						LEFT OUTER JOIN FATURA SD ON F.ID = SD.FATBASID 
						LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER SATICIBILGI ON F.SATICIKODU = SATICIBILGI.ID 
						LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERILETISIM R1 ON R1.REHBERID=f.REHBERID AND R1.ID=F.REHBERILETID  and R1.AKTIF=1 --and R1.VARSAYILAN=1
 where 	
 R.ID=@BAYIID AND
	((
		1=1 and 
		F.TUR =19 and
		convert(date,FATURATARIH,102) between convert(date,@BasTarihi,102) and convert(date,@BitTarihi,102)
	) OR 
	F.ID=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ID,'')<>'' THEN @ID ELSE 0 END )	AND
	(ISNULL(F.DURUM,0) NOT LIKE CASE WHEN @TAMAMLANAN=0 THEN '9' ELSE '-1%' END)
	and ISNULL(F.SATICIKODU,'') LIKE case when @GORMEYETKISI=1 THEN @KULLANICIKODU ELSE '%' END AND
	ISNULL(R.SUBEID,-1) LIKE case when @GORMEYETKISI=10 THEN @SUBEID ELSE -1 END 
 ORDER BY FATURANO desc,F.TUR,F.KUR 
 END
--  exec [TM_SiparisAlinan] '2023-05-27', '2023-12-03'
-- select * FROM SIPARIS WHERE ID='5544'
--select * FROM REHBER WHERE ID=1187


END

===== PROC: TM_FATBASLIKDurumGuncelle =====
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[TM_FATBASLIKDurumGuncelle](@FATBASLIKID INT)
AS
BEGIN
	DECLARE
		   @TUR INT,
		   @SATIRSAY INT,
		   @TAMAMLANDI INT,
		   @KISMI INT,
		   @YENI INT

	SET @TUR = (SELECT TUR FROM FATBASLIK WHERE ID = @FATBASLIKID)

	SET @SATIRSAY = (SELECT COUNT(ID) FROM FATURA WHERE FATBASID = @FATBASLIKID)

	SET @TAMAMLANDI = ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(SD.ID) FROM FATURA SD WHERE SD.FATBASID = @FATBASLIKID AND SD.ADET - ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(ADET,0)) FROM FATURA WHERE YERI IN (409,410,429,473) AND YERID = SD.ID),0) <= 0),0)

	SET @KISMI = ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(SD.ID) FROM FATURA SD WHERE SD.FATBASID = 10224 AND SD.ADET - ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(ADET,0)) FROM FATURA WHERE YERI IN (409,410,429,473) AND YERID = SD.ID),0) > 0 AND SD.ADET <> SD.ADET - ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ADET) FROM FATURA WHERE YERI IN (409,410,429,473) AND YERID = SD.ID),0)),0)

	SET @YENI = ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(SD.ID) FROM FATURA SD WHERE SD.FATBASID = @FATBASLIKID AND  SD.ADET = SD.ADET - ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(ADET,0)) FROM FATURA WHERE YERI IN (409,410,429,473) AND YERID = SD.ID),0)),0)

	IF (@TUR = 19)
	BEGIN
		IF (@SATIRSAY = @TAMAMLANDI)
		BEGIN
			UPDATE FATBASLIK SET DURUM = 9 WHERE ID = @FATBASLIKID
		END
		IF (@KISMI > 0 OR (@TAMAMLANDI <> 0 AND @SATIRSAY <> @TAMAMLANDI))
		BEGIN
			UPDATE FATBASLIK SET DURUM = 1 WHERE ID = @FATBASLIKID
		END
		IF (@SATIRSAY = @YENI)
		BEGIN
			UPDATE FATBASLIK SET DURUM = 0 WHERE ID = @FATBASLIKID
		END
	END
END


===== PROC: TM_FATBASLIKGuncelle =====

--rapor dövizi boþ býrakýyoruz, ilk sipariþ kaleminin döviz tipi neyse onunla update edicez. 10092024

--exec [TM_FATBASLIKGuncelle] '','2023-06-18','19','1','1874','-1','-1','2023-06-18 21:05','1901','','Anadepo','','','','','','','Hariç','','','','','2','Yapýlmadý','','2','Satýþ','','','','','-1','-1','TL'


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_FATBASLIKGuncelle](@ID INT, @TARIH DATETIME, @TUR VARCHAR(15), @TIPI SMALLINT, @REHBERID INT, @PROJEID INT, @AKTIVITEID INT,
											@FATBASLIKTARIH DATETIME, @KOCANNO INT, @FATBASLIKNO NVARCHAR(10), @CIKISDEPO VARCHAR(50), @BASLIK NVARCHAR(100),
											@ADRES NVARCHAR(150), @ILCE NVARCHAR(50), @IL NVARCHAR(50), @VD NVARCHAR(50), @VNO NVARCHAR(50), @KDVDURUM NVARCHAR(5),
											@KUR NVARCHAR(10), @DOVIZ_TUTARI MONEY, @DOVIZ_CINSI NVARCHAR(6), @ACIKLAMA NVARCHAR(100), @SATICIKODU INT, @DURUM VARCHAR(50),
											@FATBASLIKSERI NVARCHAR(10), @EKLEYEN INT, @FIYAT_LISTESI VARCHAR(50), @DOVIZKUR MONEY, @TESLIM_SEKLI varchar(50), @ODEME varchar(50), @REHBERILETID INT,
											@SUBEID INT, @SERVISID INT, @RAPORDOVIZ NVARCHAR(5), @VADE INT, @BAYIID INT, @OZELKOD VARCHAR(20), @OZELKOD2 VARCHAR(20)
											)
AS
BEGIN 
DECLARE @YENIID INT, @BASLIK10 VARCHAR(100), @ADRES10 VARCHAR(200), @IL10 VARCHAR(100), @ILCE10 VARCHAR(100), @VNO10 VARCHAR(100), @VD10 VARCHAR(100)
--@TUR SATIÞ 14 ALIÞ 10 @TUR 20 STOKTRANSFER
if @FATBASLIKNO<>'Sil'
begin
BEGIN TRY

IF @TUR='10'
BEGIN
 select TOP 1 @BASLIK10=FIRMA FROM REHBER where ID < 0 order by ID desc
 SELECT @ADRES10=BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI WHERE YER_ID=(select TOP 1 RRI.ID FROM REHBER RR INNER JOIN REHBERILETISIM RRI ON RR.ID=RRI.REHBERID where RR.ID < 0 order by 1 desc) AND ETIKET='Adres' 
 SELECT @IL10=BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI WHERE YER_ID=(select TOP 1 RRI.ID FROM REHBER RR INNER JOIN REHBERILETISIM RRI ON RR.ID=RRI.REHBERID where RR.ID < 0 order by 1 desc) AND ETIKET='Ýl' 
 SELECT @ILCE10=BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI WHERE YER_ID=(select TOP 1 RRI.ID FROM REHBER RR INNER JOIN REHBERILETISIM RRI ON RR.ID=RRI.REHBERID where RR.ID < 0 order by 1 desc) AND ETIKET='Ýlçe' 
 SELECT @VD10=BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI WHERE YER_ID=(select TOP 1 RR.ID FROM REHBER RR where RR.ID < 0 order by 1 desc) AND ETIKET='Vergi Dairesi' 
 SELECT @VNO10=BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI WHERE YER_ID=(select TOP 1 RR.ID FROM REHBER RR where RR.ID < 0 order by 1 desc) AND ETIKET='Vergi No' 
END

			IF @ID IN (SELECT ID FROM FATBASLIK UO WHERE ID=@ID)
			BEGIN
				UPDATE S SET TARIH=@TARIH,
							TUR=@TUR, 
							TIPI=@TIPI,
							REHBERID=@REHBERID, PROJEID=@PROJEID, AKTIVITEID=@AKTIVITEID, 
							FATURATARIH=@FATBASLIKTARIH, 
							KOCANNO=(SELECT top 1  KOCANNO FROM KOCANAYARLARI WHERE TUR = @TUR AND SUBEID = -1),
							FATURANO=@FATBASLIKNO, 
							CIKISDEPO=CASE WHEN @TUR=14 THEN (SELECT ID FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.DEPOADI=@CIKISDEPO) ELSE 0 END,
							GIRISDEPO=CASE WHEN @TUR=10 THEN (SELECT ID FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.DEPOADI=@CIKISDEPO) ELSE 0 END,
							BASLIK=	CASE WHEN @TUR=10 THEN ISNULL(@BASLIK10,R.FIRMA) ELSE ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=R.ID AND RB.YERI=2 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=10),R.FIRMA) END, 
							ADRES=CASE WHEN @TUR=10 THEN @ADRES10 ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=Firma.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=2) END, 
							ILCE=CASE WHEN @TUR=10 THEN @ILCE10 ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=Firma.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=6) END, 
							IL=CASE WHEN @TUR=10 THEN @IL10 ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=Firma.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=8) END, 
							VD=CASE WHEN @TUR=10 THEN @VD10 ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=R.ID AND RB.YERI=2 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=20) END, 
							VNO=CASE WHEN @TUR=10 THEN @VNO10 ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=R.ID AND RB.YERI=2 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=22) END, 
							KDVDURUM=@KDVDURUM, KUR=@KUR, DOVIZ_TUTARI=@DOVIZ_TUTARI, DOVIZ_CINSI=@DOVIZ_CINSI, 
							ACIKLAMA=@ACIKLAMA, SATICIKODU=@SATICIKODU, 
							DURUM=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2403 AND ANAHTAR=@DURUM), 
							FATURASERI=@FATBASLIKSERI, 
							DEGISTIREN=@EKLEYEN, 
							DEGISTIRMETARIHI=GETDATE(),
							FIYAT_LISTESI=CASE WHEN @TUR=14 THEN (SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -1007 AND ANAHTAR=@FIYAT_LISTESI)
												WHEN @TUR=10 THEN (SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -1008 AND ANAHTAR=@FIYAT_LISTESI) else '' end, 
							DOVIZKUR=@DOVIZKUR, 													
							REHBERILETID=0, SUBEID=@SUBEID, SERVISID=@SERVISID,
							VADE=@VADE
							, RAPORDOVIZ=@RAPORDOVIZ,
							S.OZELKOD=@OZELKOD,
							S.OZELKOD2=@OZELKOD2
				FROM FATBASLIK S INNER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID=S.REHBERID 
								LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERILETISIM Firma ON R.ID=Firma.REHBERID and Firma.VARSAYILAN=1 and Firma.AKTIF=1						

				WHERE
				R.ID=@REHBERID AND S.ID=@ID
				EXEC DBO.TM_FATBASLIK '1900-01-01','1900-01-01',@ID,'1',@BAYIID,@TUR,@EKLEYEN
			END ELSE

			BEGIN

					DECLARE @GelenBelgeNo NVARCHAR(50), @an datetime, @TUR01 VARCHAR(10)
					set @an=getdate()
					--SET @TUR01=convert(VARCHAR(10),CASE WHEN @TUR='10' THEN '888' ELSE '1401' END)
					SET @TUR01=ISNULL((select TOP 1 KOCANNO FROM KOCANAYARLARI K WHERE K.TUR=@TUR AND SUBEID=-1 AND CONVERT(DATE,BASLANGICTARIHI,102)<=CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE(),102) ORDER BY BASLANGICTARIHI DESC, ID DESC),0)
					DECLARE @TempTable TABLE (KocanNo nvarchar(50),BelgeSeri nVarchar(50),BelgeNo NVARCHAR(50))
					INSERT INTO @TempTable (kocanno,BelgeSeri,BelgeNo)
					EXEC [dbo].[sp_BelgeNoGetir] @TUR, -1, @TUR01, @an
					SELECT TOP 1 @GelenBelgeNo = BelgeNo FROM @TempTable

					INSERT INTO FATBASLIK (
								TARIH,TUR,TIPI,REHBERID,PROJEID,AKTIVITEID,FATURATARIH,KOCANNO,
								FATURANO,
								CIKISDEPO,GIRISDEPO,BASLIK,
								ADRES,
								ILCE,
								IL,
								VD,
								VNO,
								KDVDURUM,KUR,DOVIZ_TUTARI,DOVIZ_CINSI,
								ACIKLAMA,SATICIKODU,DURUM,FATURASERI,EKLEYEN, EKLEMETARIHI, FIYAT_LISTESI,DOVIZKUR,
								REHBERILETID,SUBEID,SERVISID,RAPORDOVIZ, VADE, GIRISKAYNAK, SANAL, FATURADOVIZI,
								OZELKOD,OZELKOD2) 
					SELECT @TARIH, @TUR, @TIPI, @REHBERID, @PROJEID, @AKTIVITEID, @FATBASLIKTARIH, 
							KOCANNO=(SELECT top 1 KOCANNO FROM KOCANAYARLARI WHERE TUR = @TUR AND SUBEID = -1), 
							FATBASLIKNO=@GelenBelgeNo,--(SELECT Replicate('0',6-(len(MAX(FATURANO)+1)))+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),(MAX(FATURANO)+1)) FROM FATBASLIK WHERE KOCANNO=(SELECT top 1 KOCANNO FROM KOCANAYARLARI WHERE TUR = @TUR AND SUBEID = -1)), 
							CIKISDEPO=CASE WHEN @TUR=14 THEN (SELECT ID FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.DEPOADI=@CIKISDEPO) ELSE 0 END,
							GIRISDEPO=CASE WHEN @TUR=10 THEN (SELECT ID FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.DEPOADI=@CIKISDEPO) ELSE 0 END,
							BASLIK=CASE WHEN @TUR=10 THEN ISNULL(@BASLIK10,R.FIRMA) ELSE ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=R.ID AND RB.YERI=2 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=10),R.FIRMA) END, 
							ADRES=CASE WHEN @TUR=10 THEN @ADRES10 ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=Firma.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=2) END, 
							ILCE=CASE WHEN @TUR=10 THEN @ILCE10 ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=Firma.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=6) END, 
							IL=CASE WHEN @TUR=10 THEN @IL10 ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=Firma.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=8) END, 
							ID_VERGIDAI=CASE WHEN @TUR=10 THEN @VD10 ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=R.ID AND RB.YERI=2 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=20) END, 
							ID_VERGINO=CASE WHEN @TUR=10 THEN @VNO10 ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=R.ID AND RB.YERI=2 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=22) END, 
							@KDVDURUM, @KUR, @DOVIZ_TUTARI, @DOVIZ_CINSI, 
							@ACIKLAMA, @SATICIKODU, 
							DURUM=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2403 AND ANAHTAR=@DURUM), 
							@FATBASLIKSERI, @EKLEYEN,EKLEMETARIHI=GETDATE(), 
					
							FIYAT_LISTESI=CASE WHEN @TUR=14 THEN (SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -1007 AND ANAHTAR=@FIYAT_LISTESI)
												WHEN @TUR=10 THEN (SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -1008 AND ANAHTAR=@FIYAT_LISTESI) else '' end,
					
							@DOVIZKUR, 								
							REHBERILETID=0, @SUBEID, @SERVISID, RAPORDOVIZ=@RAPORDOVIZ, @VADE, GIRISKAYNAK =1, SANAL=1,'TL', @OZELKOD, @OZELKOD2
					FROM REHBER R LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERILETISIM Firma ON R.ID=Firma.REHBERID and Firma.VARSAYILAN=1 and Firma.AKTIF=1
					WHERE
					R.ID=@REHBERID

					SET @YENIID = @@Identity
					EXEC DBO.TM_FATBASLIK '1900-01-01','1900-01-01',@YENIID,'1',@BAYIID, @TUR,@EKLEYEN
			END



			END TRY
			BEGIN CATCH
					SELECT 'Hata oluþtu....'+ERROR_MESSAGE()--+' - '+ISNULL(convert(datetime,(substring(@BasTar,7,4)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,4,2)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,1,2) +' '+@BasTarT)),'')
			END CATCH
end 
if @FATBASLIKNO='Sil'
begin
	DELETE FROM FATURA WHERE FATBASID=@ID
	DELETE FROM FATBASLIK WHERE ID=@ID
end
end

--  exec [TM_FATBASLIKAlinan] '2023-05-27', '2023-12-03'

--select * FROM REHBER WHERE KOD='120.01.001'
