===== PROC: TM_IsListesiPerformansRaporu =====


-- exec TM_IsListesiPerformansRaporu '2023-01-01','2023-12-31'
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_IsListesiPerformansRaporu](@BASTAR DATETIME, @BITTAR DATETIME, @KISI varchar(50)=NULL, @TUR varchar(50)=NULL,@FIRMA varchar(50)=NULL)
AS
BEGIN 

SET @KISI=CASE WHEN @KISI IS NULL THEN '' END
SET @TUR=CASE WHEN @TUR IS NULL THEN '' END
SET @FIRMA=CASE WHEN @FIRMA IS NULL THEN '' END
SET @BASTAR = @BASTAR+' 00:00:00'
SET @BITTAR = @BITTAR+' 23:59:59'

DECLARE 
	@ALAN VARCHAR(MAX) = '',	
	@ALAN2 VARCHAR(MAX) = '',
	@ALAN3 VARCHAR(MAX) = '',
	@ALAN4 VARCHAR(MAX) = '',
	@SQL VARCHAR(8000),
	@ID INT = 1,
	@MAXID INT

SET @BASTAR = @BASTAR+' 00:00:00'
SET @BITTAR = @BITTAR+' 23:59:59'


SELECT * INTO #TEMP_TUR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -21044
SET @MAXID = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM #TEMP_TUR)

WHILE @ID<=@MAXID
BEGIN
	SET @ALAN = @ALAN +
						(SELECT
							'['+ANAHTAR+']'
						FROM
							#TEMP_TUR
						WHERE
							SIRA = @ID)

	SET @ALAN2 = @ALAN2 +
						(SELECT
							'ISNULL(['+ANAHTAR+'],0) AS ['+ANAHTAR+'], '
						FROM
							#TEMP_TUR
						WHERE
							SIRA = @ID)

	SET @ALAN3 = @ALAN3 +
						(SELECT
							'SUM(ISNULL(['+ANAHTAR+'],0)) AS ['+ANAHTAR+'], '
						FROM
							#TEMP_TUR
						WHERE
							SIRA = @ID)
	SET @ALAN4 = @ALAN4 +
						(SELECT
							'0 AS ['+ANAHTAR+'], '
						FROM
							#TEMP_TUR
						WHERE
							SIRA = @ID)

	SET @ID = @ID + 1  
END

SET @ALAN = REPLACE(@ALAN,'][','],[')
SET @ALAN2 = SUBSTRING(@ALAN2,1,LEN(@ALAN2)-1)
SET @ALAN3 = SUBSTRING(@ALAN3,1,LEN(@ALAN3)-1)
SET @ALAN4 = SUBSTRING(@ALAN4,1,LEN(@ALAN4)-1)

SET @SQL = '
SELECT
	KISI AS [aKiþi],
	SUM(FIRMA_SAYISI) AS [Firma Sayýsý],
	SUM(FIRMASAYTEKIL) AS [Firma Tekil Sayýsý],
	'+@ALAN3+',
	ISNULL(Y.YENI,0) AS [Yeni Müþteri],
	SUM([Satýþ Fýrsatý]) AS [Satýþ Fýrsatý]
FROM(
	SELECT 
		KISI,
		FIRMA_SAYISI,
		FIRMASAYTEKIL,
		'+@ALAN2+',
		0 AS [Satýþ Fýrsatý]
	FROM(
		SELECT                                   
			RB.FIRMA AS KISI,
			ISNULL(GEN.ANAHTAR,''BOÞ'') AS TUR,
			FIRMA_SAYISI=(SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT G1.REHBERID) FROM GOREVLER G1 INNER JOIN GOREVLISTE GL ON GL.ID = G1.LISTEID WHERE G1.EKLEYEN = RB.ID AND G1.BASLAMATARIHI BETWEEN '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTAR,120)+' 00:00'' AND '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTAR,120)+' 23:59''),
			FIRMASAYTEKIL = CASE 
								WHEN ISNULL('''+@KISI+''','''') = ''''
								THEN (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT G1.REHBERID) FROM GOREVLER G1 INNER JOIN GOREVLISTE GL ON GL.ID = G1.LISTEID WHERE G1.BASLAMATARIHI BETWEEN '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTAR,120)+' 00:00'' AND '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTAR,120)+' 23:59'')
								WHEN ISNULL('''+@KISI+''','''') <> ''''
								THEN (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT G1.REHBERID) FROM GOREVLER G1 INNER JOIN GOREVLISTE GL ON GL.ID = G1.LISTEID WHERE G1.EKLEYEN = RB.ID AND G1.BASLAMATARIHI BETWEEN '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTAR,120)+' 00:00'' AND '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTAR,120)+' 23:59'')
							END,
			SAYI=COUNT(*),
			TOPLAM_IS_LISTESI = CASE 
								WHEN ISNULL('''+@KISI+''','''') = ''''
								THEN (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM GOREVLER G1 INNER JOIN GOREVLISTE GL ON GL.ID = G1.LISTEID WHERE G1.BASLAMATARIHI BETWEEN '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTAR,120)+' 00:00'' AND '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTAR,120)+' 23:59'')
								WHEN ISNULL('''+@KISI+''','''') <> ''''
								THEN (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM GOREVLER G1 INNER JOIN GOREVLISTE GL ON GL.ID = G1.LISTEID WHERE G1.EKLEYEN = RB.ID AND G1.BASLAMATARIHI BETWEEN '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTAR,120)+' 00:00'' AND '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTAR,120)+' 23:59'')
							END
         
		FROM GOREVLER G  
			    INNER JOIN GOREVLISTE GL ON GL.ID = G.LISTEID
				LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID=G.REHBERID
				LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER RB  ON G.EKLEYEN=RB.ID
				LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER RP ON RP.ID=G.MUS_ILGILI
				LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI GEN ON GEN.DEGER=G.TURU and GEN.BOLUM=-21044
		WHERE   
			G.BASLAMATARIHI BETWEEN '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTAR,120)+' 00:00'' AND '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTAR,120)+' 23:59''
			AND CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),G.EKLEYEN) LIKE '''+@KISI+'''+''%''
			AND CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),isnull(GEN.DEGER,'''')) LIKE '''+@TUR+'''+''%''
			AND ISNULL(R.FIRMA,'''') LIKE  ''%''+'''+@FIRMA+'''+''%''
		GROUP BY 
			RB.ID,RB.FIRMA,GEN.ANAHTAR--,G.REHBERID
	) AS X
	PIVOT
	(
		SUM(SAYI) FOR TUR IN ('+@ALAN+')
	) AS PVT
	UNION ALL
	SELECT
		R.FIRMA AS KISI,
		0 AS FIRMA_SAYISI,
		0 AS FIRMASAYTEKIL,
		'+@ALAN4+',
		COUNT(P.ID) AS [Satýþ Fýrsatý]
	FROM
		PROJELER P INNER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID = P.PRJ_SORUMLUSU_ID
				   INNER JOIN REHBER RF ON RF.ID = P.REHBERID
	WHERE
		P.BASLAMATARIHI BETWEEN '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTAR,120)+' 00:00'' AND '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTAR,120)+' 23:59''
		AND P.MODUL = 1
		AND CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),P.PRJ_SORUMLUSU_ID) LIKE '+@KISI+'+''%''
		AND ISNULL(RF.FIRMA,'''') LIKE  ''%''+'''+@FIRMA+'''+''%''
	GROUP BY
		R.FIRMA
) AS X  LEFT OUTER JOIN (SELECT CALISAN,SUM(YENI) AS YENI FROM(SELECT    DISTINCT R.FIRMA,RB.FIRMA AS CALISAN,CASE WHEN R.EKLEMETARIHI BETWEEN '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTAR,120)+' 00:00'' AND '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTAR,120)+' 23:59''THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS YENI       
FROM GOREVLER G INNER JOIN GOREVLISTE GL ON GL.ID = G.LISTEID LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID=G.REHBERID LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER RB  ON G.EKLEYEN=RB.ID	LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER RP ON RP.ID=G.MUS_ILGILI LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI GEN ON GEN.DEGER=G.TURU and GEN.BOLUM=-21044 WHERE G.BASLAMATARIHI BETWEEN '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTAR,120)+' 00:00'' AND '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTAR,120)+' 23:59''	AND CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),G.EKLEYEN) LIKE '''+@KISI+'''+''%''	AND CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),isnull(GEN.DEGER,'''')) LIKE '''+@TUR+'''+''%''	AND ISNULL(R.FIRMA,'''') LIKE  ''%''+'''+@FIRMA+'''+''%'') AS X GROUP BY CALISAN) Y ON Y.CALISAN = X.KISI
GROUP BY
	KISI,YENI
ORDER BY
	KISI
'

EXEC (@SQL)

DROP TABLE #TEMP_TUR
end


===== PROC: TM_KDRSonuc =====








--  exec [TM_KDRSonuc]


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_KDRSonuc]
AS
BEGIN 
DECLARE @YIL int
SET @YIL = year(GetDate())

SELECT *, SONUC=(KDR_BORCLULAR+KDR_BANKA+KDR_POS+KDR_ALINAN_CEK+KDR_ALINAN_SENET+KDR_KASA+KDR_STOK)-(KDR_ALACAKLILAR+KDR_KREDILER+KDR_KREDI_KARTI+KDR_VERILEN_CEK+KDR_VERILEN_SENET) FROM (

select
KDR_POS=isnull((select SUM(isnull(KALAN,0.0)) from
                    (      select KALAN=SUM(ALACAK-BORC)*(select top 1 D.SATIS from DOVIZ D where D.CINSI=K1.KUR order by D.TARIH desc)
                           from POS K inner join KASA K1 on K.ID=K1.HESAPID
                           where year(K1.ISLEMTARIHI)=@YIL and K1.HESAPTURU='P'
                           group by K1.KUR)as X),0.0),
KDR_ALINAN_CEK=isnull((select SUM(isnull(KALAN,0.0)) from
                    (      select KALAN=SUM(CASE WHEN DOVIZ_KURU = 'TL' THEN CONVERT(FLOAT,DOVIZ_TUTARI)WHEN C.KUR = 'TL' THEN convert(float,C.TUTAR) END)
from CEKLER C inner join REHBER R on C.REHBERID=R.ID
where C.TUR in (130,132,133,134,135,138) AND C.CEKSENET = 101)as X),0.0),
KDR_ALINAN_SENET=isnull((select SUM(isnull(KALAN,0.0)) from
                    (      select KALAN=SUM(CASE WHEN DOVIZ_KURU = 'TL' THEN CONVERT(FLOAT,DOVIZ_TUTARI)WHEN C.KUR = 'TL' THEN convert(float,C.TUTAR) END)
from CEKLER C inner join REHBER R on C.REHBERID=R.ID
where C.TUR in (130,132,133,134,135,138) AND C.CEKSENET = 121)as X),0.0),
KDR_KASA=isnull((select SUM(isnull(KALAN,0.0)) from
                    (      select KALAN=convert(float,(SUM(ALACAK-BORC)*(select top 1 D.SATIS from DOVIZ D where D.CINSI=K1.KUR order by D.TARIH desc)))
                           from KASALAR K inner join KASA K1 on K.ID=K1.HESAPID
                           where year(K1.ISLEMTARIHI)=year(GetDate()) and K1.HESAPTURU='K'
                           group by K.KASAKODU,K.KASAADI,K1.KUR)as X),0.0),
KDR_STOK=isnull((select SUM(isnull(KALAN,0.0)) from
                    (      select KALAN=DRM.KALAN*(ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 SOM.BIRIMMALIYET FROM STOK_ORT_MALIYET SOM INNER JOIN (SELECT TOP 1 SM.STOKID,SM.BIRIMMALIYET,FB.TUR,SM.DEPOID,FB.CIKISDEPO,NUM=ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY STOKID ORDER BY SM.TARIH desc) FROM STOK_ORT_MALIYET SM INNER JOIN FATBASLIK FB ON FB.ID = SM.FATBASID WHERE SM.STOKID = DRM.URUNID AND SM.DEPOID = CASE WHEN DRM.DEPO = 11 THEN 1 ELSE DRM.DEPO END ORDER BY SM.TARIH DESC) AS X ON X.STOKID = SOM.STOKID AND CASE WHEN X.BIRIMMALIYET = 0 THEN 1 ELSE X.DEPOID END = SOM.DEPOID ORDER BY SOM.TARIH DESC,X.NUM DESC),0))
                           from STOKLAR S inner join 
                                                      (SELECT DEPO,URUNID,
                                                                             GIREN=round(SUM(CASE WHEN NETMIKTAR > 0.0 THEN NETMIKTAR ELSE 0.0 END),2), 
                                                                             CIKAN=round(SUM(CASE WHEN NETMIKTAR < 0.0 THEN -1.0*NETMIKTAR ELSE 0.0 END),2), 
                                                                             KALAN=round(SUM(NETMIKTAR),2)
                                                      FROM( SELECT DEPO=GIRISDEPO,F.URUNID, CASE WHEN (FB.TUR = 6) AND (F.MIKTAR >= 0) THEN F.MIKTAR WHEN (FB.TUR = 6) AND (F.MIKTAR < 0) THEN 0 ELSE F.MIKTAR END AS NETMIKTAR
                                                                                                        FROM dbo.FATBASLIK AS FB INNER JOIN dbo.FATURA AS F ON F.FATBASID = FB.ID  
                                                                                                        WHERE (F.STOKDURUMDEGIS = 1) AND (ISNULL(FB.DURUM, 1) <> 6)  AND (ISNULL(FB.GIRISDEPO, 0) > 0) and F.TUR=1
                                                                                                        UNION ALL
                                                                                                        SELECT DEPO=CIKISDEPO,F.URUNID, CASE WHEN (FB.TUR = 6) AND (F.MIKTAR > 0) THEN 0 WHEN (FB.TUR = 6) AND (F.MIKTAR <= 0) THEN F.MIKTAR ELSE - F.MIKTAR END AS NETMIKTAR
                                                                                                        FROM dbo.FATBASLIK AS FB INNER JOIN dbo.FATURA AS F ON F.FATBASID = FB.ID 
                                                                                                        WHERE (F.STOKDURUMDEGIS = 1) AND (ISNULL(FB.DURUM, 1) <> 6)  AND (ISNULL(FB.CIKISDEPO, 0) > 0) and F.TUR=1
                                                                             ) AS ASD GROUP BY URUNID,DEPO
                                                      HAVING SUM(NETMIKTAR)<>0.0
                                                      )as DRM on S.ID=DRM.URUNID --left join 
                                                      --(select STOKID,BIRIMMALIYET,NUM=ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY STOKID ORDER BY TARIH desc) from STOK_ORT_MALIYET SM)as Mlt on Mlt.STOKID=S.ID
                           where --Mlt.NUM=1 and 
                                  S.DURUM=1 AND S.TIPI NOT IN (SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI G WHERE G.DEGER=S.TIPI AND BOLUM=-2703 AND G.DEGER LIKE '%-%'))as X),0.0),
KDR_ALACAKLILAR=isnull((select SUM(isnull(KALAN,0.0)) from
                    (      SELECT KALAN=-1.0*convert(float,(BAKIYE*(select top 1 D.SATIS from DOVIZ D where D.CINSI=XXX.KUR order by D.TARIH desc)))  
                           FROM ( select  DISTINCT R.SUBEID,R.KOD ,R.FIRMA,cast(TOPLAM_BORC as float) AS BORC ,cast(TOPLAM_ALACAK as float) AS ALACAK,KUR ,BAKIYE=TOPLAM_BORC-TOPLAM_ALACAK 
                                        FROM(  select
                                               REHBERID,TOPLAM_BORC=SUM(isnull(BORC,0)),TOPLAM_ALACAK=SUM(isnull(ALACAK,0)),KUR=isnull(KUR,'TL')
                                        from (
                                           SELECT
                                               REHBERID,
                                               SUM(CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE F.FATURA_TUTARI END) AS BORC,
                                               0 AS ALACAK,
                                               CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_CINSI else KUR end as KUR
                                               FROM FATBASLIK F
                                               WHERE  isnull(F.DURUM,0)<>6 and (F.TUR in (15,16,17)) 
                                               AND year(FATURATARIH) = @YIL
                                               GROUP BY F.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_CINSI else KUR end
                                        UNION ALL
                                               SELECT REHBERID,
                                               0 AS BORC,
                                               SUM(CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE F.FATURA_TUTARI END) AS FATURA_ALACAK, -- alýnan fatura BORCA YAZ
                                               CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_CINSI else KUR end as KUR
                                               FROM FATBASLIK F
                                               WHERE  isnull(F.DURUM,0)<>6 and (F.TUR in (8,11,12,13)) 
                                               AND year(FATURATARIH) = @YIL 
                                               GROUP BY F.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_CINSI else KUR end
                                        UNION ALL
                                               SELECT REHBERID,
                                               SUM(case when ISNULL(K.BORC,0)>0 and EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 then K.DOVIZ_TUTARI else K.BORC end) AS  BORC,
                                               SUM(case when ISNULL(K.ALACAK,0)>0 and EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 then K.DOVIZ_TUTARI else K.ALACAK end) AS ALACAK,
                                               CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1  THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end as KUR
                                               FROM KASA K
                                               WHERE TUR not between 60 and 79  AND 
                                               year(ISLEMTARIHI) = @YIL
                                               GROUP BY K.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end
                                        UNION ALL
                                               SELECT CH.REHBERID,
                                               BORC=SUM(Case when CH.ISLEM in(140,131,132,133,134,137) then (CASE WHEN c.EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN c.DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE c.TUTAR END) else 0.0 end),
                                               ALACAK=SUM(Case when CH.ISLEM in(130,141) then (CASE WHEN c.EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN C.DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE c.TUTAR END) else 0.0 end),
                                               CASE WHEN c.EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN C.DOVIZ_KURU else c.KUR end as KUR
                                               FROM CEKLER C inner join CEKHAREKET CH on C.ID=CH.CEKSENETLERID
                                               WHERE CH.ISLEM in(130,131,132,134,137,140,141) and 
                                               year(CH.TARIH) = @YIL 
                                               GROUP BY CH.REHBERID,CASE WHEN c.EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN c.DOVIZ_KURU else c.KUR end
                                        UNION ALL
                                               SELECT REHBERID,
                                               0 AS BORC,
                                               SUM(CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE TUTAR END) AS ALACAK,
                                               CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end as KUR
                                               FROM SENETLER S
                                               WHERE TUR = 24   
                                               AND year(TARIH) = @YIL
                                               GROUP BY S.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR END
                                        UNION ALL
                                               SELECT REHBERID,SUM(CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE TUTAR END) AS
                                               BORC,0 AS ALACAK,
                                               CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end as KUR
                                               FROM SENETLER S
                                               WHERE TUR = 34  
                                               AND year(TARIH) = @YIL
                                               GROUP BY S.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end
                                        ) as asd
                                  group by REHBERID,KUR) AS DSA
                                  INNER JOIN REHBER R ON     DSA.REHBERID=R.ID AND ISNULL(R.KDR_Disi,'False') ='False'
                           where  R.ID > 0 and isnull(R.ID,'')<>'' and R.GRUP<>335 
                           AND TOPLAM_BORC<=TOPLAM_ALACAK
   ) AS XXX )as X),0.0),
--KREDÝLER
KDR_KREDILER=isnull((select SUM(isnull(KALAN,0.0)) from
                    (      select KALAN=convert(float,(sum(case when P.ODENMIS=1 and P.TARIH<=Getdate() then 0.0 else P.TAKSIT end)*(select top 1 D.SATIS from DOVIZ D where D.CINSI=KR.KUR order by D.TARIH desc)))
                           from KREDILER KR left join PLANKREDI P on P.KREDIID=KR.ID
                           where KR.DURUM>0 and GENELKREDITIPI=1 and exists(select 1 from PLANKREDI P2 where P2.KREDIID=KR.ID and P2.TARIH<=Getdate())
                           GROUP BY KR.ID,KREDIKODU,ADI,SOZLESMENO,GENELKREDITIPI,KR.KUR,ALINISTARIHI,KAPANISTARIHI,KREDITAKSIT
                           union all
                           select KALAN=convert(float,(isnull(sum(BORC),0)-isnull(sum(ALACAK),0)*(select top 1 D.SATIS from DOVIZ D where D.CINSI=KR.KUR order by D.TARIH desc)))
                           from KREDILER KR left outer join KASA KS on HESAPTURU='R' and KS.HESAPID=KR.ID
                           where KR.DURUM>0 and KS.TUR<>2 and GENELKREDITIPI=2 and KS.ISLEMTARIHI<=Getdate()
                           GROUP BY KR.ID,KREDIKODU,ADI,SOZLESMENO,GENELKREDITIPI,KR.KUR,ALINISTARIHI,KAPANISTARIHI,KREDITAKSIT)as X),0.0),
KDR_KREDI_KARTI=isnull((select SUM(isnull(KALAN,0.0)) from
                    (      select KALAN=convert(float,(SUM(ALACAK-BORC)*(select top 1 D.SATIS from DOVIZ D where D.CINSI=K1.KUR order by D.TARIH desc)))
                           from KREDIKARTI K inner join KASA K1 on K.ID=K1.HESAPID
                           where year(K1.ISLEMTARIHI)=year(GetDate()) and K1.HESAPTURU='V'
                           group by K.KODU,K.ADI,K1.KUR)as X),0.0),
KDR_VERILEN_CEK=isnull((select SUM(isnull(KALAN,0.0)) from
                    (      select KALAN=SUM(CASE WHEN DOVIZ_KURU = 'TL' THEN CONVERT(FLOAT,DOVIZ_TUTARI)WHEN C.KUR = 'TL' THEN convert(float,C.TUTAR) END)
from CEKLER C inner join REHBER R on C.REHBERID=R.ID
where C.TUR in (140) AND C.CEKSENET = 103)as X),0.0),
KDR_VERILEN_SENET=isnull((select SUM(isnull(KALAN,0.0)) from
                    (      select KALAN=SUM(CASE WHEN DOVIZ_KURU = 'TL' THEN CONVERT(FLOAT,DOVIZ_TUTARI)WHEN C.KUR = 'TL' THEN convert(float,C.TUTAR) END)
from CEKLER C inner join REHBER R on C.REHBERID=R.ID
where C.TUR in (140) AND C.CEKSENET = 321)as X),0.0),
KDR_BORCLULAR=isnull((select SUM(isnull(KALAN,0.0)) from
                    (      SELECT KALAN=convert(float,(BAKIYE*(select top 1 D.SATIS from DOVIZ D where D.CINSI=XXX.KUR order by D.TARIH desc)))  
                           FROM (
                           select  DISTINCT R.SUBEID,R.KOD ,R.FIRMA,cast(TOPLAM_BORC as float) AS BORC ,cast(TOPLAM_ALACAK as float) AS ALACAK,KUR ,BAKIYE=TOPLAM_BORC-TOPLAM_ALACAK 

                           FROM(
                                  select
                                         REHBERID,TOPLAM_BORC=SUM(isnull(BORC,0)),TOPLAM_ALACAK=SUM(isnull(ALACAK,0)),KUR=isnull(KUR,'TL')
                                  from (
                                     SELECT
                                        REHBERID,
                                        SUM(CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE F.FATURA_TUTARI END) AS BORC,
                                        0 AS ALACAK,
                                        CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_CINSI else KUR end as KUR
                                        FROM FATBASLIK F
                                        WHERE  isnull(F.DURUM,0)<>6 and (F.TUR in (15,16,17)) 
                                        AND year(FATURATARIH) = @YIL
                                        GROUP BY F.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_CINSI else KUR end
                                  UNION ALL
                                        SELECT REHBERID,
                                        0 AS BORC,
                                        SUM(CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE F.FATURA_TUTARI END) AS FATURA_ALACAK, -- alýnan fatura BORCA YAZ
                                        CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_CINSI else KUR end as KUR
                                        FROM FATBASLIK F
                                        WHERE  isnull(F.DURUM,0)<>6 and (F.TUR in (8,11,12,13)) 
                                        AND year(FATURATARIH) = @YIL 
                                        GROUP BY F.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_CINSI else KUR end
                                  UNION ALL
                                        SELECT REHBERID,
                                        SUM(case when ISNULL(K.BORC,0)>0 and EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 then K.DOVIZ_TUTARI else K.BORC end) AS  BORC,
                                        SUM(case when ISNULL(K.ALACAK,0)>0 and EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 then K.DOVIZ_TUTARI else K.ALACAK end) AS ALACAK,
                                        CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1  THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end as KUR
                                        FROM KASA K
                                        WHERE TUR not between 60 and 79  AND 
                                        year(ISLEMTARIHI) = @YIL
                                        GROUP BY K.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end
                                  UNION ALL
                                        SELECT CH.REHBERID,
                                        BORC=SUM(Case when CH.ISLEM in(140,131,132,133,134,137) then (CASE WHEN c.EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN c.DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE c.TUTAR END) else 0.0 end),
                                        ALACAK=SUM(Case when CH.ISLEM in(130,141) then (CASE WHEN c.EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN C.DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE c.TUTAR END) else 0.0 end),
                                        CASE WHEN c.EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN C.DOVIZ_KURU else c.KUR end as KUR
                                        FROM CEKLER C inner join CEKHAREKET CH on C.ID=CH.CEKSENETLERID
                                        WHERE CH.ISLEM in(130,131,132,134,137,140,141) and 
                                        year(CH.TARIH) = @YIL 
                                        GROUP BY CH.REHBERID,CASE WHEN c.EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN c.DOVIZ_KURU else c.KUR end
                                  UNION ALL
                                        SELECT REHBERID,
                                        0 AS BORC,
                                        SUM(CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE TUTAR END) AS ALACAK,
                                        CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end as KUR
                                        FROM SENETLER S
                                        WHERE TUR = 24   
                                        AND year(TARIH) = @YIL
                                        GROUP BY S.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR END
                                  UNION ALL
                                        SELECT REHBERID,SUM(CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE TUTAR END) AS
                                        BORC,0 AS ALACAK,
                                        CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end as KUR
                                        FROM SENETLER S
                                        WHERE TUR = 34  
                                        AND year(TARIH) = @YIL
                                        GROUP BY S.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end
                                        ) as asd
                                  group by REHBERID,KUR) AS DSA
                                  INNER JOIN REHBER R ON     DSA.REHBERID=R.ID AND ISNULL(R.KDR_Disi,'False') ='False'
                           where  R.ID > 0 and isnull(R.ID,'')<>'' and R.GRUP<>335  
                           AND TOPLAM_BORC>=TOPLAM_ALACAK
                              ) AS XXX       )as X),0.0),
KDR_BANKA = isnull((select SUM(isnull(KALAN,0.0)) from
                    (      select KALAN=SUM(ALACAK-BORC)*(select top 1 D.SATIS from DOVIZ D where D.CINSI=K1.KUR order by D.TARIH desc)
                           from BANKAHESAPLAR K inner join KASA K1 on K.ID=K1.HESAPID
                           where year(K1.ISLEMTARIHI)=@YIL and K1.HESAPTURU='B' AND ISNULL(ONLINEHESAPHAREKETI,0) = 0
                           group by K1.KUR)as X),0.0)) D
end

===== PROC: TM_KDRSonucDetay =====









--  exec [TM_KDRSonucDetay] 2


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_KDRSonucDetay](@TIP varchar(2))
AS
BEGIN 

DECLARE @Bugun DATETIME = GETDATE();

-- Yýlýn ilk günü ve ilk aný
DECLARE @YilIlkAn DATETIME = CAST(DATEFROMPARTS(YEAR(@Bugun), 1, 1) AS DATETIME);

-- Yýlýn son günü ve son aný
DECLARE @YilSonAn DATETIME = DATEADD(MILLISECOND, -3, DATEADD(YEAR, 1, @YilIlkAn));

-- String olarak almak
DECLARE @YilIlkAnStr VARCHAR(20) = CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), @YilIlkAn, 120);
DECLARE @YilSonAnStr VARCHAR(20) = CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), @YilSonAn, 120);

	DECLARE @SQL VARCHAR(8000)
	IF @TIP='1' 
	BEGIN
			CREATE TABLE #TempResults
				(	KOD NVARCHAR(1000),
					FIRMA NVARCHAR(1000),
					BAKIYE NVARCHAR(1000),
					KUR NVARCHAR(1000),
					TLKARSILIK NVARCHAR(1000));

			SET @SQL=(select replace(REPLACE(CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),[SQL]),':PDonemBas',''''+@YilIlkAnStr+''''),':PDonemSon',''''+@YilSonAnStr+'''') from DOKUMLER where RAPORADI =  'KDR_BORÇLULAR')
			INSERT INTO #TempResults (KOD,FIRMA, BAKIYE,KUR,TLKARSILIK) -- Yeni sütunu dahil etmeden ekliyoruz
			EXEC(@SQL);		
			SELECT DETAY='Ekstre',*  FROM #TempResults;
			DROP TABLE #TempResults;
	END
	IF @TIP='2' 
	BEGIN
			SET @SQL=(select replace(REPLACE(CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),[SQL]),':PDonemBas',''''+@YilIlkAnStr+''''),':PDonemSon',''''+@YilSonAnStr+'''') from DOKUMLER where RAPORADI =  'KDR_BANKA')			
			EXEC (@SQL)
	END
	IF @TIP='3' 
	BEGIN
			SET @SQL=(select replace(REPLACE(CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),[SQL]),':PDonemBas',''''+@YilIlkAnStr+''''),':PDonemSon',''''+@YilSonAnStr+'''') from DOKUMLER where RAPORADI =  'KDR_POS')
			EXEC (@SQL)
	END
	IF @TIP='4' 
	BEGIN
			SET @SQL=(select replace(REPLACE(CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),[SQL]),':PDonemBas',''''+@YilIlkAnStr+''''),':PDonemSon',''''+@YilSonAnStr+'''') from DOKUMLER where RAPORADI =  'KDR_ALINAN_ÇEK')
			EXEC (@SQL)
	END
	IF @TIP='5' 
	BEGIN
			SET @SQL=(select replace(REPLACE(CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),[SQL]),':PDonemBas',''''+@YilIlkAnStr+''''),':PDonemSon',''''+@YilSonAnStr+'''') from DOKUMLER where RAPORADI =  'KDR_ALINAN_SENET')
			EXEC (@SQL)
	END
	IF @TIP='6' 
	BEGIN
			SET @SQL=(select replace(REPLACE(CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),[SQL]),':PDonemBas',''''+@YilIlkAnStr+''''),':PDonemSon',''''+@YilSonAnStr+'''') from DOKUMLER where RAPORADI =  'KDR_KASA')
			EXEC (@SQL)
	END
	IF @TIP='7' 
	BEGIN
			SET @SQL=(select replace(REPLACE(CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),[SQL]),':PDonemBas',''''+@YilIlkAnStr+''''),':PDonemSon',''''+@YilSonAnStr+'''') from DOKUMLER where RAPORADI =  'KDR_STOK')
			EXEC (@SQL)
	END
	IF @TIP='8' 
	BEGIN			
			CREATE TABLE #TempResults1
				(	KOD NVARCHAR(1000),
					FIRMA NVARCHAR(1000),
					BAKIYE NVARCHAR(1000),
					KUR NVARCHAR(1000),
					TLKARSILIK NVARCHAR(1000));

			SET @SQL=(select replace(REPLACE(CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),[SQL]),':PDonemBas',''''+@YilIlkAnStr+''''),':PDonemSon',''''+@YilSonAnStr+'''') from DOKUMLER where RAPORADI =  'KDR_ALACAKLILAR')
			INSERT INTO #TempResults1 (KOD,FIRMA, BAKIYE,KUR,TLKARSILIK) -- Yeni sütunu dahil etmeden ekliyoruz
			EXEC(@SQL);		
			SELECT DETAY='Ekstre',* FROM #TempResults1;
			DROP TABLE #TempResults1;
	END
	IF @TIP='9' 
	BEGIN
			SET @SQL=(select replace(REPLACE(CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),[SQL]),':PDonemBas',''''+@YilIlkAnStr+''''),':PDonemSon',''''+@YilSonAnStr+'''') from DOKUMLER where RAPORADI =  'KDR_KREDÝLER')
			EXEC (@SQL)
	END
	IF @TIP='10' 
	BEGIN
			SET @SQL=(select replace(REPLACE(CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),[SQL]),':PDonemBas',''''+@YilIlkAnStr+''''),':PDonemSon',''''+@YilSonAnStr+'''') from DOKUMLER where RAPORADI =  'KDR_KREDÝ_KARTI')
			EXEC (@SQL)
	END
	IF @TIP='11' 
	BEGIN
			SET @SQL=(select replace(REPLACE(CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),[SQL]),':PDonemBas',''''+@YilIlkAnStr+''''),':PDonemSon',''''+@YilSonAnStr+'''') from DOKUMLER where RAPORADI =  'KDR_VERÝLEN_ÇEK')
			EXEC (@SQL)
	END
	IF @TIP='12' 
	BEGIN
			SET @SQL=(select replace(REPLACE(CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),[SQL]),':PDonemBas',''''+@YilIlkAnStr+''''),':PDonemSon',''''+@YilSonAnStr+'''') from DOKUMLER where RAPORADI =  'KDR_VERÝLEN_SENET')
			EXEC (@SQL)
	END
	IF @TIP='13' 
	BEGIN
			SET @SQL=(select replace(REPLACE(CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),[SQL]),':PDonemBas',''''+@YilIlkAnStr+''''),':PDonemSon',''''+@YilSonAnStr+'''') from DOKUMLER where RAPORADI =  'KDR_BORÇLULAR')
			EXEC (@SQL)
	END
end


===== PROC: TM_KdvOzet =====


-- exec [TM_KdvOzet] '2023-01-01','2023-12-31',''
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_KdvOzet](@BasTar DATETIME, @BitTar DATETIME, @KDVDURUM VARCHAR(20)=NULL)
AS
BEGIN 
SET @KDVDURUM=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@KDVDURUM,'')='' THEN '' END
SET @BasTar = @BasTar+' 00:00:00'
SET @BitTar = @BitTar+' 23:59:59'

SELECT KDV,TUR,
       KDVTUTARI =CASE WHEN TUR='Hesaplanan KDV' THEN SUM(KDVTUTARI)
		       WHEN TUR='Ýndirilen KDV'  THEN -1*SUM(KDVTUTARI) END,
       KDVMATRAHI=CASE WHEN TUR='Hesaplanan KDV' THEN SUM(MATRAH)
		       WHEN TUR='Ýndirilen KDV'  THEN -1*SUM(MATRAH) END
            
FROM (
SELECT 
	F.KDV, 
	TUR=CASE WHEN FB.TUR IN (15,16)   THEN 'Hesaplanan KDV'
		 WHEN FB.TUR IN (8,11,12) THEN 'Ýndirilen KDV' END,
	KDVTUTARI=SUM(CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' AND ISNULL(KDVMUHAFIYETI,0)=0 THEN (F.TUTAR*(1+(F.KDV/100.0)))-F.TUTAR
			   WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Dahil' AND ISNULL(KDVMUHAFIYETI,0)=0 THEN CONVERT(FLOAT,F.TUTAR)-(F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)))
                    
                           WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' AND ISNULL(KDVMUHAFIYETI,0)<>0 THEN ((F.TUTAR*(1+(F.KDV/100.0)))-F.TUTAR) * (1.0-(KDVMUHAFIYETI/100.0))
			   WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Dahil' AND ISNULL(KDVMUHAFIYETI,0)<>0 THEN (F.TUTAR-(F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)))) * (1.0-(KDVMUHAFIYETI/100.0))
				   END) ,
        MATRAH= SUM(CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç'  THEN CONVERT(FLOAT,F.TUTAR)
			 WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Dahil'  THEN F.TUTAR-(F.TUTAR-(F.TUTAR/(1+(F.KDV/100.0))))
				   END )
FROM
	FATURA AS F 
		INNER JOIN FATBASLIK FB ON 
			F.FATBASID = FB.ID 
		INNER JOIN ISLEMTURLERI IT ON 
			FB.TUR = IT.TUR and 
			FB.TIPI=IT.TIP
WHERE 
	    FB.FATURATARIH BETWEEN @BasTar AND @BitTar
	AND FB.TUR IN (8,11,12,15,16) AND ISNULL(FB.DURUM,0)<>6
GROUP BY 
	F.KDV,FB.TUR,IT.TIP
) AS X
WHERE 
    
    TUR LIKE @KDVDURUM+'%'
	
GROUP BY KDV,TUR

ORDER BY TUR,KDV
end


===== PROC: TM_MizanToplu =====


-- exec TM_MizanToplu '2023-01-01','2023-12-31'
create procedure [dbo].[TM_MizanToplu](@BasTar DATETIME, @BitTar DATETIME)
AS
BEGIN 

SET @BasTar = @BasTar+' 00:00:00'
SET @BitTar = @BitTar+' 23:59:59'
--Mizan Varlýklar
SELECT HESAPKODU,HESAPADI,
       BORC=ROUND(SUM(convert(FLOAT,BORC)),2),
       ALACAK=ROUND(SUM(convert(FLOAT,ALACAK)),2),
       BAKIYE=ROUND(SUM(convert(FLOAT,KALAN)),2),
       KUR,
       TLBORC=ROUND(SUM(convert(FLOAT,TLBORC)),2),
       TLALACAK=ROUND(SUM(convert(FLOAT,TLALACAK)),2),
       TLBAKIYE=ROUND(SUM(convert(FLOAT,TLKALAN)),2)
FROM(
-- pos 
SELECT  HESAPKODU,HESAPADI,BORC=SUM(BORC),ALACAK=SUM(ALACAK),KALAN=SUM(BORC)-SUM(ALACAK),KUR,TLBORC=SUM(BORC),TLALACAK=SUM(ALACAK),TLKALAN=SUM(BORC)-SUM(ALACAK),TLKUR='TL'
FROM(
	SELECT 
		ID,
		KODU as HESAPKODU,
		ADI as HESAPADI, 
		BORC= (SELECT ISNULL(SUM(B),0.0) FROM (
							select  B=  case when DOVIZ_KURU=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-10135) AND ALACAK>0.0 then K2.DOVIZ_TUTARI else K2.ALACAK end
							from KASA K2 (NOLOCK) 
							where  K1.ID=K2.HESAPID AND ISLEMTARIHI between @BasTar and @BitTar and K2.TUR not between 60 and 79 and K2.HESAPTURU='P' AND TUR <> 2) AS X ),
		ALACAK=(SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A),0.0) FROM (
							select A=           case when DOVIZ_KURU=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-10135) AND BORC>0.0 then K2.DOVIZ_TUTARI else K2.BORC end
							from KASA K2 (NOLOCK) where K1.ID=K2.HESAPID AND ISLEMTARIHI between @BasTar and @BitTar and K2.TUR not between 60 and 79 and K2.HESAPTURU='P' --AND TUR <> 2
				) AS X ),
		KUR='TL'	
	FROM 
		POS k1 (NOLOCK)
) AS X
WHERE ((BORC<>0.0) or (ALACAK<>0.0))
GROUP BY HESAPKODU,HESAPADI,KUR
union all
--KASA
SELECT HESAPKODU,HESAPADI,SUM(BORC) AS BORC,SUM(ALACAK) AS ALACAK,KALAN=SUM(BORC)-SUM(ALACAK),KUR,TLBORC=SUM(TLBORC),TLALACAK=SUM(TLALACAK),TLKALAN=SUM(TLBORC)-SUM(TLALACAK),TLKUR='TL'
FROM(  
	SELECT 
		ID,
		KASAKODU as HESAPKODU,
		KASAADI as HESAPADI, 
		BORC= (SELECT ISNULL(SUM(B),0) FROM (select  B=case when DOVIZ_KURU=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-10135) AND K2.ALACAK>0 then K2.DOVIZ_TUTARI when K2.TUR=88 then K2.DOVIZ_TUTARI else K2.ALACAK end from KASA K2 (NOLOCK)  where  K1.ID=K2.HESAPID AND ISLEMTARIHI between @BasTar and @BitTar and K2.TUR not between 60 and 79 AND ((K2.HESAPTURU ='K') or (K2.HESAPTURU='B' AND KASA IN (195,196)) ) --and K2.TUR <> 2
			) AS X ),
				ALACAK=(SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A),0) FROM (select  A= case when DOVIZ_KURU=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-10135) AND K2.BORC>0 then K2.DOVIZ_TUTARI 
		when K2.TUR=98 then K2.DOVIZ_TUTARI  else K2.BORC end from KASA K2 (NOLOCK) where K1.ID=K2.HESAPID AND ISLEMTARIHI between @BasTar and @BitTar and K2.TUR not between 60 and 79 AND ((K2.HESAPTURU ='K') or (K2.HESAPTURU='B' AND KASA IN (195,196)) ) --and K2.TUR <> 2
		) AS X ),
		TLBORC= (SELECT ISNULL(SUM(B),0) FROM (select  B=case when K2.ALACAK>0.0 then K2.DOVIZ_TUTARI when K2.TUR=88 then K2.DOVIZ_TUTARI else 0.0 end  from KASA K2 (NOLOCK) 
		where  K1.ID=K2.HESAPID AND ISLEMTARIHI between @BasTar and @BitTar and K2.TUR not between 60 and 79 AND ((K2.HESAPTURU ='K') or (K2.HESAPTURU='B' AND KASA IN (195,196)) ) --and K2.TUR <> 2
		) AS X ),
		TLALACAK=(SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A),0) FROM (select  A= case when K2.BORC>0.0 then K2.DOVIZ_TUTARI when K2.TUR=98 then K2.DOVIZ_TUTARI else 0.0 end from KASA K2 (NOLOCK)  where K1.ID=K2.HESAPID AND ISLEMTARIHI between @BasTar and @BitTar and K2.TUR not between 60 and 79 AND ((K2.HESAPTURU ='K') or (K2.HESAPTURU='B' AND KASA IN (195,196)) ) --and K2.TUR <> 2
		) AS X ),
		KUR='TL'  
	FROM KASALAR K1 (NOLOCK) 
) AS X
WHERE 
	((BORC<>0.0) or (ALACAK<>0.0) or (TLBORC<>0.0) or (TLALACAK<>0.0))
GROUP BY HESAPKODU,HESAPADI,KUR
--BANKA 
union all 
SELECT HESAPKODU,HESAPADI,BORC=SUM(BORC),ALACAK=SUM(ALACAK),KALAN=SUM(BORC)-SUM(ALACAK),KUR,TLBORC=SUM(TLBORC),TLALACAK=SUM(TLALACAK),TLKALAN=SUM(TLBORC)-SUM(TLALACAK),TLKUR='TL'
FROM(
	SELECT
		ID,
		HESAPKODU,
		HESAPADI, 
		BORC   =  (SELECT ISNULL(SUM(B),0) FROM (select B= K2.ALACAK from KASA K2 (NOLOCK) where  K1.ID=K2.HESAPID AND ISLEMTARIHI between @BasTar and @BitTar and K2.TUR not between 60 and 79 and K2.HESAPTURU='B' --and K2.TUR <> 2
                ) AS X ),
		ALACAK = (SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A),0) FROM (select  A= K2.BORC from KASA K2 (NOLOCK) where K1.ID=K2.HESAPID AND ISLEMTARIHI between @BasTar and @BitTar and K2.TUR not between 60 and 79 and K2.HESAPTURU='B' --and K2.TUR <> 2
                 ) AS X ), 

		TLBORC   =  (SELECT ISNULL(SUM(B),0) FROM (select  B= case when K2.TUR=88 then K2.DOVIZ_TUTARI when ALACAK>0.0 then (case when K2.DOVIZ_KURU='TL' then K2.DOVIZ_TUTARI else K2.ALACAK end)  else 0.0 end from KASA K2 (NOLOCK) where  K1.ID=K2.HESAPID AND ISLEMTARIHI between @BasTar and @BitTar and K2.TUR not between 60 and 79 and K2.HESAPTURU='B' --and K2.TUR <> 2 
                ) AS X ),
		TLALACAK = (SELECT ISNULL(SUM(A),0) FROM (select  A= case when K2.TUR=98 then K2.DOVIZ_TUTARI when BORC>0.0 then (case when K2.DOVIZ_KURU='TL' then K2.DOVIZ_TUTARI else K2.ALACAK end) else 0.0 end  from KASA K2 (NOLOCK) where K1.ID=K2.HESAPID AND ISLEMTARIHI between @BasTar and @BitTar and K2.TUR not between 60 and 79 and K2.HESAPTURU='B' --and K2.TUR <> 2
                ) AS X ),
		K1.KUR
	FROM BANKAHESAPLAR K1 (NOLOCK)  
) AS X
WHERE ((BORC<>0.0) or (ALACAK<>0.0) or (TLBORC<>0.0) or (TLALACAK<>0.0))
GROUP BY HESAPKODU,HESAPADI,KUR
--ÇEKLER
union all 
select HESAPKODU,HESAPADI,BORC=SUM(BORC),ALACAK=SUM(ALACAK),KALAN=SUM(BAKIYE),KUR,TLBORC=SUM(TLBORC),TLALACAK=SUM(TLALACAK),TLKALAN=SUM(TLBORC)-SUM(TLALACAK),TLKUR='TL'
from (
	select 
		HESAPKODU,HESAPADI,
		BORC=case when SUM(BAKIYE)>0.0 then SUM(BAKIYE) else 0.0 end,
		ALACAK=case when SUM(BAKIYE)>0.0 then 0.0 else -SUM(BAKIYE)end,
		BAKIYE=SUM(BAKIYE),
		KUR,
		TLBORC=case when SUM(TLBAKIYE)>0.0 then SUM(TLBAKIYE) else 0.0 end,
		TLALACAK=case when SUM(TLBAKIYE)>0.0 then 0.0 else -SUM(TLBAKIYE)end,
		TLBAKIYE=SUM(TLBAKIYE)
	from (
	--devir borc
		select 
			H.HESAPKODU,H.HESAPADI,BORC=C.TUTAR,ALACAK=0.0,BAKIYE=C.TUTAR,KUR=C.KUR,TLBORC=CH.DOVIZ_TUTARI,TLALACAK=0.0,TLBAKIYE=CH.DOVIZ_TUTARI
        from
			CEKLER C inner join 
			CEKHAREKET CH on C.ID=CH.CEKSENETLERID inner join 
			HESAPPLANI H on H.KUR=C.KUR and H.MUHASEBE=CH.ISLEM AND H.VARSAYILAN = C.CEKSENET
		where
			CH.TARIH < @BasTar

		union all
		--devir alacak
		select 
			H.HESAPKODU,H.HESAPADI,BORC=0.0,ALACAK=C.TUTAR,BAKIYE=-C.TUTAR,KUR=C.KUR,TLBORC=0.0,TLALACAK=CH.DOVIZ_TUTARI,TLBAKIYE=CH.DOVIZ_TUTARI
		from 
			CEKLER C inner join 
			CEKHAREKET CH on C.ID=CH.CEKSENETLERID inner join
			CEKHAREKET CHD on CH.GERIDONUSID=CHD.ID inner join 
			HESAPPLANI H on H.KUR=C.KUR and H.MUHASEBE=CHD.ISLEM AND H.VARSAYILAN = C.CEKSENET
		where 
			CH.TARIH < @BasTar 
	)as XX
	group by 
		HESAPKODU,HESAPADI,KUR

	union all
	--çekin ilgili tarihteki hareketi /hesabýn borcuna
	select 
		H.HESAPKODU,H.HESAPADI,BORC=C.TUTAR,ALACAK=0.0,BAKIYE=C.TUTAR,KUR=C.KUR,TLBORC=CH.DOVIZ_TUTARI,TLALACAK=0.0,TLBAKIYE=CH.DOVIZ_TUTARI
	from 

                    CEKLER C inner join 
                    CEKHAREKET CH on C.ID=CH.CEKSENETLERID inner join
                    HESAPPLANI H on H.KUR=C.KUR and H.MUHASEBE=CH.ISLEM AND H.VARSAYILAN = C.CEKSENET
             where CH.TARIH between @BasTar and @BitTar
             union all
             --çekin ilgili tarihteki hareketinin karþý hesabý /hesabýn alacaðýna
             select H.HESAPKODU,H.HESAPADI,BORC=0.0,ALACAK=C.TUTAR,BAKIYE=-C.TUTAR,KUR=C.KUR,TLBORC=0.0,TLALACAK=CH.DOVIZ_TUTARI,TLBAKIYE=CH.DOVIZ_TUTARI
             from 
                    CEKLER C inner join 
                    CEKHAREKET CH on C.ID=CH.CEKSENETLERID inner join 
                    CEKHAREKET CHD on CH.GERIDONUSID=CHD.ID inner join 
                    HESAPPLANI H on H.KUR=C.KUR and H.MUHASEBE=CHD.ISLEM AND H.VARSAYILAN = C.CEKSENET
             where CH.TARIH between @BasTar and @BitTar
)as XX
WHERE ((BORC<>0.0) or (ALACAK<>0.0) or (TLBORC<>0.0) or (TLALACAK<>0.0))
GROUP BY HESAPKODU,HESAPADI,KUR
) AS X
GROUP BY HESAPKODU,HESAPADI,KUR
--Mizan Borç Alacak
UNION ALL
SELECT
       XXX.KOD,
	   XXX.FIRMA,
       round(SUM(ISNULL(BORC,0)),2) as BORC,
       round(SUM(ISNULL(ALACAK,0)),2) as ALACAK,
       round(SUM(ISNULL(BAKIYE,0)),2) as BAKIYE,
       KUR,
       TLBORC=ROUND(SUM(convert(FLOAT,TLBORC)),2),
       TLALACAK=ROUND(SUM(convert(FLOAT,TLALACAK)),2),
       TLBAKIYE=ROUND(SUM(convert(FLOAT,TLBAKIYE)),2)
FROM (
select  DISTINCT R.SUBEID,R.KOD ,R.FIRMA,SINIFADI=GSnf.ANAHTAR,GRUPADI=GGrp.ANAHTAR,CONVERT(FLOAT,TOPLAM_BORC) AS BORC ,CONVERT(FLOAT,TOPLAM_ALACAK) AS ALACAK,KUR ,BAKIYE=CONVERT(FLOAT,TOPLAM_BORC)-CONVERT(FLOAT,TOPLAM_ALACAK),TLBORC,TLALACAK,TLBAKIYE=(TLBORC-TLALACAK)

FROM(
    select
		REHBERID,TOPLAM_BORC=SUM(isnull(BORC,0)),TOPLAM_ALACAK=SUM(isnull(ALACAK,0)),KUR=isnull(KUR,'TL'),TLBORC=SUM(isnull(TLBORC,0.0)),TLALACAK=SUM(isnull(TLALACAK,0.0))
	from (
		SELECT
			REHBERID,
			SUM(CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE F.FATURA_TUTARI END) AS BORC,
			0.0 AS ALACAK,
			CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_CINSI else KUR end as KUR,
			TLBORC=SUM(CONVERT(FLOAT,F.FATURA_TUTARI)),
			TLALACAK=0.0
			FROM FATBASLIK F
			WHERE  isnull(F.DURUM,0)<>6 and (F.TUR in (15,16,17))
			AND FATURATARIH BETWEEN @BasTar and @BitTar
			GROUP BY F.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_CINSI else KUR end

			UNION ALL

			SELECT REHBERID,
			0 AS BORC,
			SUM(CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE F.FATURA_TUTARI END) AS FATURA_ALACAK, -- alýnan fatura BORCA YAZ
			CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_CINSI else KUR end as KUR,
			TLBORC=0.0,
			TLALACAK=SUM(CONVERT(FLOAT,F.FATURA_TUTARI))
			FROM FATBASLIK F
			WHERE  isnull(F.DURUM,0)<>6 and (F.TUR in (8,11,12,13))
			AND FATURATARIH BETWEEN @BasTar and @BitTar
			GROUP BY F.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_CINSI else KUR end

			UNION ALL

			SELECT REHBERID,
			SUM(case when ISNULL(K.BORC,0)>0 and EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 then K.DOVIZ_TUTARI else K.BORC end) AS  BORC,
			SUM(case when ISNULL(K.ALACAK,0)>0 and EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 then K.DOVIZ_TUTARI else K.ALACAK end) AS ALACAK,
			CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1  THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end as KUR,
			TLBORC=sum(case when (K.TUR=88) then K.DOVIZ_TUTARI
			                                            when (K.KUR='TL' and K.BORC>0.0) then K.BORC
			                                                when (K.DOVIZ_KURU='TL' and K.BORC>0.0) then K.DOVIZ_TUTARI
			                                                else 0.0 end),
			TLALACAK=sum(case when (K.TUR=98) then K.DOVIZ_TUTARI
			                                            when (K.KUR='TL' and K.ALACAK>0.0) then K.ALACAK
			                                            when (K.DOVIZ_KURU='TL' and K.ALACAK>0.0) then K.DOVIZ_TUTARI
			                                            else 0.0 end)
			FROM KASA K
			WHERE TUR not between 60 and 79  AND
			ISLEMTARIHI  BETWEEN @BasTar and @BitTar
			GROUP BY K.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end

			UNION ALL
			SELECT CH.REHBERID,
			BORC=SUM(Case when CH.ISLEM in(140,131,132,133,134,137) then (CASE WHEN c.EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN C.DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE c.TUTAR END) else 0.0 end),
			ALACAK=SUM(Case when CH.ISLEM in(130,141) then (CASE WHEN c.EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN C.DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE c.TUTAR END) else 0.0 end),
			CASE WHEN c.EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN C.DOVIZ_KURU else c.KUR end as KUR,
			TLBORC=SUM(Case when CH.ISLEM in(140,131,132,133,134,137) then C.DOVIZ_TUTARI else 0.0 end),
			TLALACAK=SUM(Case when CH.ISLEM in(130,141) then C.DOVIZ_TUTARI else 0.0 end)
			FROM CEKLER C inner join CEKHAREKET CH on C.ID=CH.CEKSENETLERID
			WHERE CH.ISLEM in(130,131,132,134,137,140,141) and
			CH.TARIH  BETWEEN @BasTar and @BitTar
			GROUP BY CH.REHBERID,CASE WHEN c.EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN C.DOVIZ_KURU else c.KUR end

			UNION ALL

			SELECT REHBERID,
			0 AS BORC,
			SUM(CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE TUTAR END) AS ALACAK,
			CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end as KUR,
			TLBORC=0.0,
			TLALACAK=SUM(S.DOVIZ_TUTARI)
			FROM SENETLER S
			WHERE TUR = 24
			AND TARIH  BETWEEN @BasTar and @BitTar
			GROUP BY S.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR END

			UNION ALL

			SELECT REHBERID,SUM(CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_TUTARI ELSE TUTAR END) AS
			BORC,0 AS ALACAK,
			CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end as KUR,
			TLBORC=SUM(S.DOVIZ_TUTARI),
			TLALACAK=0.0
			FROM SENETLER S
			WHERE TUR = 34
			AND TARIH  BETWEEN @BasTar and @BitTar
			GROUP BY S.REHBERID,CASE WHEN EKSTREDEKULLAN=1 THEN DOVIZ_KURU else KUR end

           ) as asd
           group by REHBERID,KUR) AS DSA
                               INNER JOIN REHBER R ON            DSA.REHBERID=R.ID
                               LEFT OUTER JOIN             REHBERPERSONEL P on R.ID = P.REHBERID
                               left outer join REHBER R2 on R2.ID=R.TEMSILCI
                               left outer join (Select R1.ID,RB.BILGI from REHBER R1
                               left outer join REHBERILETISIM RI on R1.ID=RI.REHBERID
                               Left outer join REHBERBILGI RB on RB.YER_ID=RI.ID
                               left outer join REHBERAYAR RA on RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA Where RI.VARSAYILAN=1 and RB.YERI=1 and RA.VARSAYILAN=8) X on X.ID=R.ID
                               left outer join GENINI GSnf on R.SINIF=GSnf.DEGER and GSnf.BOLUM=-2203 and GSnf.DIL=-1 --2203  sýnýf
                               left outer join GENINI GGrp on R.GRUP=GGrp.DEGER and GGrp.BOLUM=-2202 and GGrp.DIL=-1  --2202  grup

 where R.GRUP<>335 and R.ID > 0
 and R.DURUM=1  and  isnull(R.ID,'')<>''
   ) AS XXX
GROUP BY
	KOD,FIRMA,KUR
--Mizan Masraf Gelir Gider
UNION ALL
SELECT
	MASRAFKOD,MASRAFAD,
	ROUND(ISNULL(SUM(BORC),0),2) AS BORC,
	ROUND(ISNULL(SUM(ALACAK),0),2) AS ALACAK,
	BAKIYE=ROUND(ISNULL(SUM(BORC),0),2)-ROUND(ISNULL(SUM(ALACAK),0),2),
	KUR,
	TLBORC=ROUND(ISNULL(SUM(BORC),0),2),
	TLALACAK=ROUND(ISNULL(SUM(ALACAK),0),2),
	TLBAKIYE=ROUND(ISNULL(SUM(BORC),0),2)-ROUND(ISNULL(SUM(ALACAK),0),2)
FROM (
	SELECT * FROM (
	SELECT
		FB.SUBEID,
		F.ID,                         
		FB.TUR,
		IT.AD,
		FB.FATURANO AS BELGENO,                  
		FB.FATURATARIH AS ISLEMTARIHI,
		MG.KOD AS MASRAFKOD,
		MG.AD AS MASRAFAD,
		R.KOD AS CARIKOD,
		R.FIRMA AS CARIAD,
		KODU= CASE WHEN F.TUR = 1 THEN (SELECT KOD FROM STOKLAR WHERE ID = F.URUNID ) ELSE (SELECT AD FROM MASRAFGELIR WHERE ID = F.URUNID) END,
		ADI = CASE WHEN F.TUR = 1 THEN (SELECT STOKADI FROM STOKLAR WHERE ID = F.URUNID ) ELSE (SELECT AD FROM MASRAFGELIR WHERE ID = F.URUNID) END,
		BORC=CASE WHEN FB.TUR IN (11,12) AND FB.TIPI<>4 and FB.KUR IN ('€','$') THEN F.DOVIZ_TUTARI /(1+(F.KDV/100.0))
				  WHEN FB.TUR IN (11,12) AND FB.TIPI=4  and FB.KUR IN ('€','$') THEN F.DOVIZ_TUTARI /(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) + ISNULL((-1*FB.EKVERGI),0)
				  WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Dahil' AND FB.TUR IN (11,12) AND FB.TIPI<>4 and FB.KUR NOT IN ('€','$') THEN (F.TUTAR)/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) 
				  WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' AND FB.TUR IN (11,12) AND FB.TIPI<>4 and FB.KUR NOT IN ('€','$') THEN (F.TUTAR)
				  WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Dahil' AND FB.TUR IN (11,12) AND FB.TIPI=4  and FB.KUR NOT IN ('€','$') THEN (F.TUTAR)/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) + ISNULL((-1*FB.EKVERGI),0)
				  WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' AND FB.TUR IN (11,12) AND FB.TIPI=4  and FB.KUR NOT IN ('€','$') THEN (F.TUTAR)  + ISNULL((-1*FB.EKVERGI),0)
				END,
		ALACAK=CASE WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Dahil' AND FB.TUR IN (15,16) and FB.KUR IN ('€','$') THEN (F.DOVIZ_TUTARI)/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) 
					WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' AND FB.TUR IN (15,16) and FB.KUR IN ('€','$') THEN (CONVERT(FLOAT,F.DOVIZ_TUTARI))

					WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Dahil' AND FB.TUR IN (15,16) and FB.KUR NOT IN ('€','$') THEN (F.TUTAR)/(1+(F.KDV/100.0)) 
					WHEN FB.KDVDURUM='Hariç' AND FB.TUR IN (15,16) and FB.KUR NOT IN ('€','$') THEN (CONVERT(FLOAT,F.TUTAR))
		END,
		F.KUR
		FROM FATURA F INNER JOIN FATBASLIK FB ON F.FATBASID=FB.ID
														 LEFT JOIN MASRAFGELIR MG ON F.TUR=0 AND F.URUNID=MG.ID
														 LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R ON F.REHBERID=R.ID
														 LEFT OUTER JOIN KULLANICI KL ON R.ID=KL.REHBERID
						LEFT JOIN ISLEMTURLERI IT ON FB.TUR=IT.TUR AND IT.TIP IN (0,1)
		WHERE 
			 FB.FATURATARIH BETWEEN @BasTar and @BitTar
			 AND FB.TUR IN (11,12,15,16,1)
	) AS X
	WHERE ISNULL(MASRAFKOD,'')<>''
	UNION ALL
	SELECT * FROM (
		SELECT
		FB.SUBEID,
		F.ID,
		FB.TUR,
		IT.AD,
		FB.FATURANO AS BELGENO,                  
		FB.FATURATARIH AS ISLEMTARIHI,
		MASRAFKOD=(SELECT KOD FROM MASRAFGELIR MG WHERE KOD=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-24045)),
		MASRAFAD=(SELECT AD FROM MASRAFGELIR MG WHERE KOD=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-24045)),
		R.KOD AS CARIKOD,
		R.FIRMA AS CARIAD,
		KODU= (SELECT KOD FROM MASRAFGELIR MG WHERE KOD=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-24045)),
		ADI = (SELECT AD FROM MASRAFGELIR MG WHERE KOD=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-24045)),
		BORC=0,
		ALACAK=-1*EKVERGI,
		KUR='TL'
		FROM FATURA F INNER JOIN FATBASLIK FB ON F.FATBASID=FB.ID
														 LEFT JOIN MASRAFGELIR MG ON F.TUR=0 AND F.URUNID=MG.ID
														 LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R ON F.REHBERID=R.ID
														 LEFT OUTER JOIN KULLANICI KL ON R.ID=KL.REHBERID
						LEFT JOIN ISLEMTURLERI IT ON FB.TUR=IT.TUR AND IT.TIP IN (0,1)
		WHERE 
			 FB.FATURATARIH BETWEEN @BasTar and @BitTar
			 AND FB.TUR IN (11)
			 AND FB.TIPI = 4
	) AS X
	WHERE ISNULL(MASRAFKOD,'')<>''
	UNION ALL
	SELECT * FROM (
		SELECT
		FB.SUBEID,
		FB.ID,
		FB.TUR,  
		IT.AD,  
		FB.FATURANO AS BELGENO,            
		FB.FATURATARIH AS ISLEMTARIHI,
		MG.KOD AS MASRAFKOD,
		MG.AD AS MASRAFAD,
		R.KOD AS CARIKOD,
		R.FIRMA AS CARIAD,
		KODU= MG.KOD,
		ADI = MG.AD,
		BORC   = case when FB.TUR=13 and FB.KUR IN ('€','$') then FB.DOVIZ_TUTARI
					  when FB.TUR=13 and FB.KUR NOT IN ('€','$') then FB.FATURA_TUTARI END ,
		ALACAK = case when FB.TUR=17 and FB.KUR IN ('€','$') then FB.DOVIZ_TUTARI
					  when FB.TUR=17 and FB.KUR NOT IN ('€','$') then FB.FATURA_TUTARI END,
		KUR='TL'

		FROM FATBASLIK FB 
														 LEFT JOIN MASRAFGELIR MG ON FB.MASRAFID=MG.ID AND ISNULL(FB.DURUM,0)<>6
														 LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R ON FB.REHBERID=R.ID
														 LEFT OUTER JOIN KULLANICI KL ON R.ID=KL.REHBERID
						LEFT JOIN ISLEMTURLERI IT ON FB.TUR=IT.TUR AND IT.TIP IN (0,1)
		WHERE 
			 FB.FATURATARIH BETWEEN @BasTar and @BitTar
			 and FB.TUR IN (13,17,1)
	) AS X
	WHERE ISNULL(MASRAFKOD,'')<>''
	UNION ALL
	SELECT * FROM (
		Select 
		K.SUBEID, 
		K.ID,
		K.TUR,
		IT.AD,
		K.BELGENO,
		ISLEMTARIHI, 
		MASRAFKOD=M.KOD, MASRAFAD=M.AD,
		CARIKOD = R.KOD, 
		CARIAD  = R.FIRMA, 
		KODU    = M.KOD,
		ADI     = M.AD,
		BORC    = CASE	when K.TUR=1 and K.KUR <> 'TL' and ALACAK>0.0 then K.DOVIZ_TUTARI
						when K.TUR=1 and K.KUR = 'TL' and ALACAK>0.0 then K.ALACAK
						WHEN K.TUR<>1 and K.KUR <> 'TL' AND BORC>0 THEN K.DOVIZ_TUTARI 
						WHEN K.TUR<>1 and K.KUR = 'TL' AND BORC>0 THEN K.BORC 
						END, 
		 ALACAK  = CASE when K.TUR=1 and K.KUR <> 'TL' and BORC>0.0 then K.DOVIZ_TUTARI
						when K.TUR=1 and K.KUR = 'TL' and BORC>0.0 then K.BORC
						WHEN K.TUR<>1 and K.KUR <> 'TL' AND ALACAK>0 THEN K.DOVIZ_TUTARI 
						WHEN K.TUR<>1 and K.KUR = 'TL' AND ALACAK>0 THEN K.ALACAK 
						END,  

		KUR     = 'TL'
		From KASA K (NOLOCK)
									   left outer join REHBER R on K.REHBERID=R.ID left outer join KASALAR KS on KS.ID= K.HESAPID  
									   left outer join MASRAFGELIR M on M.ID=K.MASRAFID
									   LEFT JOIN ISLEMTURLERI IT ON K.TUR=IT.TUR AND IT.TIP IN (0,1)
		Where 
			K.REHBERID=0 and 
			K.ISLEMTARIHI BETWEEN @BasTar and @BitTar  and
			K.TUR IN (21,22,23,31,32,33,35,44,58,1) 
	) AS XYZ
	WHERE ISNULL(MASRAFKOD,'')<>''       
 ) AS X 
GROUP BY MASRAFKOD,MASRAFAD,KUR
end


===== PROC: TM_MobilAyarlar =====

-- EXEC TM_MobilAyarlar 'kontrol'
--select * from yetký order by ID DESC
CREATE PROC [dbo].[TM_MobilAyarlar](@KULLANICI VARCHAR(50)) 
AS
begin
SELECT * FROM TMMOBILAYARLAR 
where
ANAHTAR NOT LIKE 'Btn_%'
UNION ALL

------ana ekran görme yetkileri---------------
SELECT 
	ID=ISNULL(Y.ID,0),
	ANAHTAR='Btn_'+@KULLANICI,
	DEGER=case when MD.MODULID='8005' THEN 'BtnSiparis'
			   when MD.MODULID='8006' THEN 'BtnFatura'
			   when MD.MODULID='8010' THEN 'BtnUretimEmri'
			   when MD.MODULID='8009' THEN 'UretimHizliGiris'
			   when MD.MODULID='801011' THEN 'BtnEk1'
			   when MD.MODULID='801012' THEN 'BtnEk2'
			   when MD.MODULID='801013' THEN 'BtnEk3'
			   when MD.MODULID='80105001' THEN 'BtnCalisma'
			   when MD.MODULID='80105002' THEN 'BtnOlcum'
			   when MD.MODULID='80105003' THEN 'BtnEkSatir1'
			   when MD.MODULID='80105004' THEN 'BtnEkSatir2'
			   when MD.MODULID='8015' THEN 'BtnKararDestek'
			   when MD.MODULID='8020' THEN 'BtnYonetimRaporlari'
			   when MD.MODULID='8025' THEN 'BtnDonusumRaporlari'
			   when MD.MODULID='802501' THEN 'BtnUretimEmriDonusum'
			   when MD.MODULID='802502' THEN 'BtnSiparisDonusum'
			   when MD.MODULID='802503' THEN 'BtnAlisDonusumleri'			   
			   when MD.MODULID='802504' THEN 'BtnSatisDonusum'
			   when MD.MODULID='8030' THEN 'BtnSAPRapor'			   
			   when MD.MODULID='21' THEN 'BtnCrm'
			   when MD.MODULID='2105' THEN 'BtnPotansiyel'
			   when MD.MODULID='2121' THEN 'BtnSatis'
			   when MD.MODULID='2131' THEN 'BtnIsListesi'
			   when MD.MODULID='2201' THEN 'BtnCari'
			   when MD.MODULID='32' THEN 'BtnDokuman'
			   when MD.MODULID='8035' THEN 'BtnDashBoard'  else CONVERT(VARCHAR(50),MD.MODULID) 
			   END,
	YETKI=CASE WHEN ISNULL(RO.TY,0)=0 THEN case when ISNULL(HAK,'0')='0' THEN 'False' else 'True' END ELSE 'True' end
	
FROM REHBER R INNER JOIN KULLANICI K ON K.REHBERID=R.ID
			  INNER JOIN YETKI Y ON K.ROLID=Y.ROLID AND (Y.MODULID LIKE '80_%' or Y.MODULID LIKE '21%' or Y.MODULID LIKE '2201' or MODULID LIKE '32') AND TUR=1
			  INNER JOIN REHBERILETISIM RI ON RI.REHBERID=R.ID
			  INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RB.YER_ID=RI.ID AND RB.ETIKET='Eposta' AND (R.FIRMA = @KULLANICI or ISNULL(RB.BILGI,'')=@KULLANICI) 
			  FULL OUTER JOIN (select MODULID from MODUL where MODULID LIKE '80%' or MODULID LIKE '21%' or MODULID LIKE '2201' or MODULID LIKE '32') MD ON MD.MODULID=Y.MODULID
			  LEFT OUTER JOIN ROLLER RO ON RO.ID=K.ROLID
where MD.MODULID<>''
UNION ALL

SELECT ID=-1,ANAHTAR='Btn_Kontrol',DEGER='BtnFatura', YETKI='True' --fatura butonu için ekledim gentegrenin içine yetki eklenince kaldýrýcam

UNION ALL
------ekleme yetkileri---------------
SELECT 
	ID=ISNULL(Y.ID,0),
	ANAHTAR='Btn_'+@KULLANICI,
	DEGER=case when Y.MODULID='2105' THEN 'PotansiyelEkle'
			   when Y.MODULID='2201' THEN 'CariEkle' else CONVERT(VARCHAR(50),Y.MODULID) END,
	YETKI=CASE WHEN ISNULL(RO.TY,0)=0 THEN case when ISNULL(HAK,'0')='0' THEN 'False' else 'True' END ELSE 'True' end
	
FROM REHBER R INNER JOIN KULLANICI K ON K.REHBERID=R.ID
			  INNER JOIN YETKI Y ON K.ROLID=Y.ROLID AND Y.MODULID IN ('2105','2201') AND TUR=2
			  INNER JOIN REHBERILETISIM RI ON RI.REHBERID=R.ID
			  INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RB.YER_ID=RI.ID AND RB.ETIKET='Eposta' AND (R.FIRMA = @KULLANICI or ISNULL(RB.BILGI,'')=@KULLANICI) 
			  LEFT OUTER JOIN ROLLER RO ON RO.ID=K.ROLID
			  --FULL OUTER JOIN (select MODULID from MODUL where MODULID LIKE '80%' or MODULID LIKE '21%' or MODULID LIKE '2201' or MODULID LIKE '32') MD ON MD.MODULID=Y.MODULID
------degiþtirme yetkileri---------------
UNION ALL 
SELECT 
	ID=ISNULL(Y.ID,0),
	ANAHTAR='Btn_'+@KULLANICI,
	DEGER=case when Y.MODULID='2105' THEN 'PotansiyelDegistirme'
			   when Y.MODULID='2201' THEN 'CariDegistirme' else CONVERT(VARCHAR(50),Y.MODULID) END,
	YETKI=CASE WHEN ISNULL(RO.TY,0)=0 THEN case when ISNULL(HAK,'0')='0' THEN 'False' else 'True' END ELSE 'True' end
	
FROM REHBER R INNER JOIN KULLANICI K ON K.REHBERID=R.ID
			  INNER JOIN YETKI Y ON K.ROLID=Y.ROLID AND Y.MODULID IN ('2105','2201') AND TUR=3
			  INNER JOIN REHBERILETISIM RI ON RI.REHBERID=R.ID
			  INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RB.YER_ID=RI.ID AND RB.ETIKET='Eposta' AND (R.FIRMA = @KULLANICI or ISNULL(RB.BILGI,'')=@KULLANICI) 
			  LEFT OUTER JOIN ROLLER RO ON RO.ID=K.ROLID
			  --FULL OUTER JOIN (select MODULID from MODUL where MODULID LIKE '80%' or MODULID LIKE '21%' or MODULID LIKE '2201' or MODULID LIKE '32') MD ON MD.MODULID=Y.MODULID
 ------görme yetkileri---------------
UNION ALL 
SELECT 
	ID=ISNULL(Y.ID,0),
	ANAHTAR='Btn_'+@KULLANICI,
	DEGER=case when Y.MODULID='2431' THEN 'UcretGorme' else CONVERT(VARCHAR(50),Y.MODULID) END,
	YETKI=CASE WHEN ISNULL(RO.TY,0)=0 THEN case when ISNULL(HAK,'0')='0' THEN 'False' else 'True' END ELSE 'True' end
	
FROM REHBER R INNER JOIN KULLANICI K ON K.REHBERID=R.ID
			  INNER JOIN YETKI Y ON K.ROLID=Y.ROLID AND Y.MODULID IN ('2431') AND TUR=1
			  INNER JOIN REHBERILETISIM RI ON RI.REHBERID=R.ID
			  INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RB.YER_ID=RI.ID AND RB.ETIKET='Eposta' AND (R.FIRMA = @KULLANICI or ISNULL(RB.BILGI,'')=@KULLANICI) 
			  LEFT OUTER JOIN ROLLER RO ON RO.ID=K.ROLID
			  --FULL OUTER JOIN (select MODULID from MODUL where MODULID LIKE '80%' or MODULID LIKE '21%' or MODULID LIKE '2201' or MODULID LIKE '32') MD ON MD.MODULID=Y.MODULID
UNION ALL

------------depo yetkileri----------
SELECT 
	ID=ISNULL(Y.ID,0),
	ANAHTAR='DEPOLISTESI',
	DEGER=D.DEPOADI,
	YETKI=CASE WHEN ISNULL(RO.TY,0)=0 THEN case when ISNULL(HAK,'0')='0' THEN 'False' else 'True' END ELSE 'True' end
	
FROM REHBER R INNER JOIN KULLANICI K ON K.REHBERID=R.ID
			  INNER JOIN YETKI Y ON K.ROLID=Y.ROLID AND Y.MODULID LIKE '2470%' AND TUR=1
			  INNER JOIN REHBERILETISIM RI ON RI.REHBERID=R.ID
			  INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RB.YER_ID=RI.ID AND RB.ETIKET='Eposta' AND (R.FIRMA = @KULLANICI or ISNULL(RB.BILGI,'')=@KULLANICI) 
			  --FULL OUTER JOIN (select MODULID from MODUL where MODULID LIKE '80%' or MODULID LIKE '21%' or MODULID LIKE '2201' or MODULID LIKE '32') MD ON MD.MODULID=Y.MODULID 
			  INNER JOIN DEPOLAR D ON D.DURUM > 0 and MODULID = '2470' +  CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), D.ID) 
			  LEFT OUTER JOIN ROLLER RO ON RO.ID=K.ROLID
WHERE ISNULL(HAK,'0')='1'

end


===== PROC: TM_MobilAyarlar1 =====



 
CREATE PROC [dbo].[TM_MobilAyarlar1](@KULLANICI VARCHAR(50)) 
AS
begin
SELECT * FROM TMMOBILAYARLAR 
where
ANAHTAR NOT LIKE 'Btn_%'
UNION ALL
SELECT * FROM TMMOBILAYARLAR 
where
ANAHTAR LIKE 'Btn_'+@KULLANICI+'%'

end


===== PROC: TM_PotansiyelDetay =====

-- EXEC TM_PotansiyelDetay 4130, 'Listele','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','',''
 
CREATE PROC [dbo].[TM_PotansiyelDetay](@ID FLOAT, @TIP VARCHAR(20), @FIRMA VARCHAR(200), @GRUP VARCHAR(100), @TEMAS VARCHAR(100), @SEKTOR  VARCHAR(100), @KATEGORI VARCHAR(100), @SINIF VARCHAR(100), @DURUM VARCHAR(100), @BOLGE VARCHAR(100), @ALTBOLGE VARCHAR(100), @OZELKOD VARCHAR(100), @TEMSILCI VARCHAR(100), @ILETISIMISTEL VARCHAR(100), @ILETISIMADRES VARCHAR(200), @ILETISIMILCE VARCHAR(100), @ILETISIMIL VARCHAR(100), @ILETISIMEPOSTA VARCHAR(100), @ILETISIMWEB VARCHAR(100), @VD VARCHAR(100), @VNO VARCHAR(100), @EKLEYEN VARCHAR(100), @ILGILIJSON VARCHAR(2000), @VARSAYILANKOD VARCHAR(20), @FATBASLIK VARCHAR(100)) 
AS
begin
BEGIN TRY
DECLARE @RI INT, @DIGITSAY INT
					


if @TIP='Listele'
begin
SELECT 
	R.ID, 
	R.KOD, 
	R.FIRMA, 
	ADSOYAD='',--P.FIRMA, 
	GRUP=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2200 AND DEGER=R.GRUP), 
	TEMAS=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2207 AND DEGER=R.TEMAS), 
	SEKTOR=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2204 AND DEGER=R.SEKTOR), 
	KATEGORI=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2208 AND DEGER=R.KATEGORI), 
	SINIF=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2203 AND DEGER=R.SINIF), 
	DURUM=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2201 AND DEGER=R.DURUM), 
	BOLGE=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-2210 and DIL=-1 AND DEGER=R.BOLGE), 	
	ALTBOLGE=(select ANAHTAR from GENINI where BOLUM=(select CAST(CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),BOLUM)+''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),DEGER) AS INT)from GENINI   where  BOLUM=-2210 and DIL=-1 AND DEGER = R.BOLGE) and DIL=-1 and DEGER=R.ALTBOLGE),
	R.OZELKOD,
	R.TEMSILCI,
	TEMSILCIADI=(select R1.FIRMA from REHBER R1 WHERE R1.ID=R.TEMSILCI ),
	ILETISIMISTEL=(SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýþ Tel' AND RB.YER_ID=RI.ID),
	ILETISIMADRES=(SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Adres' AND RB.YER_ID=RI.ID),
	ILETISIMILCE=(SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýlçe' AND RB.YER_ID=RI.ID),
	ILETISIMIL=(SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýl' AND RB.YER_ID=RI.ID),
	ILETISIMEPOSTA=(SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Eposta' AND RB.YER_ID=RI.ID),
	ILETISIMWEB=(SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Web' AND RB.YER_ID=RI.ID),		
	VD=(SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Vergi Dairesi' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID),
	VNO=(SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Vergi No' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID),
	ILGILILER=(SELECT JSOND=(select DISTINCT
							ILGILIAD=R1.FIRMA,
							ILGILIFIRMAID=R1.ID,
							ILGILIISTEL=ISNULL((SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýþ Tel' AND RB.YER_ID=RI1.ID),''),
							ILGILICEPTEL=ISNULL((SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Cep Tel' AND RB.YER_ID=RI1.ID),''),	
							ILGILIEPOSTA=ISNULL((SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Eposta' AND RB.YER_ID=RI1.ID),''),
							ILGILIGOREVI=ISNULL((SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Görevi' AND RB.YER_ID=RI1.ID),''),
							ILGILIILETISIMI=ISNULL((SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýletiþimi' AND RB.YER_ID=RI1.ID),'') 
						from REHBER R1 LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERILETISIM RI1 ON R1.ID=RI1.REHBERID	where R1.BAGID=R.ID order by 1	
				FOR  JSON PATH, root('results'))),
	FATBASLIK=(SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Fatura Baþlýðý' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID)
FROM REHBER R LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERILETISIM RI ON R.ID=RI.REHBERID				
WHERE R.ID=@ID
end 

if @TIP='Guncelle'
begin
		if @ID<>0
		begin
		UPDATE R SET 
			R.FIRMA=@FIRMA,
			GRUP=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2200 AND ANAHTAR=@GRUP), 
			TEMAS=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2207 AND ANAHTAR=@TEMAS), 
			SEKTOR=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2204 AND ANAHTAR=@SEKTOR), 
			KATEGORI=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2208 AND ANAHTAR=@KATEGORI), 
			SINIF=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2203 AND ANAHTAR=@SINIF), 
			DURUM=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2201 AND ANAHTAR=@DURUM), 
			BOLGE=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-2210 and DIL=-1 AND ANAHTAR=@BOLGE), 	
			ALTBOLGE=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM LIKE '-2210'+(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-2210 and DIL=-1 AND ANAHTAR=@BOLGE) and DIL=-1 AND ANAHTAR=@ALTBOLGE), 	
			OZELKOD=@OZELKOD,
			TEMSILCI=@TEMSILCI
		FROM REHBER R
		WHERE ID=@ID
		end else
		BEGIN
			DECLARE @KOD VARCHAR(20), @SONKISIM FLOAT
			if @VARSAYILANKOD<>''
			begin
					Select @DIGITSAY=isnull(DIGITSAY,2) from HESAPPLANI where HESAPKODU= @VARSAYILANKOD

					Select top 1
						@SONKISIM=CASE WHEN ISNULL(CHARINDEX('.',KOD),0)<1 THEN KOD ELSE  REVERSE( SUBSTRING(REVERSE(KOD),1,CHARINDEX('.',REVERSE(KOD),1)-1)) END,--ROOTKOD=REVERSE( SUBSTRING(REVERSE(KOD),CHARINDEX('.',REVERSE(KOD),1)+1,LEN(KOD)-(CHARINDEX('.',REVERSE(KOD),1)-1))),KOD ,
						@KOD = REVERSE(SUBSTRING(REVERSE(KOD), CHARINDEX('.', REVERSE(KOD), 1) + 1, LEN(KOD) - (CHARINDEX('.', REVERSE(KOD), 1) - 1))) 
       + '.' 
       + RIGHT(
            REPLICATE('0', @DIGITSAY) + 
            CONVERT(VARCHAR(30), 
                CAST(CASE WHEN ISNULL(CHARINDEX('.', KOD), 0) < 1 THEN KOD ELSE REVERSE(SUBSTRING(REVERSE(KOD), 1, CHARINDEX('.', REVERSE(KOD), 1) - 1)) END AS INT) + 1
            ), 
            @DIGITSAY
         )
					from REHBER where KOD like @VARSAYILANKOD+'%' and LEN(CASE WHEN ISNULL(CHARINDEX('.',KOD),0)<1 THEN KOD ELSE  REVERSE( SUBSTRING(REVERSE(KOD),1,CHARINDEX('.',REVERSE(KOD),1)-1)) END) >= @DIGITSAY order by LEN(KOD) desc , 1 desc
			end else
			begin					
					SET @KOD = convert(varchar(10),(select isnull(max(ID),0) + 1 from REHBER))
			end
			INSERT INTO REHBER (KOD,FIRMA,GRUP,SINIF,SEKTOR,KATEGORI,BOLGE,ALTBOLGE,DURUM,TEMSILCI,EKLEYEN,EKLEMETARIHI,SUBEID,TEMAS, OZELKOD) 
			SELECT @KOD,@FIRMA,
					GRUP=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2200 AND ANAHTAR=@GRUP), 
					SINIF=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2203 AND ANAHTAR=@SINIF), 
					SEKTOR=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2204 AND ANAHTAR=@SEKTOR), 
					KATEGORI=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2208 AND ANAHTAR=@KATEGORI), 				
					BOLGE=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-2210 and DIL=-1 AND ANAHTAR=@BOLGE), 	
					ALTBOLGE=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM LIKE '-2210'+(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-2210 and DIL=-1 AND ANAHTAR=@BOLGE) and DIL=-1 AND ANAHTAR=@ALTBOLGE), 	
					DURUM=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2201 AND ANAHTAR=@DURUM), 								
					TEMSILCI=@TEMSILCI,
					@EKLEYEN,
					EKLEMETARIHI=GETDATE(),
					SUBEID=-1,
					TEMAS=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2207 AND ANAHTAR=@TEMAS),
					@OZELKOD
				SET @ID = (SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY())
		END


		

		SET @RI=0
		SELECT @RI=RI.ID FROM REHBER R LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERILETISIM RI ON R.ID=RI.REHBERID WHERE R.ID=@ID
		IF ISNULL(@RI,0)=0
		BEGIN
				INSERT INTO REHBERILETISIM (REHBERID,AD,VARSAYILAN,AKTIF,EKLEYEN,EKLEMETARIHI,SUBEID)
				SELECT @ID,'Merkez',1,(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2201 AND ANAHTAR=@DURUM),@EKLEYEN,GETDATE(),'-1'
				SET @RI = (SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY())
		END




		UPDATE REHBERBILGI SET BILGI=@ILETISIMISTEL WHERE YER_ID=@RI AND YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýþ Tel' 
		IF @@ROWCOUNT=0  
		BEGIN 
			INSERT INTO REHBERBILGI (YERI,YER_ID,SIRA,ETIKET,BILGI,EKLEYEN) 
			SELECT 1,@RI,(SELECT SIRA FROM REHBERAYAR WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýþ Tel'),'Ýþ Tel',@ILETISIMISTEL,@EKLEYEN
		END
		UPDATE REHBERBILGI SET BILGI=@ILETISIMADRES WHERE YER_ID=@RI AND YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Adres' 
		IF @@ROWCOUNT=0  
		BEGIN 
			INSERT INTO REHBERBILGI (YERI,YER_ID,SIRA,ETIKET,BILGI,EKLEYEN) 
			SELECT 1,@RI,(SELECT SIRA FROM REHBERAYAR WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Adres'),'Adres',@ILETISIMADRES,@EKLEYEN
		END
		UPDATE REHBERBILGI SET BILGI=@ILETISIMILCE WHERE YER_ID=@RI AND YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýlçe' 
		IF @@ROWCOUNT=0  
		BEGIN 
			INSERT INTO REHBERBILGI (YERI,YER_ID,SIRA,ETIKET,BILGI,EKLEYEN) 
			SELECT 1,@RI,(SELECT SIRA FROM REHBERAYAR WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýlçe'),'Ýlçe',@ILETISIMILCE,@EKLEYEN
		END
		UPDATE REHBERBILGI SET BILGI=@ILETISIMIL WHERE YER_ID=@RI AND YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýl' 
		IF @@ROWCOUNT=0  
		BEGIN 
			INSERT INTO REHBERBILGI (YERI,YER_ID,SIRA,ETIKET,BILGI,EKLEYEN) 
			SELECT 1,@RI,(SELECT SIRA FROM REHBERAYAR WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýl'),'Ýl',@ILETISIMIL,@EKLEYEN
		END
		UPDATE REHBERBILGI SET BILGI=@ILETISIMEPOSTA WHERE YER_ID=@RI AND YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Eposta' 
		IF @@ROWCOUNT=0  
		BEGIN 
			INSERT INTO REHBERBILGI (YERI,YER_ID,SIRA,ETIKET,BILGI,EKLEYEN) 
			SELECT 1,@RI,(SELECT SIRA FROM REHBERAYAR WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Eposta'),'Eposta',@ILETISIMEPOSTA,@EKLEYEN
		END
	 	UPDATE REHBERBILGI SET BILGI=@ILETISIMWEB WHERE YER_ID=@RI AND YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Web' 
		IF @@ROWCOUNT=0  
		BEGIN 
			INSERT INTO REHBERBILGI (YERI,YER_ID,SIRA,ETIKET,BILGI,EKLEYEN) 
			SELECT 1,@RI,(SELECT SIRA FROM REHBERAYAR WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Web'),'Web',@ILETISIMWEB,@EKLEYEN
		END	
		


		UPDATE REHBERBILGI SET BILGI=@VD WHERE YER_ID=@ID AND YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Vergi Dairesi' 
		IF @@ROWCOUNT=0  
		BEGIN 
			INSERT INTO REHBERBILGI (YERI,YER_ID,SIRA,ETIKET,BILGI,EKLEYEN) 
			SELECT 2,@ID,(SELECT SIRA FROM REHBERAYAR WHERE YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Vergi Dairesi'),'Vergi Dairesi',@VD,@EKLEYEN
		END		
		UPDATE REHBERBILGI SET BILGI=@VNO WHERE YER_ID=@ID AND YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Vergi No' 
		IF @@ROWCOUNT=0  
		BEGIN 
			INSERT INTO REHBERBILGI (YERI,YER_ID,SIRA,ETIKET,BILGI,EKLEYEN) 
			SELECT 2,@ID,(SELECT SIRA FROM REHBERAYAR WHERE YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Vergi No'),'Vergi No',@VNO,@EKLEYEN
		END				
	
		UPDATE REHBERBILGI SET BILGI=@FATBASLIK WHERE YER_ID=@ID AND YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Fatura Baþlýðý' 
		IF @@ROWCOUNT=0  
		BEGIN 
			INSERT INTO REHBERBILGI (YERI,YER_ID,SIRA,ETIKET,BILGI,EKLEYEN) 
			SELECT 2,@ID,(SELECT SIRA FROM REHBERAYAR WHERE YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Fatura Baþlýðý'),'Fatura Baþlýðý',@FATBASLIK,@EKLEYEN
		END				
		

		IF @ILGILIJSON<>''
		BEGIN 
		begin try

		IF  EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.objects WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'[dbo].[ILGILIJSON]') AND type in (N'U')) DROP TABLE [dbo].[ILGILIJSON]

		SELECT  ILGILIAD, ILGILIFIRMAID, ILGILIISTEL, ILGILICEPTEL, ILGILIEPOSTA, ILGILIGOREVI, ILGILIILETISIMI INTO ILGILIJSON FROM OPENJSON(@ILGILIJSON,'$."FDMemIlgililer"')  WITH ( ILGILIAD VARCHAR(100) '$.ILGILIAD',
																						ILGILIFIRMAID FLOAT '$.ILGILIFIRMAID',
																						ILGILIISTEL VARCHAR(30) '$.ILGILIISTEL',
																						ILGILICEPTEL VARCHAR(30) '$.ILGILICEPTEL',
																						ILGILIEPOSTA VARCHAR(100) '$.ILGILIEPOSTA',
																						ILGILIGOREVI VARCHAR(100) '$.ILGILIGOREVI',
																						ILGILIILETISIMI VARCHAR(100) '$.ILGILIILETISIMI') D
		
		UPDATE R SET R.BAGID=NULL FROM ILGILIJSON I FULL OUTER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID=I.ILGILIFIRMAID WHERE ISNULL(I.ILGILIFIRMAID,'')='' AND R.BAGID=@ID

		DECLARE
			@ILGILIFIRMAID FLOAT, 
			@ILGILIAD VARCHAR(100), 
			@ILGILIISTEL VARCHAR(30), 
			@ILGILICEPTEL VARCHAR(30), 
			@ILGILIEPOSTA VARCHAR(100), 
			@ILGILIGOREVI VARCHAR(100),
			@ILGILIILETISIMI VARCHAR(100)
			
        
		declare ilgililer cursor for
		select ILGILIFIRMAID, ILGILIAD, ILGILIISTEL, ILGILICEPTEL, ILGILIEPOSTA, ILGILIGOREVI, ILGILIILETISIMI  FROM ILGILIJSON 

		open ilgililer
		fetch next from ilgililer into @ILGILIFIRMAID, @ILGILIAD, @ILGILIISTEL, @ILGILICEPTEL, @ILGILIEPOSTA, @ILGILIGOREVI, @ILGILIILETISIMI
		while @@FETCH_STATUS =0
		BEGIN		
			EXEC [TM_Ilgililer] @ILGILIFIRMAID, @ILGILIAD, @ILGILIISTEL, @ILGILICEPTEL, @ILGILIEPOSTA, @ILGILIGOREVI, @ILGILIILETISIMI, @EKLEYEN, @DURUM, @ID
			FETCH NEXT FROM ilgililer Into @ILGILIFIRMAID, @ILGILIAD, @ILGILIISTEL, @ILGILICEPTEL, @ILGILIEPOSTA, @ILGILIGOREVI, @ILGILIILETISIMI
		end
		close ilgililer
		deallocate ilgililer 
		END TRY
		BEGIN CATCH
		end CATCH
				--IF @ILGILIFIRMAID=0 
		--BEGIN
		--		DECLARE @KODILGILI VARCHAR(20), @ILGILIFIRMAILETISIMID VARCHAR(20)
		--		SET @KODILGILI = convert(varchar(10),(select isnull(max(ID),0) + 1 from REHBER))
		--		INSERT INTO REHBER (BAGID,FIRMA,STATU,GRUP,EKLEYEN,DURUM,SUBEID) 
		--		SELECT  @ID, @ILGILIAD, 1,334,2,1,-1
		--		SET @ILGILIFIRMAID = (SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY())
		--END	
		--		INSERT INTO REHBERILETISIM (REHBERID,AD,VARSAYILAN,AKTIF,EKLEYEN,EKLEMETARIHI,SUBEID)
		--		SELECT @ILGILIFIRMAID,'Merkez',1,(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2201 AND ANAHTAR=@DURUM),@EKLEYEN,GETDATE(),'-1'
		--		SET @ILGILIFIRMAILETISIMID = (SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY())

		--		UPDATE REHBERBILGI SET BILGI=@ILGILIISTEL WHERE YER_ID=@ILGILIFIRMAILETISIMID AND YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýþ Tel' 
		--		IF @@ROWCOUNT=0  
		--		BEGIN 
		--			INSERT INTO REHBERBILGI (YERI,YER_ID,SIRA,ETIKET,BILGI,EKLEYEN) 
		--			SELECT 1,@ILGILIFIRMAILETISIMID,(SELECT SIRA FROM REHBERAYAR WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýþ Tel'),'Ýþ Tel',@ILGILIISTEL,@EKLEYEN
		--		END

		--		UPDATE REHBERBILGI SET BILGI=@ILGILICEPTEL WHERE YER_ID=@ILGILIFIRMAILETISIMID AND YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Cep Tel' 
		--		IF @@ROWCOUNT=0  
		--		BEGIN 
		--			INSERT INTO REHBERBILGI (YERI,YER_ID,SIRA,ETIKET,BILGI,EKLEYEN) 
		--			SELECT 1,@ILGILIFIRMAILETISIMID,(SELECT SIRA FROM REHBERAYAR WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Cep Tel'),'Cep Tel',@ILGILICEPTEL,@EKLEYEN
		--		END
		--		UPDATE REHBERBILGI SET BILGI=@ILGILIEPOSTA WHERE YER_ID=@ILGILIFIRMAILETISIMID AND YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Eposta' 
		--		IF @@ROWCOUNT=0  
		--		BEGIN 
		--			INSERT INTO REHBERBILGI (YERI,YER_ID,SIRA,ETIKET,BILGI,EKLEYEN) 
		--			SELECT 1,@ILGILIFIRMAILETISIMID,(SELECT SIRA FROM REHBERAYAR WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Eposta'),'Eposta',@ILGILIEPOSTA,@EKLEYEN
		--		END
	 --			UPDATE REHBERBILGI SET BILGI=@ILGILIWEB WHERE YER_ID=@ILGILIFIRMAILETISIMID AND YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Web' 
		--		IF @@ROWCOUNT=0  
		--		BEGIN 
		--			INSERT INTO REHBERBILGI (YERI,YER_ID,SIRA,ETIKET,BILGI,EKLEYEN) 
		--			SELECT 1,@ILGILIFIRMAILETISIMID,(SELECT SIRA FROM REHBERAYAR WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Web'),'Web',@ILGILIWEB,@EKLEYEN
		--		END	
			END
		 SELECT ACIKLAMA='Kayýt baþarýlý þekilde gerçekleþti'
end 
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
		SELECT ACIKLAMA='Hata oluþtu....'+ERROR_MESSAGE()
		--EXEC TM_PotansiyelListe '', '','',''
		
		--IF  EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.objects WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'[dbo].[ILGILIJSON]') AND type in (N'U')) DROP TABLE [dbo].[ILGILIJSON]
		
		--SELECT 'Hata oluþtu....'+ERROR_MESSAGE()--+' - '+ISNULL(convert(datetime,(substring(@BasTar,7,4)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,4,2)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,1,2) +' '+@BasTarT)),'')
END CATCH
end
