===== PROC: TM_PotansiyelListe =====

-- DROP INDEX IX_REHBER_SORGU_OPTIMIZASYONU ON REHBER
-- CREATE INDEX IX_REHBER_SORGU_OPTIMIZASYONU ON REHBER (FIRMA, ID, BOLGE, ALTBOLGE, KOD, BAGID, TEMSILCI, GRUP, DURUM,TEMAS, SEKTOR, SINIF, KATEGORI, STATU)
-- EXEC TM_PotansiyelListe '', '','','','1','','','',4095,'Cari'
 
CREATE PROC [dbo].[TM_PotansiyelListe](@UNVAN VARCHAR(100), @ILGILI VARCHAR(100), @DURUM VARCHAR(50), @TEMSILCIAD VARCHAR(100), @GRUP FLOAT, @TUML VARCHAR(5), @DETAYARA VARCHAR(100), @KOD VARCHAR(20)=NULL, @KULLANICIKODU VARCHAR(20)=NULL, @EKRAN VARCHAR(20)=NULL) 
AS
begin
DECLARE @ILGILIARAMA INT, @GORMEYETKISI INT, @SUBEID INT

 
SET @KOD=ISNULL(@KOD,'')

SET @ILGILIARAMA = 0
SET @GORMEYETKISI = 0
SET @SUBEID = 0

SELECT @GORMEYETKISI=BILGI, @SUBEID=r.SUBEID FROM REHBER R INNER JOIN KULLANICI K ON K.REHBERID=R.ID INNER JOIN YETKIEK YE ON YE.ROLID=K.ROLID AND R.ID=@KULLANICIKODU AND MODULID=CASE WHEN @EKRAN='Cari' THEN '2201' ELSE '2105' END

SELECT TOP 500 * FROM (
select   
	TEMSILCIAD = R2.FIRMA,
	R.FIRMA, 
	BOLGE=ISNULL(G1.ANAHTAR,''), 	
	ALTBOLGE= ISNULL(G2.ANAHTAR,''), 
	ILETISIMISTEL=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERILETISIM RI1 INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RI1.ID=RB.YER_ID WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýþ Tel' AND RI1.REHBERID=R.ID),
	ILGILI=(SELECT TOP 1 P1.FIRMA FROM REHBER P1 WHERE R.ID = P1.BAGID ORDER BY P1.STATU DESC),
	ILGILISAY=(SELECT COUNT(P1.FIRMA) FROM REHBER P1 WHERE R.ID = P1.BAGID),
	ILGILICEP=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERILETISIM RI1 INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RI1.ID=RB.YER_ID WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Cep Tel' AND RI1.REHBERID=(SELECT TOP 1 P1.ID FROM REHBER P1 WHERE R.ID = P1.BAGID ORDER BY P1.STATU DESC)),
	ILETISIMILCE=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERILETISIM RI1 INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RI1.ID=RB.YER_ID WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýlçe' AND RI1.REHBERID=R.ID),
	ILETISIMIL=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERILETISIM RI1 INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RI1.ID=RB.YER_ID WHERE YERI = 1 AND ETIKET = 'Ýl' AND RI1.REHBERID=R.ID),
	R.ID, 
	R.KOD, 		
	GRUP=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2200 AND DEGER=R.GRUP), 
	TEMAS=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2207 AND DEGER=R.TEMAS), 
	SEKTOR=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2204 AND DEGER=R.SEKTOR), 
	KATEGORI=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2208 AND DEGER=R.KATEGORI), 
	SINIF=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2203 AND DEGER=R.SINIF), 
	DURUM=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2201 AND DEGER=R.DURUM),	
	R.OZELKOD  ,		
	SONAKTIVITEKONUSU = (SELECT TOP 1 KONUSU FROM GOREVLER A WHERE A.REHBERID=R.ID ORDER BY BITISTARIHI DESC   ) ,
	SONAKTIVITETARIHI =  (SELECT TOP 1 convert(DateTime,(BITISTARIHI),103) AS BITISTARIHI FROM GOREVLER A  WHERE  A.REHBERID =R.ID  ORDER BY BITISTARIHI DESC) ,
	SONSATBELGETARIHI = (SELECT TOP 1  convert(DateTime,(FATURATARIH),103) as FATURATARIH FROM FATBASLIK F  WHERE  F.TUR  in (10,11,12,14,15,16) and F.REHBERID =R.ID  ORDER BY FATURATARIH DESC)  ,
	SONSATTUTARI = (SELECT TOP 1  FATURA_TUTARI FROM FATBASLIK F  WHERE  F.TUR  in (10,11,12,14,15,16) and F.REHBERID =R.ID  ORDER BY FATURATARIH DESC ),
	FATBASLIK=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Fatura Baþlýðý' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID)
from REHBER R 
			  LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R2 on R2.ID=R.TEMSILCI  
			  LEFT OUTER JOIN (Select R1.ID,RB.BILGI from REHBER R1 LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERILETISIM RI on R1.ID=RI.REHBERID and RI.VARSAYILAN=1 
																	LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB on RB.YER_ID=RI.ID  
																	LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERAYAR RA on RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=1 
							   Where RI.VARSAYILAN=1 and RB.YERI=1 and RA.VARSAYILAN=8) X on X.ID=R.ID   
			LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI G1 ON G1.BOLUM=-2210 and G1.DIL=-1 AND G1.DEGER=R.BOLGE
			LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI G2 ON G2.DIL=-1 and G2.DEGER = R.ALTBOLGE AND G2.BOLUM=convert(int,'-2210'+convert(varchar(10),R.BOLGE))
where 	
	R.GRUP<>334 and 
	R.GRUP<>335 and 
	R.ID > 1 and 
	R.DURUM> CASE WHEN @DURUM=1 THEN -1 ELSE 0 END and 

	ISNULL(R.TEMSILCI,'') LIKE case when @GORMEYETKISI=1 THEN @KULLANICIKODU ELSE '%' END AND
	ISNULL(R.SUBEID,-1) LIKE case when @GORMEYETKISI=10 THEN @SUBEID ELSE -1 END AND
	
	(R.GRUP = CASE WHEN @GRUP=0 THEN 1 ELSE 99999999999 end or R.GRUP > CASE WHEN @GRUP=1 THEN 1 ELSE 99999999999 end OR @TUML='1') and 	
	--ISNULL(R2.FIRMA,'') LIKE '%'+@TEMSILCIAD+'%' AND 
	(@TEMSILCIAD = '' OR ISNULL(R2.FIRMA,'') LIKE '%'+@TEMSILCIAD+'%') AND 
	R.ID LIKE CASE WHEN @KOD<>'' THEN @KOD ELSE '%' END
	AND ISNULL(G1.ANAHTAR,'') LIKE '%'+@UNVAN+'%' AND 
	ISNULL(G2.ANAHTAR,'') LIKE '%'+@ILGILI+'%'
	) D
	WHERE
	(				
				ISNULL(FIRMA,'') LIKE '%' +@DETAYARA+'%'
			    OR ISNULL(BOLGE,'') LIKE '%'+@DETAYARA+'%'
				OR ISNULL(ALTBOLGE,'') LIKE '%' +@DETAYARA+'%'
				OR ISNULL(ILGILI,'') LIKE '%' +@DETAYARA+'%'
				OR ISNULL(ILETISIMIL,'') LIKE '%' +@DETAYARA+'%'
				OR ISNULL(ILETISIMILCE,'') LIKE '%' +@DETAYARA+'%'
				OR ISNULL(KOD,'') LIKE '%' +@DETAYARA+'%'
				OR ISNULL(FATBASLIK,'') LIKE '%' +@DETAYARA+'%'
				OR ISNULL(SONAKTIVITEKONUSU,'') LIKE '%' +@DETAYARA+'%'
	)
order by 1

end


===== PROC: TM_Rehber =====

--SELECT * FROM GOREVYORUM WHERE ID>14153

--SELECT * FROM STOKFIYAT
--SELECT * FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Fiyat Liste Adý' AND  RB.YER_ID=R.ID



--  exec [TM_Rehber] '120.01.041','X',''
--	exec [TM_Rehber] 'A','A','335'


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_Rehber](@KOD varchar(20),@FIRMA VARCHAR(50), @TIP VARCHAR(50), @KULLANICIKODU VARCHAR(20)=NULL)
AS
BEGIN 
IF @TIP='335'
BEGIN
		SELECT top 50 
				KOD, 
				FIRMA, 
				R.ID,
				VADESI=(SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Vadesi' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID),
				FIYATLISTESI=ISNULL((SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Fiyat Liste Adý' AND  RB.YER_ID=R.ID),'Satýþ'),
				FIYATLISTESIALIS='Alýþ',
				UYARINOTYASAK=ISNULL((SELECT STRING_AGG(CASE WHEN TUR=11 THEN 'Not' when TUR=12 THEN 'Uyarý' when TUR=13 THEN 'Yasak' end +' / '+ CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),G.EKLEMETARIHI,103) +' / '+ ISNULL(R1.FIRMA,'') +CHAR(13)+CHAR(10)+G.YORUM, +CHAR(13)+CHAR(10)+CHAR(13)+CHAR(10)) FROM GOREVYORUM G LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R1 ON G.EKLEYEN=R1.ID WHERE R.ID=G.GOREVID AND TUR IN (11,12,13)),'-'),
				FATBASLIK=ISNULL(RB.BILGI,'-'),
				REHBERILETISIMID=(SELECT TOP 1 ID FROM REHBERILETISIM Firma WHERE R.ID=Firma.REHBERID and Firma.VARSAYILAN=1 and Firma.AKTIF=1),
				TEMSILCI=(SELECT TOP 1 FIRMA FROM REHBER RT WHERE RT.ID=R.TEMSILCI)
		FROM REHBER R LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Fatura Baþlýðý' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID
		WHERE (R.KOD LIKE '%'+@KOD+'%' or R.FIRMA LIKE '%'+@FIRMA+'%' OR ISNULL(RB.BILGI,'') LIKE '%'+@FIRMA+'%') --AND KOD LIKE @TIP+'%'
		AND ISNULL(DURUM,1)<>0
		AND R.GRUP<>334 
		AND R.GRUP<>335 
		ORDER BY 1,2
END
IF @TIP='YETKIKISITLI'
BEGIN
DECLARE @ILGILIARAMA INT, @GORMEYETKISI INT, @SUBEID INT

 
SET @ILGILIARAMA = 0
SET @GORMEYETKISI = 0
SET @SUBEID = 0
SELECT 
		@GORMEYETKISI=CASE WHEN ISNULL(RO.TY,0)=0 THEN BILGI ELSE '100' end, 
		@SUBEID=r.SUBEID 
FROM REHBER R INNER JOIN KULLANICI K ON K.REHBERID=R.ID INNER JOIN YETKIEK YE ON YE.ROLID=K.ROLID AND R.ID=@KULLANICIKODU AND MODULID= '2201'
															LEFT OUTER JOIN ROLLER RO ON RO.ID=K.ROLID

--SELECT @GORMEYETKISI=BILGI, @SUBEID=r.SUBEID FROM REHBER R INNER JOIN KULLANICI K ON K.REHBERID=R.ID INNER JOIN YETKIEK YE ON YE.ROLID=K.ROLID AND R.ID=@KULLANICIKODU AND MODULID= '2201'

		SELECT top 50 
				KOD, 
				FIRMA, 
				R.ID,
				VADESI=(SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Vadesi' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID),
				FIYATLISTESI=ISNULL((SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Fiyat Liste Adý' AND  RB.YER_ID=R.ID),'Satýþ'),
				FIYATLISTESIALIS='Alýþ',
				UYARINOTYASAK=ISNULL((SELECT STRING_AGG(CASE WHEN TUR=11 THEN 'Not' when TUR=12 THEN 'Uyarý' when TUR=13 THEN 'Yasak' end +' / '+ CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),G.EKLEMETARIHI,103) +' / '+ ISNULL(R1.FIRMA,'') +CHAR(13)+CHAR(10)+G.YORUM, +CHAR(13)+CHAR(10)+CHAR(13)+CHAR(10)) FROM GOREVYORUM G LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R1 ON G.EKLEYEN=R1.ID WHERE R.ID=G.GOREVID AND TUR IN (11,12,13)),'-'),
				FATBASLIK=ISNULL(RB.BILGI,'-'),
				REHBERILETISIMID=(SELECT TOP 1 ID FROM REHBERILETISIM Firma WHERE R.ID=Firma.REHBERID and Firma.VARSAYILAN=1 and Firma.AKTIF=1),
				TEMSILCI=(SELECT TOP 1 FIRMA FROM REHBER RT WHERE RT.ID=R.TEMSILCI)
		FROM REHBER R LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Fatura Baþlýðý' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID
		WHERE (R.KOD LIKE '%'+@KOD+'%' or R.FIRMA LIKE '%'+@FIRMA+'%' OR ISNULL(RB.BILGI,'') LIKE '%'+@FIRMA+'%') --AND KOD LIKE @TIP+'%'
		AND ISNULL(DURUM,1)<>0
		AND R.GRUP<>334 
		AND R.GRUP<>335 
		and ISNULL(R.TEMSILCI,'') LIKE case when @GORMEYETKISI=1 THEN @KULLANICIKODU ELSE '%' END AND
	ISNULL(R.SUBEID,-1) LIKE case when @GORMEYETKISI=10 THEN @SUBEID ELSE -1 END
		ORDER BY 1,2
END
IF @TIP='SATICI'
BEGIN
		SELECT top 50 
				R.KOD, 
				FIRMA, 
				R.ID,
				VADESI=(SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Vadesi' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID),
				FIYATLISTESI=ISNULL((SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Fiyat Liste Adý' AND  RB.YER_ID=R.ID),'Satýþ'),
				FIYATLISTESIALIS='Alýþ',
				UYARINOTYASAK=ISNULL((SELECT STRING_AGG(G.YORUM, ';') FROM GOREVYORUM G WHERE R.ID=G.GOREVID AND TUR IN (11,12,13)),'-'),
				FATBASLIK=ISNULL(RB.BILGI,''),
				REHBERILETISIMID=(SELECT TOP 1 ID FROM REHBERILETISIM Firma WHERE R.ID=Firma.REHBERID and Firma.VARSAYILAN=1 and Firma.AKTIF=1),
				TEMSILCI=(SELECT TOP 1 FIRMA FROM REHBER RT WHERE RT.ID=R.TEMSILCI)
		FROM KULLANICI KL INNER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID=KL.REHBERID
							 LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Fatura Baþlýðý' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID
		WHERE (R.KOD LIKE '%'+@KOD+'%' or R.FIRMA LIKE '%'+@FIRMA+'%' OR ISNULL(RB.BILGI,'') LIKE '%'+@FIRMA+'%') AND ISNULL(R.DURUM,1)<>0
		ORDER BY 1,2
END
IF @TIP='BAGLI'
BEGIN
		SELECT top 50 
				R.KOD, 
				FIRMA, 
				R.ID,
				VADESI=ISNULL((SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Vadesi' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID),''),
				FIYATLISTESI=ISNULL((SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Fiyat Liste Adý' AND  RB.YER_ID=R.ID),'Satýþ'),
				FIYATLISTESIALIS='Alýþ',
				UYARINOTYASAK=ISNULL((SELECT STRING_AGG(G.YORUM, ';') FROM GOREVYORUM G WHERE R.ID=G.GOREVID AND TUR IN (11,12,13)),'-'),
				FATBASLIK=ISNULL(RB.BILGI,''),
				REHBERILETISIMID=(SELECT TOP 1 ID FROM REHBERILETISIM Firma WHERE R.ID=Firma.REHBERID and Firma.VARSAYILAN=1 and Firma.AKTIF=1),
				TEMSILCI=(SELECT TOP 1 FIRMA FROM REHBER RT WHERE RT.ID=R.TEMSILCI)
		FROM REHBER R LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Fatura Baþlýðý' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID
		WHERE R.BAGID=@KOD AND ISNULL(DURUM,1)<>0
		ORDER BY 1,2
END

IF @TIP='RAKIP'
BEGIN
		SELECT top 50
				KOD, 
				FIRMA, 
				R.ID,
				VADESI=(SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Vadesi' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID),
				FIYATLISTESI=ISNULL((SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Fiyat Liste Adý' AND  RB.YER_ID=R.ID),'Satýþ'),
				FIYATLISTESIALIS='Alýþ',
				UYARINOTYASAK=ISNULL((SELECT STRING_AGG(G.YORUM, ';') FROM GOREVYORUM G WHERE R.ID=G.GOREVID AND TUR IN (11,12,13)),'-'),
				FATBASLIK=ISNULL(RB.BILGI,''),
				REHBERILETISIMID=(SELECT TOP 1 ID FROM REHBERILETISIM Firma WHERE R.ID=Firma.REHBERID and Firma.VARSAYILAN=1 and Firma.AKTIF=1),
				TEMSILCI=(SELECT TOP 1 FIRMA FROM REHBER RT WHERE RT.ID=R.TEMSILCI)
		FROM REHBER R LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Fatura Baþlýðý' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID
		WHERE (R.KOD LIKE '%'+@KOD+'%' or R.FIRMA LIKE '%'+@FIRMA+'%'  OR ISNULL(RB.BILGI,'') LIKE '%'+@FIRMA+'%') AND GRUP = 950 AND ISNULL(DURUM,1)<>0
		ORDER BY 1,2
END
IF @TIP='BAYI'
BEGIN
		SELECT top 50 
				KOD, 
				FIRMA, 
				R.ID,
				VADESI=(SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Vadesi' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID),
				FIYATLISTESI=ISNULL((SELECT BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Fiyat Liste Adý' AND  RB.YER_ID=R.ID),'Satýþ'),
				FIYATLISTESIALIS='Alýþ',
				UYARINOTYASAK=ISNULL((SELECT STRING_AGG(CASE WHEN TUR=11 THEN 'Not' when TUR=12 THEN 'Uyarý' when TUR=13 THEN 'Yasak' end +' / '+ CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),G.EKLEMETARIHI,103) +' / '+ ISNULL(R1.FIRMA,'') +CHAR(13)+CHAR(10)+G.YORUM, +CHAR(13)+CHAR(10)+CHAR(13)+CHAR(10)) FROM GOREVYORUM G LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R1 ON G.EKLEYEN=R1.ID WHERE R.ID=G.GOREVID AND TUR IN (11,12,13)),'-'),
				FATBASLIK=ISNULL(RB.BILGI,'-'),
				REHBERILETISIMID=(SELECT TOP 1 ID FROM REHBERILETISIM Firma WHERE R.ID=Firma.REHBERID and Firma.VARSAYILAN=1 and Firma.AKTIF=1),
				TEMSILCI=(SELECT TOP 1 FIRMA FROM REHBER RT WHERE RT.ID=R.TEMSILCI)
		FROM REHBER R LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Fatura Baþlýðý' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID
		WHERE R.ID=@KOD
		AND ISNULL(DURUM,1)<>0
		AND R.GRUP<>334 
		AND R.GRUP<>335 
		ORDER BY 1,2
END
end



===== PROC: TM_RehberIletisim =====

--SELECT * FROM GOREVYORUM WHERE ID>14153

--SELECT * FROM STOKFIYAT
--SELECT * FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE RB.ETIKET='Fiyat Liste Adý' AND  RB.YER_ID=R.ID



--  exec [TM_Rehber] '120.01.041','X',''
--	exec [TM_Rehber] 'A','A','335'


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_RehberIletisim](@REHBERID INTEGER)
AS
BEGIN 
select 
	ID,
	AD, 
	ADRES=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=RI.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=2), 
	ILCE=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=RI.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=6), 
	IL=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=RI.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=8), 
	ID_VERGIDAI=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=RI.ID AND RB.YERI=2 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=20), 
	ID_VERGINO=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=RI.ID AND RB.YERI=2 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=22) 
FROM REHBERILETISIM RI where REHBERID=@REHBERID 

end



===== PROC: TM_SAP_ENG_KDRSonucDetay =====



  --exec [TM_SAP_ENG_KDRSonucDetay] 7

CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_SAP_ENG_KDRSonucDetay](@TIP varchar(2))
AS
BEGIN 	
	DECLARE @SQL VARCHAR(8000)

	IF @TIP='1' 
	BEGIN
			--Borç Detay
						SELECT 
				[CardCode]=			T0.[CardCode], 
				[CardName]=			T0.[CardName],
				[Balance]=			CASE WHEN T0.[Currency] = '##' AND SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
								 WHEN T0.[Currency] = 'TRY' THEN SUM(T2.[Debit]-T2.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) END  ,
				[Currency]=			T0.[Currency],
				[Balance (Local Currency)]=			SUM(T2.[Debit]-T2.[Credit])    
						FROM  
							[dbo].[OCRD] T0 LEFT OUTER  JOIN [dbo].[OCRG] T1  ON  T1.[GroupCode] = T0.[GroupCode]    
											LEFT OUTER JOIN [dbo].[JDT1] T2  ON  T2.[ShortName] = T0.[CardCode]    
											LEFT OUTER JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T3  ON  T3.[TransId] = T2.[TransId]  
						WHERE 
							T0.[CardType] <> 'L' 
							AND  T2.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate) 
							AND  T2.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
							AND  T2.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
							AND  (T2.[SourceLine] <> ('-8')  OR  T2.[SourceLine] IS NULL  )  
						GROUP BY 
							T0.[CardCode], T0.[CardName], T0.[CardType], T0.[GroupCode], T0.[Currency], T1.[GroupName]
						HAVING
							SUM(T2.Debit-T2.Credit) > 0
						ORDER BY
							T0.[CardCode]
	END
	IF @TIP='2' 
	BEGIN
							--Banka Detay
							SELECT 
						[CardCode]=		T0.[Account],
						[CardName]=		T2.[AcctName],
						[Balance]=		CASE WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
									 WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY'  THEN  SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END,
						[Currency]=		T2.[ActCurr],
						[Balance (Local Currency)]=		SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
							FROM  
								[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
												INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
												INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
												INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
							WHERE 
								T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate) 
								AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
								AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
								AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
								AND	T4.[Descr] = 'BANKA'
							GROUP BY 
								T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
							HAVING
								SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='3' 
	BEGIN
											--Pos
								SELECT 
							[CardCode]=		T0.[Account],
							[CardName]=		T2.[AcctName],
							[Balance]=		CASE  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
										  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY'  THEN  SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
							[Currency]=		T2.[ActCurr],
							[Balance (Local Currency)]=		SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate) 
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'POS'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='4' 
	BEGIN
								--Alýnan Çek Detay
								SELECT 
							[CardCode]=		T0.[Account],
							[CardName]=		T2.[AcctName],
							[Balance]=		CASE WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
										 WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY'  THEN  SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
							[Currency]=		T2.[ActCurr],
							[Balance (Local Currency)]=		SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'ALINAN ÇEK'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='5' 
	BEGIN
								--Alacak Senet Detay
								SELECT 
						[CardCode]=			T0.[Account],
						[CardName]=			T2.[AcctName],
						[Balance]=			CASE  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
											WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY' THEN SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
						[Currency]=			T2.[ActCurr],
						[Balance (Local Currency)]=			SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)  
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'ALINAN SENET'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='6' 
	BEGIN
								--Kasa Detay
								SELECT 
							[CardCode]=		T0.[Account],
							[CardName]=		T2.[AcctName],
							[Balance]=		CASE  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
											WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY' THEN SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
							[Currency]=		T2.[ActCurr],
							[Balance (Local Currency)]=		SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)  
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'KASA'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='7' 
	BEGIN	--STOK
	  --exec [TM_SAP_KDRSonucDetay] 7
								SELECT
									[CardCode]=NM.ItemCode,
									[CardName]=TM.ItemName+' Warehouse '+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),NM.Warehouse)+' Quantity ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(110),SUM(ISNULL(CASE WHEN NM.InQty> 0 THEN NM.InQty ELSE -1*NM.OutQty END,0))) ,	
									[Balance]=SUM(CAST(TransValue AS decimal(15,2))) ,
									[Currency]='TRY' ,
									[Balance (Local Currency)]=SUM(CAST(TransValue AS decimal(15,2))) 
								FROM 
									OINM NM 
											INNER JOIN OITM TM ON TM.ItemCode = NM.ItemCode
											INNER JOIN OUGP GP ON GP.UgpEntry = TM.UgpEntry
											LEFT JOIN OITB TB ON TB.ItmsGrpCod = TM.ItmsGrpCod
								GROUP BY
									NM.ItemCode,
									TM.ItemName,
									NM.Warehouse
	END
	IF @TIP='8' 
	BEGIN
								--Alacak Detay
								SELECT 
						[CardCode]=			T0.[CardCode], 
						[CardName]=			T0.[CardName],
						[Balance]=			CASE  WHEN T0.[Currency] = '##' AND SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
											WHEN T0.[Currency] = 'TRY' THEN SUM(T2.[Debit]-T2.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) END ,
						[Currency]=			T0.[Currency],
						[Balance (Local Currency)]=			SUM(T2.[Debit]-T2.[Credit])     
								FROM  
									[dbo].[OCRD] T0 LEFT OUTER  JOIN [dbo].[OCRG] T1  ON  T1.[GroupCode] = T0.[GroupCode]    
													LEFT OUTER  JOIN [dbo].[JDT1] T2  ON  T2.[ShortName] = T0.[CardCode]    
													LEFT OUTER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T3  ON  T3.[TransId] = T2.[TransId]  
								WHERE 
									T0.[CardType] <> 'L' 
									AND  T2.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)
									AND  T2.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T2.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T2.[SourceLine] <> ('-8')  OR  T2.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[CardCode], T0.[CardName], T0.[CardType], T0.[GroupCode], T0.[Currency], T1.[GroupName] 
								HAVING
									SUM(T2.Debit-T2.Credit) < 0
								ORDER BY
									T0.[CardCode]
	END
	IF @TIP='9' 
	BEGIN
								--Kredi
								SELECT 
						[CardCode]=			T0.[Account],
						[CardName]=			T2.[AcctName],
						[Balance]=			CASE  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
											WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY'  THEN  SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
						[Currency]=			T2.[ActCurr],
						[Balance (Local Currency)]=			SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate) 
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'KREDÝLER'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='10' 
	BEGIN
--Kredi Kartý
								SELECT 
							[CardCode]=		T0.[Account],
							[CardName]=		T2.[AcctName],
							[Balance]=		CASE  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
											WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY'  THEN  SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
							[Currency]=		T2.[ActCurr],
							[Balance (Local Currency)]=		SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'KREDÝ KARTI'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='11' 
	BEGIN
								--Verilen Çek Detay
								SELECT 
						[CardCode]=			T0.[Account],
						[CardName]=			T2.[AcctName],
						[Balance]=			CASE  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
											WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY'  THEN  SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
						[Currency]=			T2.[ActCurr],
						[Balance (Local Currency)]=			SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'VERÝLEN ÇEK'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='12' 
	BEGIN
								--Verilen Senet Detay
								SELECT 
						[CardCode]=			T0.[Account],
						[CardName]=			T2.[AcctName],
						[Balance]=			CASE  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
											WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY' THEN SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
						[Currency]=			T2.[ActCurr],
						[Balance (Local Currency)]=			SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'VERÝLEN SENET'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='13' 
	BEGIN
								--Borç Detay
								SELECT 
							[CardCode]=		T0.[CardCode], 
							[CardName]=		T0.[CardName],
							[Balance]=		CASE  WHEN T0.[Currency] = '##' AND SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
										  WHEN T0.[Currency] = 'TRY' THEN SUM(T2.[Debit]-T2.[Credit])  ELSE SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) END ,
							[Currency]=		T0.[Currency],
							[Balance (Local Currency)]=		SUM(T2.[Debit]-T2.[Credit])   
								FROM  
									[dbo].[OCRD] T0 LEFT OUTER  JOIN [dbo].[OCRG] T1  ON  T1.[GroupCode] = T0.[GroupCode]    
													LEFT OUTER JOIN [dbo].[JDT1] T2  ON  T2.[ShortName] = T0.[CardCode]    
													LEFT OUTER JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T3  ON  T3.[TransId] = T2.[TransId]  
								WHERE 
									T0.[CardType] <> 'L' 
									AND  T2.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate) 
									AND  T2.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T2.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T2.[SourceLine] <> ('-8')  OR  T2.[SourceLine] IS NULL  )  
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[CardCode], T0.[CardName], T0.[CardType], T0.[GroupCode], T0.[Currency], T1.[GroupName]
								HAVING
									SUM(T2.Debit-T2.Credit) > 0
								ORDER BY
									T0.[CardCode]
	END


end


===== PROC: TM_SAP_KDRSonuc =====

--EXEC TM_SAP_KDRSonuc												


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_SAP_KDRSonuc]
AS
BEGIN 

select 
[ALACAKLILAR] KDR_ALACAKLILAR,
[ALINAN ÇEK] KDR_ALINAN_CEK,
[ALINAN SENET] KDR_ALINAN_SENET,
[BANKA] KDR_BANKA,
[BORÇLULAR] KDR_BORCLULAR,
[KASA] KDR_KASA,
[KREDÝ KARTI] KDR_KREDI_KARTI,
[KREDÝLER] KDR_KREDILER,
[POS] KDR_POS,
[STOK] KDR_STOK,
[VERÝLEN ÇEK] KDR_VERILEN_CEK,
[VERÝLEN SENET] KDR_VERILEN_SENET,
[SONUÇ] SONUC
FROM (
select * from (
SELECT
	ISNULL(Descr,'SONUÇ') AS Descr,
	SUM(Balance) AS [Balance]
FROM (
SELECT
	ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY Descr) AS IndexID,
	Descr,
	SUM([Balance (Local Currency)]) AS Balance
FROM (
	SELECT 
		'BORÇLULAR' AS Descr,
		T0.[CardCode], 
		T0.[CardName],
		CASE 
			WHEN T0.[Currency] = '##' AND SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN '****'
			WHEN T0.[Currency] = '##' AND SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN NULL
			ELSE CONVERT(varchar(100),SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit])) 
		END AS [Balance],
		T0.[Currency],
		SUM(T2.[Debit]-T2.[Credit]) AS [Balance (Local Currency)]  
	FROM  
		[dbo].[OCRD] T0 LEFT OUTER  JOIN [dbo].[OCRG] T1  ON  T1.[GroupCode] = T0.[GroupCode]    
						LEFT OUTER JOIN [dbo].[JDT1] T2  ON  T2.[ShortName] = T0.[CardCode]    
						LEFT OUTER JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T3  ON  T3.[TransId] = T2.[TransId]  
	WHERE 
		T0.[CardType] <> 'L' 
		AND  T2.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)   
		AND  T2.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
		AND  T2.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
		AND  (T2.[SourceLine] <> ('-8')  OR  T2.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
	GROUP BY 
		T0.[CardCode], T0.[CardName], T0.[CardType], T0.[GroupCode], T0.[Currency], T1.[GroupName]
	HAVING
		SUM(T2.Debit-T2.Credit) > 0
	UNION ALL
	--Alacak Detay
	SELECT 
		'ALACAKLILAR' AS Descr,
		T0.[CardCode], 
		T0.[CardName],
		CASE 
			WHEN T0.[Currency] = '##' AND SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN '****'
			WHEN T0.[Currency] = '##' AND SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN NULL
			ELSE CONVERT(varchar(100),SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit])) 
		END AS [Balance],
		T0.[Currency],
		SUM(T2.[Debit]-T2.[Credit]) AS [Balance (Local Currency)]    
	FROM  
		[dbo].[OCRD] T0 LEFT OUTER  JOIN [dbo].[OCRG] T1  ON  T1.[GroupCode] = T0.[GroupCode]    
						LEFT OUTER  JOIN [dbo].[JDT1] T2  ON  T2.[ShortName] = T0.[CardCode]    
						LEFT OUTER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T3  ON  T3.[TransId] = T2.[TransId]  
	WHERE 
		T0.[CardType] <> 'L' 
		AND  T2.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)   
		AND  T2.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
		AND  T2.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
		AND  (T2.[SourceLine] <> ('-8')  OR  T2.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
	GROUP BY 
		T0.[CardCode], T0.[CardName], T0.[CardType], T0.[GroupCode], T0.[Currency], T1.[GroupName] 
	HAVING
		SUM(T2.Debit-T2.Credit) < 0
	UNION ALL
	--STOK
	SELECT
		'STOK' AS Descr,
		NM.ItemCode,
		TM.ItemName,
		SUM(CAST(TransValue AS decimal(15,2))) AS [Balance],
		'TRY' AS [Currency],
		SUM(CAST(TransValue AS decimal(15,2))) AS [Balance (Local Currency)]
	FROM 
		OINM NM 
				INNER JOIN OITM TM ON TM.ItemCode = NM.ItemCode
				INNER JOIN OUGP GP ON GP.UgpEntry = TM.UgpEntry
				LEFT JOIN OITB TB ON TB.ItmsGrpCod = TM.ItmsGrpCod
	GROUP BY
		NM.ItemCode,
		TM.ItemName,
		NM.Warehouse
) AS X
GROUP BY
	Descr
UNION ALL
SELECT
	TX.IndexID+2,
	TX.Descr,
	ISNULL(X.Balance,0) AS [Balance]
FROM [dbo].[UFD1] TX LEFT JOIN (
	SELECT
		T4.Descr,
		SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) AS [Balance]
	FROM  
		[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
						INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
						INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
						INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
	WHERE
		T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)   
		AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
		AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
		AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)
	GROUP BY 
		T4.Descr
) AS X ON X.Descr = TX.Descr
WHERE
	TX.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR'
) AS CSD
GROUP BY 
	CUBE(Descr)) d

PIVOT(SUM(Balance) FOR Descr IN (
[ALACAKLILAR],
[ALINAN ÇEK],
[ALINAN SENET],
[BANKA],
[BORÇLULAR],
[KASA],
[KREDÝ KARTI],
[KREDÝLER],
[POS],
[STOK],
[VERÝLEN ÇEK],
[VERÝLEN SENET],
[SONUÇ])) AS pvt) DDD
end




===== PROC: TM_SAP_KDRSonucDetay =====









  --exec [TM_SAP_KDRSonucDetay] 1


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_SAP_KDRSonucDetay](@TIP varchar(2))
AS
BEGIN 	
	DECLARE @SQL VARCHAR(8000)

	IF @TIP='1' 
	BEGIN
			--Borç Detay
						SELECT 
				KOD=			T0.[CardCode], 
				FIRMA=			T0.[CardName],
				BAKIYE=			CASE WHEN T0.[Currency] = '##' AND SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
								 WHEN T0.[Currency] = 'TRY' THEN SUM(T2.[Debit]-T2.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) END  ,
				KUR=			T0.[Currency],
				TLKARSILIK=			SUM(T2.[Debit]-T2.[Credit])    
						FROM  
							[dbo].[OCRD] T0 LEFT OUTER  JOIN [dbo].[OCRG] T1  ON  T1.[GroupCode] = T0.[GroupCode]    
											LEFT OUTER JOIN [dbo].[JDT1] T2  ON  T2.[ShortName] = T0.[CardCode]    
											LEFT OUTER JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T3  ON  T3.[TransId] = T2.[TransId]  
						WHERE 
							T0.[CardType] <> 'L' 
							AND  T2.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate) 
							AND  T2.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
							AND  T2.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
							AND  (T2.[SourceLine] <> ('-8')  OR  T2.[SourceLine] IS NULL  )  
						GROUP BY 
							T0.[CardCode], T0.[CardName], T0.[CardType], T0.[GroupCode], T0.[Currency], T1.[GroupName]
						HAVING
							SUM(T2.Debit-T2.Credit) > 0
						ORDER BY
							T0.[CardCode]
	END
	IF @TIP='2' 
	BEGIN
							--Banka Detay
							SELECT 
						KOD=		T0.[Account],
						FIRMA=		T2.[AcctName],
						BAKIYE=		CASE WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
									 WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY'  THEN  SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END,
						KUR=		T2.[ActCurr],
						TLKARSILIK=		SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
							FROM  
								[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
												INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
												INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
												INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
							WHERE 
								T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate) 
								AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
								AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
								AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
								AND	T4.[Descr] = 'BANKA'
							GROUP BY 
								T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
							HAVING
								SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='3' 
	BEGIN
											--Pos
								SELECT 
							KOD=		T0.[Account],
							FIRMA=		T2.[AcctName],
							BAKIYE=		CASE  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
										  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY'  THEN  SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
							KUR=		T2.[ActCurr],
							TLKARSILIK=		SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate) 
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'POS'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='4' 
	BEGIN
								--Alýnan Çek Detay
								SELECT 
							KOD=		T0.[Account],
							FIRMA=		T2.[AcctName],
							BAKIYE=		CASE WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
										 WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY'  THEN  SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
							KUR=		T2.[ActCurr],
							TLKARSILIK=		SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'ALINAN ÇEK'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='5' 
	BEGIN
								--Alacak Senet Detay
								SELECT 
						KOD=			T0.[Account],
						FIRMA=			T2.[AcctName],
						BAKIYE=			CASE  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
											WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY' THEN SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
						KUR=			T2.[ActCurr],
						TLKARSILIK=			SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)  
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'ALINAN SENET'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='6' 
	BEGIN
								--Kasa Detay
								SELECT 
							KOD=		T0.[Account],
							FIRMA=		T2.[AcctName],
							BAKIYE=		CASE  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
											WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY' THEN SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
							KUR=		T2.[ActCurr],
							TLKARSILIK=		SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)  
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'KASA'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='7' 
	BEGIN	--STOK
	  --exec [TM_SAP_KDRSonucDetay] 7
								SELECT
									KOD=NM.ItemCode,
									FIRMA=TM.ItemName+' Warehouse '+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),NM.Warehouse)+' Quantity ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(110),SUM(ISNULL(CASE WHEN NM.InQty> 0 THEN NM.InQty ELSE -1*NM.OutQty END,0))) ,	
									BAKIYE=SUM(CAST(TransValue AS decimal(15,2))) ,
									KUR='TRY' ,
									TLKARSILIK=SUM(CAST(TransValue AS decimal(15,2))) 
								FROM 
									OINM NM 
											INNER JOIN OITM TM ON TM.ItemCode = NM.ItemCode
											INNER JOIN OUGP GP ON GP.UgpEntry = TM.UgpEntry
											LEFT JOIN OITB TB ON TB.ItmsGrpCod = TM.ItmsGrpCod
								GROUP BY
									NM.ItemCode,
									TM.ItemName,
									NM.Warehouse
	END
	IF @TIP='8' 
	BEGIN
								--Alacak Detay
								SELECT 
						KOD=			T0.[CardCode], 
						FIRMA=			T0.[CardName],
						BAKIYE=			CASE  WHEN T0.[Currency] = '##' AND SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
											WHEN T0.[Currency] = 'TRY' THEN SUM(T2.[Debit]-T2.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) END ,
						KUR=			T0.[Currency],
						TLKARSILIK=			SUM(T2.[Debit]-T2.[Credit])     
								FROM  
									[dbo].[OCRD] T0 LEFT OUTER  JOIN [dbo].[OCRG] T1  ON  T1.[GroupCode] = T0.[GroupCode]    
													LEFT OUTER  JOIN [dbo].[JDT1] T2  ON  T2.[ShortName] = T0.[CardCode]    
													LEFT OUTER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T3  ON  T3.[TransId] = T2.[TransId]  
								WHERE 
									T0.[CardType] <> 'L' 
									AND  T2.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)
									AND  T2.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T2.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T2.[SourceLine] <> ('-8')  OR  T2.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[CardCode], T0.[CardName], T0.[CardType], T0.[GroupCode], T0.[Currency], T1.[GroupName] 
								HAVING
									SUM(T2.Debit-T2.Credit) < 0
								ORDER BY
									T0.[CardCode]
	END
	IF @TIP='9' 
	BEGIN
								--Kredi
								SELECT 
						KOD=			T0.[Account],
						FIRMA=			T2.[AcctName],
						BAKIYE=			CASE  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
											WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY'  THEN  SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
						KUR=			T2.[ActCurr],
						TLKARSILIK=			SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate) 
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'KREDÝLER'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='10' 
	BEGIN
--Kredi Kartý
								SELECT 
							KOD=		T0.[Account],
							FIRMA=		T2.[AcctName],
							BAKIYE=		CASE  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
											WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY'  THEN  SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
							KUR=		T2.[ActCurr],
							TLKARSILIK=		SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'KREDÝ KARTI'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='11' 
	BEGIN
								--Verilen Çek Detay
								SELECT 
						KOD=			T0.[Account],
						FIRMA=			T2.[AcctName],
						BAKIYE=			CASE  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
											WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY'  THEN  SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
						KUR=			T2.[ActCurr],
						TLKARSILIK=			SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'VERÝLEN ÇEK'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='12' 
	BEGIN
								--Verilen Senet Detay
								SELECT 
						KOD=			T0.[Account],
						FIRMA=			T2.[AcctName],
						BAKIYE=			CASE  WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = '##' AND SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
											WHEN T2.[ActCurr] = 'TRY' THEN SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) ELSE SUM(T0.[FCDebit]-T0.[FCCredit]) END ,
						KUR=			T2.[ActCurr],
						TLKARSILIK=			SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) 
								FROM  
									[dbo].[JDT1] T0 INNER  JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T1  ON  T1.[TransId] = T0.[TransId]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[OACT] T2  ON  T2.[AcctCode] = T0.[Account]
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[@GRNT_KDR] T3 ON T2.AcctCode LIKE T3.U_GRNT_HESAP_KODU +'%'
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[UFD1] T4 ON T4.TableID = '@GRNT_KDR' AND T4.FldValue = T3.U_GRNT_Grup
								WHERE 
									T0.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate)
									AND  T0.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T0.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T0.[SourceLine] <> (-8)  OR  T0.[SourceLine] IS NULL)  
									AND	T4.[Descr] = 'VERÝLEN SENET'
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[Account], T2.[AcctName], T2.[ActCurr]
								HAVING
									SUM(T0.[Debit]-T0.[Credit]) <> 0
	END
	IF @TIP='13' 
	BEGIN
								--Borç Detay
								SELECT 
							KOD=		T0.[CardCode], 
							FIRMA=		T0.[CardName],
							BAKIYE=		CASE  WHEN T0.[Currency] = '##' AND SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) <> 0 THEN 0
										  WHEN T0.[Currency] = 'TRY' THEN SUM(T2.[Debit]-T2.[Credit])  ELSE SUM(T2.[FCDebit]-T2.[FCCredit]) END ,
							KUR=		T0.[Currency],
							TLKARSILIK=		SUM(T2.[Debit]-T2.[Credit])   
								FROM  
									[dbo].[OCRD] T0 LEFT OUTER  JOIN [dbo].[OCRG] T1  ON  T1.[GroupCode] = T0.[GroupCode]    
													LEFT OUTER JOIN [dbo].[JDT1] T2  ON  T2.[ShortName] = T0.[CardCode]    
													LEFT OUTER JOIN [dbo].[OJDT] T3  ON  T3.[TransId] = T2.[TransId]  
								WHERE 
									T0.[CardType] <> 'L' 
									AND  T2.[RefDate] >= (SELECT TOP 1 F_RefDate FROM OFPR ORDER BY F_RefDate) 
									AND  T2.[RefDate] <= (GETDATE())  
									AND  T2.[TransType] <> ('-3')  
									AND  (T2.[SourceLine] <> ('-8')  OR  T2.[SourceLine] IS NULL  )  
								GROUP BY 
									T0.[CardCode], T0.[CardName], T0.[CardType], T0.[GroupCode], T0.[Currency], T1.[GroupName]
								HAVING
									SUM(T2.Debit-T2.Credit) > 0
								ORDER BY
									T0.[CardCode]
	END


end


===== PROC: TM_SatisFirsatiPerformansRaporu =====


-- exec TM_SatisFirsatiPerformansRaporu '2023-01-01','2023-12-31'


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_SatisFirsatiPerformansRaporu](@BASTAR DATETIME, @BITTAR DATETIME)
AS
BEGIN 
DECLARE 
	@ALAN VARCHAR(MAX) = '',	
	@ALAN2 VARCHAR(MAX) = '',
	@ALAN3 VARCHAR(MAX) = '',
	@SQL VARCHAR(8000),
	@ID INT = 1,
	@MAXID INT	

SET @BASTAR = @BASTAR+' 00:00:00'
SET @BITTAR = @BITTAR+' 23:59:59'

SELECT * INTO #TEMP_ASAMA FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2114
SET @MAXID = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM #TEMP_ASAMA)

WHILE @ID<=@MAXID
BEGIN
	SET @ALAN = @ALAN +
						(SELECT
							'['+ANAHTAR+']'
						FROM
							#TEMP_ASAMA
						WHERE
							SIRA = @ID)

	SET @ALAN2 = @ALAN2 +
						(SELECT
							'SUM(ISNULL(['+ANAHTAR+'],0)) AS ['+ANAHTAR+'], '
						FROM
							#TEMP_ASAMA
						WHERE
							SIRA = @ID)

	SET @ALAN3 = @ALAN3 +
						(SELECT
							'SUM(ISNULL(['+ANAHTAR+'],0)) + '
						FROM
							#TEMP_ASAMA
						WHERE
							SIRA = @ID)

	SET @ID = @ID + 1  
END

SET @ALAN = REPLACE(@ALAN,'][','],[')
SET @ALAN2 = SUBSTRING(@ALAN2,1,LEN(@ALAN2)-1)
SET @ALAN3 = SUBSTRING(@ALAN3,1,LEN(@ALAN3)-1) + ' AS [Toplam]'

SET @SQL = '
SELECT 
	SORUMLU AS [Sorumlu],
	MAX(FIRMA_SAYISI) AS [Firma Sayýsý],
	'+@ALAN2+',
	'+@ALAN3+',
	SUM(YENI_MUSTERI) AS [Yeni müþteri],
	SUM(TEKLIF_SAYISI) AS [Teklif]
FROM(
	SELECT
		COUNT(P.ID) AS PROJEID,
		(SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT REHBERID) FROM PROJELER P1 WHERE P1.MODUL = 1 AND P1.PRJ_SORUMLUSU_ID = P.PRJ_SORUMLUSU_ID AND P1.BASLAMATARIHI BETWEEN '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTAR,120)+' 00:00'' AND '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTAR,120)+' 23:59'' ) AS FIRMA_SAYISI,
		GASM.ANAHTAR AS ASAMA,
		RS.FIRMA AS SORUMLU,
		(SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT R.ID) FROM PROJELER P1 INNER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID = P1.REHBERID WHERE P1.MODUL = 1 AND P1.PRJ_SORUMLUSU_ID = P.PRJ_SORUMLUSU_ID AND P1.BASLAMATARIHI BETWEEN '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTAR,120)+' 00:00'' AND '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTAR,120)+' 23:59'' AND R.EKLEMETARIHI BETWEEN '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTAR,120)+' 00:00'' AND '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTAR,120)+' 23:59'') AS YENI_MUSTERI,
		SUM((SELECT COUNT(T.ID) FROM TEKLIF T WHERE T.PROJEID = P.ID)) OVER (PARTITION BY P.PRJ_SORUMLUSU_ID) AS TEKLIF_SAYISI
	FROM PROJELER P
		INNER JOIN GENINI GASM ON GASM.BOLUM = -2114 AND GASM.DEGER = P.ASAMA
		INNER JOIN REHBER RS ON RS.ID = P.PRJ_SORUMLUSU_ID
	WHERE
		P.MODUL=1
		AND P.BASLAMATARIHI BETWEEN '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BASTAR,120)+' 00:00'' AND '''+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@BITTAR,120)+' 23:59''
	GROUP BY
		GASM.ANAHTAR,
		RS.FIRMA,
        P.PRJ_SORUMLUSU_ID,
        P.ID,
		P.REHBERID
) AS X
PIVOT(
	SUM(PROJEID) FOR ASAMA IN ('+@ALAN+')
) AS PVT
GROUP BY
	SORUMLU
'
EXEC (@SQL)

DROP TABLE #TEMP_ASAMA
end


===== PROC: TM_SatisFirsatlarListeleGuncelle =====

-- exec [TM_SatisFirsatlarListeleGuncelle] 'Listele','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','',0
-- exec [TM_SatisFirsatlarListeleGuncelle] 'Guncelle','','2024-03-10 00:00:00','2024-03-10 00:00:00','ÜRET','123','€','2','62','0','ÜRET','','','2','','','1874','1'

CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_SatisFirsatlarListeleGuncelle](@TIP VARCHAR(20), @ACKAPA INT, @BASLAMATARIHI DATE, @BITISTARIHI DATE, @KONUSU VARCHAR(200), @SATISFIYATI FLOAT, @SATISKUR VARCHAR(20), @PROJESORUMLUSUID INT, @TURU VARCHAR(100), @TIPI VARCHAR(50), @PROJEKODU VARCHAR(200), @ILGILI VARCHAR(200), @OLASILIK VARCHAR(50),@ID INT, @SEBEBI VARCHAR(200), @RAKIP VARCHAR(250), @REHBERID FLOAT, @ASAMA VARCHAR(50), @KAPALILARDAGELSIN VARCHAR(10))
AS
BEGIN

IF @TIP='Listele'
begin
select 
    ACKAPA=CASE WHEN ISNULL(P.DURUM,0) IN (0,1) THEN NULL ELSE 'True' END,
	DURUM=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2114 AND DEGER=P.ASAMA),
	BASLAMATARIHI=CONVERT(DATE,BASLAMATARIHI),
	BITISTARIHI=CONVERT(DATE,BITISTARIHI),
	KONUSU,
	SATISFIYATI,
	SATISKUR,
	PRJ_SORUMLUSU_ID,
	PRJ_SORUMLUSU_AD=(SELECT TOP 1 FIRMA FROM REHBER WHERE ID = PRJ_SORUMLUSU_ID),
	TURU=ISNULL(G1.ANAHTAR,''), 	
	TIPI=ISNULL(G2.ANAHTAR,''), 	
	P.PROJEKODU,
	P.ILGILI,
	ILGILIAD=(SELECT TOP 1 FIRMA FROM REHBER WHERE ID = ILGILI),
	OLASILIK=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2119 AND DEGER=P.OLASILIK),
	SONAKTIVITEKONUSU = (SELECT TOP 1 KONUSU FROM GOREVLER A WHERE PROJEID = P.ID and ACKAPA=1 ORDER BY BITISTARIHI DESC ),
    SONAKTIVITETARIHI =  convert(Date,(SELECT TOP 1 BITISTARIHI FROM GOREVLER A WHERE PROJEID = P.ID and ACKAPA=1 ORDER BY BITISTARIHI DESC),103),
	P.ID, 
	SEBEBI=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2116 AND DEGER=P.SEBEBI), 
	RAKIP,
	R1.FIRMA,
	REHBERID,
	YORUM=(SELECT TOP 1 TRIM(char(10) from GY1.YORUM) FROM GOREVYORUM GY1 WHERE GY1.GOREVID=P.ID AND GY1.TUR=70 ORDER BY ID DESC),
	YORUMSAY=(SELECT COUNT(GY1.ID) FROM GOREVYORUM GY1 WHERE GY1.GOREVID=P.ID AND GY1.TUR=70),
	YORUMDETAY=23
from PROJELER P
	inner join REHBER R1 on R1.ID=P.REHBERID
	left outer join REHBER RP on RP.ID=P.ILGILI   
	LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI G1 ON G1.BOLUM=-2112 and G1.DIL=-1 AND G1.DEGER=P.TURU
	LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI G2 ON G2.DIL=-1 and G2.DEGER = P.TIPI AND G2.BOLUM=convert(int,'-2112'+convert(varchar(10),P.TURU))
WHERE
P.MODUL=1
AND P.DURUM <> CASE WHEN  @KAPALILARDAGELSIN = '1' THEN -1 ELSE 2 END
ORDER BY P.SATISKUR
end

IF @TIP='Guncelle'
begin
	if @ID<>0
	begin
		UPDATE PROJELER SET
			DURUM=@ACKAPA,
			ASAMA=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2114 AND ANAHTAR=@ASAMA),
			BASLAMATARIHI=	@BASLAMATARIHI, 
			BITISTARIHI=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@BITISTARIHI,'')='' THEN NULL ELSE @BITISTARIHI END, 
			KONUSU=@KONUSU, 
			SATISFIYATI=@SATISFIYATI, 
			SATISKUR=@SATISKUR, 
			PRJ_SORUMLUSU_ID=@PROJESORUMLUSUID, 
			TURU=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2112 AND ANAHTAR=@TURU), 
			TIPI=(SELECT TOP 1 G.DEGER FROM GENINI G INNER JOIN GENINI G1 ON G1.BOLUM = -2112 AND G.BOLUM = '-2112'+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),G1.DEGER) AND G.ANAHTAR=@TIPI),
			PROJEKODU=@PROJEKODU, 
			ILGILI= @ILGILI, 
			OLASILIK=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2119 AND ANAHTAR=@OLASILIK),
			SEBEBI=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2116 AND ANAHTAR=@SEBEBI),  
			RAKIP=@RAKIP, 
			REHBERID=@REHBERID
		WHERE ID=@ID
	end 
	if @ID=0
	begin 
		INSERT INTO PROJELER(DURUM,ASAMA,BASLAMATARIHI, BITISTARIHI, KONUSU, SATISFIYATI, SATISKUR, PRJ_SORUMLUSU_ID, TURU, TIPI, PROJEKODU, ILGILI, OLASILIK, SEBEBI, RAKIP, REHBERID, SUBEID,MODUL)		
		SELECT 
			DURUM=@ACKAPA,
			ASAMA=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2114 AND ANAHTAR=@ASAMA),
			@BASLAMATARIHI, 
			BITISTARIHI=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@BITISTARIHI,'')='' THEN NULL ELSE @BITISTARIHI END, 
			@KONUSU, 
			@SATISFIYATI, 
			@SATISKUR, 
			@PROJESORUMLUSUID, 
			TURU=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2112 AND ANAHTAR=@TURU), 
			TIPI=(SELECT TOP 1 G.DEGER FROM GENINI G INNER JOIN GENINI G1 ON G1.BOLUM = -2112 AND G.BOLUM = '-2112'+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),G1.DEGER) AND G.ANAHTAR=@TIPI), 
			@PROJEKODU, 
			@ILGILI, 
			OLASILIK=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2119 AND ANAHTAR=@OLASILIK), 
			SEBEBI=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2116 AND ANAHTAR=@SEBEBI), 
			@RAKIP, 
			@REHBERID, SUBEID=-1, MODUL='1'
	end
	exec [TM_SatisFirsatlarListeleGuncelle] 'Listele','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','', @KAPALILARDAGELSIN
end

IF @TIP='Sil'
begin
	delete from PROJELER WHERE ID=@ID
	exec [TM_SatisFirsatlarListeleGuncelle] 'Listele','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','','', @KAPALILARDAGELSIN
end
end



===== PROC: TM_SiparisAlinan =====




-- exec [TM_SiparisAlinan] '2024-09-11','2024-09-11',2

CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_SiparisAlinan](@BasTarihi VARCHAR(10), @BitTarihi VARCHAR(10), @ID INT, @TAMAMLANAN INT, @BAYIID INT, @KULLANICIKODU VARCHAR(20)=NULL)
AS
BEGIN 
DECLARE @ILGILIARAMA INT, @GORMEYETKISI INT, @SUBEID INT

 
SET @ILGILIARAMA = 0
SET @GORMEYETKISI = 0
SET @SUBEID = 0

SELECT @GORMEYETKISI=BILGI, @SUBEID=r.SUBEID FROM REHBER R INNER JOIN KULLANICI K ON K.REHBERID=R.ID INNER JOIN YETKIEK YE ON YE.ROLID=K.ROLID AND R.ID=@KULLANICIKODU AND MODULID= '2201'
 
IF @BAYIID=-1
BEGIN
SELECT distinct 
	F.ID,
	DURUM=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -24020 AND DEGER=F.DURUM),
	F.ODEMEPLANI,
	FATURATARIH=CONVERT(DATE,SIPARISTARIH,102),
	FATURATARIHT=CONVERT(VARCHAR(5),SIPARISTARIH,108),
	FATURANO=SIPARISNO,
	FATURASERI=F.SIPARISSERI,
	F.TIPI,
	F.REHBERID,
	F.TUR,
	F.SUBEID,
	F.BASLIK, 
	FATURA_MATRAHI=SIPARIS_TUTARI-KDV_TUTARI, 
	KDV_TUTARI,
	FATURA_TUTARI=SIPARIS_TUTARI,
	F.KUR,
	KURFATURA_MALIYETI_ORT=0.0,
	ORTKARORAN=0.0, 
	ORTKAR=0.0  ,
	F.ACIKLAMA,
	F.OZELKOD,
	F.OZELKOD2,
	CARIKOD=R.KOD,
	CARIAD=R.FIRMA,
	F.DOVIZ_CINSI,
	F.DOVIZKUR,
	DOVIZ_TUTARI=(SIPARIS_TUTARI/nullif(DOVIZKUR,0.0)),  
	DOVIZ_FATURA_MATRAHI=((SIPARIS_TUTARI-KDV_TUTARI)/nullif(DOVIZKUR,0.0)),
	DOVIZ_KDV_TUTARI=(convert(float,KDV_TUTARI)/nullif(convert(float,DOVIZKUR),0.0))  ,
	F.GIRISDEPO,
	CIKISDEPO=(SELECT DEPOADI FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.ID=F.CIKISDEPO),
	F.IRSALIYENO , 
	F.SATICIKODU,
	F.DETAYBOLUMU, 
	SATICIADI = SATICIBILGI.FIRMA, 
	F.VADE, 
	VADETARIH=SIPARISTARIH + F.VADE,   
	DURUMNEREDEN = case     when (F.TUR=9)and(412 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Teklifden'     
							when (F.TUR=19)and(413 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Teklifden'    
							when (F.TUR=9)and(83 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Servisden'     
							when (F.TUR=19)and(83 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Servisden'    
							when (F.TUR=9)and(428 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Talepten'    
							when (F.TUR=101)and(465 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Üretimden'    else '' end,   
	DURUMNEREYE = case  	when (F.TUR=9)and(407 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Faturaya'     	
							when (F.TUR=9)and(406 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Ýrsaliyeye'   	
							when (F.TUR=101)and(428 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Sipariþe'   	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(410 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Faturaya'    	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(409 in(select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Ýrsaliyeye'  	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(429 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Konsinyeye'  	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(415 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Üretim Fiþine'  	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(420 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Üretim Fiþine'  	else '' end,     
	TESLIMTARIHI =(Select Min(TESLIMTARIHI) from SIPARISDETAY Where SIPARISID=F.ID ), 
	FATURA_GON_TARIHI=null,  
	ZARFID=null,
	ZARF=null,
	ISEMRIDURUM=isnull((select I.DURUM from ISEMRI I where I.YERI=F.TUR and I.YERID=F.ID),-1),
	F.YAZDIRILDI,  
	F.ONAYLAYACAK,
	F.ONAYLAYAN,
	EFATURADURUM=0,
	REHBERILETID,
	REHBERILETAD=ISNULL(R1.AD,''),
	FIYAT_LISTESI=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -1007 AND DEGER=F.FIYAT_LISTESI),
	ODEME=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2904 AND DEGER=F.ODEME),
	TESLIM_SEKLI=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2903 AND DEGER=F.TESLIM_SEKLI),
	F.RAPORDOVIZ,
	FATBASLIK=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Fatura Baþlýðý' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID)
from SIPARIS F (NOLOCK) inner join REHBER R on R.ID = F.REHBERID  
						LEFT OUTER JOIN SIPARISDETAY SD ON F.ID = SD.SIPARISID 
						LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER SATICIBILGI ON F.SATICIKODU = SATICIBILGI.ID 
						LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERILETISIM R1 ON R1.REHBERID=f.REHBERID AND R1.ID=F.REHBERILETID  and R1.AKTIF=1 --and R1.VARSAYILAN=1
 where 	
	((
		1=1 and 
		F.TUR =19 and
		convert(date,SIPARISTARIH,102) between convert(date,@BasTarihi,102) and convert(date,@BitTarihi,102)
	) OR 
	F.ID=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ID,'')<>'' THEN @ID ELSE 0 END )	AND
	(ISNULL(F.DURUM,0) NOT LIKE CASE WHEN @TAMAMLANAN=0 THEN '9' ELSE '-1%' END)
		and ISNULL(F.SATICIKODU,'') LIKE case when @GORMEYETKISI=1 THEN @KULLANICIKODU ELSE '%' END AND
	ISNULL(R.SUBEID,-1) LIKE case when @GORMEYETKISI=10 THEN @SUBEID ELSE -1 END  
 ORDER BY SIPARISNO desc,F.TUR,F.KUR 
 


end
ELSE
BEGIN

SELECT distinct 
	F.ID,
	DURUM=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -24020 AND DEGER=F.DURUM),
	F.ODEMEPLANI,
	FATURATARIH=CONVERT(DATE,SIPARISTARIH,102),
	FATURATARIHT=CONVERT(VARCHAR(5),SIPARISTARIH,108),
	FATURANO=SIPARISNO,
	FATURASERI=F.SIPARISSERI,
	F.TIPI,
	F.REHBERID,
	F.TUR,
	F.SUBEID,
	F.BASLIK, 
	FATURA_MATRAHI=SIPARIS_TUTARI-KDV_TUTARI, 
	KDV_TUTARI,
	FATURA_TUTARI=SIPARIS_TUTARI,
	F.KUR,
	KURFATURA_MALIYETI_ORT=0.0,
	ORTKARORAN=0.0, 
	ORTKAR=0.0  ,
	F.ACIKLAMA,
	F.OZELKOD,
	F.OZELKOD2,
	CARIKOD=R.KOD,
	CARIAD=R.FIRMA,
	F.DOVIZ_CINSI,
	F.DOVIZKUR,
	DOVIZ_TUTARI=(SIPARIS_TUTARI/nullif(DOVIZKUR,0.0)),  
	DOVIZ_FATURA_MATRAHI=((SIPARIS_TUTARI-KDV_TUTARI)/nullif(DOVIZKUR,0.0)),
	DOVIZ_KDV_TUTARI=(convert(float,KDV_TUTARI)/nullif(convert(float,DOVIZKUR),0.0))  ,
	F.GIRISDEPO,
	CIKISDEPO=(SELECT DEPOADI FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.ID=F.CIKISDEPO),
	F.IRSALIYENO , 
	F.SATICIKODU,
	F.DETAYBOLUMU, 
	SATICIADI = SATICIBILGI.FIRMA, 
	F.VADE, 
	VADETARIH=SIPARISTARIH + F.VADE,   
	DURUMNEREDEN = case     when (F.TUR=9)and(412 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Teklifden'     
							when (F.TUR=19)and(413 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Teklifden'    
							when (F.TUR=9)and(83 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Servisden'     
							when (F.TUR=19)and(83 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Servisden'    
							when (F.TUR=9)and(428 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Talepten'    
							when (F.TUR=101)and(465 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID)) then 'Üretimden'    else '' end,   
	DURUMNEREYE = case  	when (F.TUR=9)and(407 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Faturaya'     	
							when (F.TUR=9)and(406 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Ýrsaliyeye'   	
							when (F.TUR=101)and(428 in (select YERI from SIPARISDETAY where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Sipariþe'   	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(410 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Faturaya'    	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(409 in(select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Ýrsaliyeye'  	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(429 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Konsinyeye'  	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(415 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Üretim Fiþine'  	
							when (F.TUR=19)and(420 in (select YERI from FATURA where YERID in (select ID from SIPARISDETAY where SIPARISID=F.ID))) then 'Üretim Fiþine'  	else '' end,     
	TESLIMTARIHI =(Select Min(TESLIMTARIHI) from SIPARISDETAY Where SIPARISID=F.ID ), 
	FATURA_GON_TARIHI=null,  
	ZARFID=null,
	ZARF=null,
	ISEMRIDURUM=isnull((select I.DURUM from ISEMRI I where I.YERI=F.TUR and I.YERID=F.ID),-1),
	F.YAZDIRILDI,  
	F.ONAYLAYACAK,
	F.ONAYLAYAN,
	EFATURADURUM=0,
	REHBERILETID,
	REHBERILETAD=ISNULL(R1.AD,''),
	FIYAT_LISTESI=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -1007 AND DEGER=F.FIYAT_LISTESI),
	ODEME=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2904 AND DEGER=F.ODEME),
	TESLIM_SEKLI=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2903 AND DEGER=F.TESLIM_SEKLI),
	F.RAPORDOVIZ,
	FATBASLIK=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERBILGI RB WHERE YERI = 2 AND ETIKET = 'Fatura Baþlýðý' AND RB.YER_ID=R.ID)
from SIPARIS F (NOLOCK) inner join REHBER R on R.ID = F.REHBERID  
						LEFT OUTER JOIN SIPARISDETAY SD ON F.ID = SD.SIPARISID 
						LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER SATICIBILGI ON F.SATICIKODU = SATICIBILGI.ID 
						LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERILETISIM R1 ON R1.REHBERID=f.REHBERID AND R1.ID=F.REHBERILETID  and R1.AKTIF=1 --and R1.VARSAYILAN=1
 where 	
 R.ID=@BAYIID AND
	((
		1=1 and 
		F.TUR =19 and
		convert(date,SIPARISTARIH,102) between convert(date,@BasTarihi,102) and convert(date,@BitTarihi,102)
	) OR 
	F.ID=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ID,'')<>'' THEN @ID ELSE 0 END )	AND
	(ISNULL(F.DURUM,0) NOT LIKE CASE WHEN @TAMAMLANAN=0 THEN '9' ELSE '-1%' END)
		and ISNULL(F.SATICIKODU,'') LIKE case when @GORMEYETKISI=1 THEN @KULLANICIKODU ELSE '%' END AND
	ISNULL(R.SUBEID,-1) LIKE case when @GORMEYETKISI=10 THEN @SUBEID ELSE -1 END  
 ORDER BY SIPARISNO desc,F.TUR,F.KUR 
 END
--  exec [TM_SiparisAlinan] '2023-05-27', '2023-12-03'
-- select * FROM SIPARIS WHERE ID='5544'
--select * FROM REHBER WHERE ID=1187


END

===== PROC: TM_SiparisAlinanDetay =====




-- exec [TM_SiparisAlinanDetay] '5543'

CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_SiparisAlinanDetay](@SIPARISID INT)
AS
BEGIN 

Select 
		AD = CASE WHEN F.TUR =0 THEN  (select MG.AD from MASRAFGELIR MG where MG.ID=F.URUNID) ELSE (select S.STOKADI from STOKLAR S where S.ID=F.URUNID) END,
		KOD= CASE WHEN F.TUR =0 THEN  (select MG.KOD from MASRAFGELIR MG where MG.ID=F.URUNID) ELSE (select S.KOD from STOKLAR S where S.ID=F.URUNID) END,
		URUNNO= CASE WHEN F.TUR =0 THEN  '''' ELSE (select S.URUNNO from STOKLAR S where S.ID=F.URUNID) END,
		RESIM=(select case when S.RESIM is null then 0 else 1 end from STOKLAR S where S.ID = F.URUNID ),
		DOKUMAN=(select case when exists(select GY.ID, STOKID=GY.GOREVID, DOKUMANID=D.ID, DOKUMANAD=D.AD from GOREVYORUM GY inner join DOKUMAN D on D.MODUL=210 and D.MODULID=GY.ID  where GY.TUR=88 and GOREVID=S.ID) then 1 else 0 end   from STOKLAR S where S.ID = F.URUNID), 
		BIRIMAD = (SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM=-2702 and DIL=-1 and DEGER = F.BIRIM),
		BIRIM2MIKTAR=CASE WHEN F.TUR=0 THEN F.MIKTAR ELSE F.MIKTAR/(select S.BIRIM2MIKTAR from STOKLAR S where S.ID=F.URUNID) END,
		BIRIM2AD= CASE WHEN F.TUR =0 THEN  (SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM =-2702 and DEGER = convert(varchar(10),F.BIRIM) )  ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM =-2702 and DEGER = convert(varchar(10),(select S.BIRIM2 from STOKLAR S where S.ID=F.URUNID)) ) END,
		PROJEKODU=(Select P.PROJEKODU from PROJELER P Where P.ID=F.PROJEID),
		SATICIADI=(select R.FIRMA from REHBER R where R.ID=F.SATICIKODU),
		DONUSENMIKTAR=isnull((select top 1 F.MIKTAR*(SC.ADET2/SC.ADET1) from STOKCEVRIM SC where SC.STOKID=F.URUNID and F.TUR=1 and F.BIRIM=SC.BIRIM1 and SC.BIRIM2=(select S.DONUSUMTURU from SIPARIS S where S.ID=F.SIPARISID) ),0.0),
		URETIMPLANINDAGOSTER=case when isnull(URETIMPLANDETAYID,0)<0 then 0 when isnull(URETIMPLANDETAYID,0)=0 then 1 else null end,
		EKIPMAN=(select E1.AD from EKIPMANLAR E1 inner join EKIPMANREHBER ER1 on E1.ID=ER1.EKIPMANID where ER1.ID=F.EKIPMANID),
		SERINO=(select ER2.SERINO from EKIPMANREHBER ER2  where ER2.ID=F.EKIPMANID),
		F.*,
		KDVTUTAR=KDVDAHILFIYAT-TUTAR
from SIPARISDETAY F
Where SIPARISID = @SIPARISID
order by 2
end

--  exec [TM_SiparisAlinan] '2023-05-27', '2023-12-03',''
-- select * FROM SIPARIS WHERE ID='5544'
--select * FROM REHBER WHERE ID=1187


===== PROC: TM_SiparisDetayGir =====




-- exec [TM_SiparisDetayGir] '5567','1115','Stok','505','','1','51','','150','0','','8','','','TL','','TL','','','','2','','2023-06-15','-1','','220','','','','',''

 
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_SiparisDetayGir](@ID INT, @SIPARISID INT, @REHBERID INT, @TUR varchar(50), @URUNID INT, @ACIKLAMA VARCHAR(250), @ADET INT, @BIRIM varchar(50), 
											@MIKTAR INT, @BIRIMFIYAT FLOAT, @TUTAR FLOAT, @ISKONTO FLOAT, @KDV FLOAT, @MASRAFID INT, @OZELKOD VARCHAR(50), 
											@KUR VARCHAR(50), @DOVIZ_TUTARI FLOAT, @DOVIZ_KURU VARCHAR(50), @ISKONTO2 FLOAT, @DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT FLOAT, 
											@DOVIZKURDEGERI FLOAT, @EKLEYEN  INT, @PROJEID INT, @TESLIMTARIHI DATETIME, @SUBEID INT, @URETIMPLANDETAYID INT, 
											@STOKDURUM FLOAT, @EKIPMANID INT, @GIRISKAYNAK FLOAT, @OTVYUZDE FLOAT, @OTVMIKTAR FLOAT, @OZELKOD2 VARCHAR(50))
AS
BEGIN 
IF @TUR='Sil'
begin
	DELETE FROM SIPARISDETAY WHERE ID=@ID
end else
BEGIN TRY
IF ISNULL(@ID,'')=''
BEGIN
INSERT INTO SIPARISDETAY 
		(SIPARISID,REHBERID,TUR,URUNID, ACIKLAMA,ADET,
		 BIRIM,
		 MIKTAR,BIRIMFIYAT,TUTAR,ISKONTO,KDV,
			MASRAFID,OZELKOD,KUR,DOVIZ_TUTARI,DOVIZ_KURU,ISKONTO2,DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT,
			DOVIZKURDEGERI,
			EKLEYEN,PROJEID,TESLIMTARIHI,SUBEID,URETIMPLANDETAYID,STOKDURUM,EKIPMANID,GIRISKAYNAK,
			OTVYUZDE,OTVMIKTAR,OZELKOD2 ) 
	SELECT 
		@SIPARISID, @REHBERID, 
		TUR=CASE WHEN @TUR='Stok' then 1 else 0 end,
		@URUNID, @ACIKLAMA, @ADET,
		@BIRIM, 
		@MIKTAR, 
		
		BIRIMFIYAT=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@DOVIZ_KURU,'TL')='TL' THEN @BIRIMFIYAT ELSE  (@BIRIMFIYAT*(SELECT TOP 1 SATIS FROM DOVIZ D WHERE CONVERT(DATE,D.TARIH,102)=CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE(),102) AND CINSI=@DOVIZ_KURU)) END,
		TUTAR=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@DOVIZ_KURU,'TL')='TL' THEN ((@MIKTAR*@BIRIMFIYAT)/(100))*(100-@ISKONTO) ELSE  ((@MIKTAR*(@BIRIMFIYAT*(SELECT TOP 1 SATIS FROM DOVIZ D WHERE CONVERT(DATE,D.TARIH,102)=CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE(),102) AND CINSI=@DOVIZ_KURU)))/(100))*(100-@ISKONTO) END, 
		@ISKONTO, @KDV,
		@MASRAFID, @OZELKOD, @KUR,
		
		DOVIZ_TUTARI=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@DOVIZ_KURU,'TL')='TL' THEN ((@MIKTAR*@BIRIMFIYAT)/(100))*(100-@ISKONTO) ELSE ((@MIKTAR*@BIRIMFIYAT)/(100))*(100-@ISKONTO) END, 
		
		@DOVIZ_KURU, @ISKONTO2,
		DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@DOVIZ_KURU,'TL')='TL' THEN @BIRIMFIYAT ELSE @BIRIMFIYAT END,  
		DOVIZKURDEGERI=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@DOVIZ_KURU,'TL')='TL' THEN 1 ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 SATIS FROM DOVIZ D WHERE CONVERT(DATE,D.TARIH,102)=CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE(),102) AND CINSI=@DOVIZ_KURU) END, 
		@EKLEYEN,
		@PROJEID, @TESLIMTARIHI, @SUBEID,
		@URETIMPLANDETAYID, @STOKDURUM, @EKIPMANID,
		@GIRISKAYNAK, @OTVYUZDE, @OTVMIKTAR,
		@OZELKOD2		

		update SIPARIS SET RAPORDOVIZ=@DOVIZ_KURU, DOVIZ_CINSI=@DOVIZ_KURU, DOVIZKUR=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@DOVIZ_KURU,'TL')='TL' THEN 1 ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 SATIS FROM DOVIZ D WHERE CONVERT(DATE,D.TARIH,102)=CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE(),102) AND CINSI=@DOVIZ_KURU) END WHERE ID = @SIPARISID AND ISNULL(RAPORDOVIZ,'')='TL'
end ELSE
BEGIN
	UPDATE SIPARISDETAY SET 
		
		SIPARISID=@SIPARISID, REHBERID=@REHBERID, 
		TUR=CASE WHEN @TUR='Stok' then 1 else 0 end,
		URUNID=@URUNID, ACIKLAMA=@ACIKLAMA, ADET=@ADET,
		BIRIM=@BIRIM, 
		MIKTAR=@MIKTAR, 
		BIRIMFIYAT=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@DOVIZ_KURU,'TL')='TL' THEN @BIRIMFIYAT ELSE  (@BIRIMFIYAT*(SELECT TOP 1 SATIS FROM DOVIZ D WHERE CONVERT(DATE,D.TARIH,102)=CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE(),102) AND CINSI=@DOVIZ_KURU)) END,
		TUTAR=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@DOVIZ_KURU,'TL')='TL' THEN ((@MIKTAR*@BIRIMFIYAT)/(100))*(100-@ISKONTO) ELSE  ((@MIKTAR*(@BIRIMFIYAT*(SELECT TOP 1 SATIS FROM DOVIZ D WHERE CONVERT(DATE,D.TARIH,102)=CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE(),102) AND CINSI=@DOVIZ_KURU)))/(100))*(100-@ISKONTO) END,
		ISKONTO=@ISKONTO, KDV=@KDV,
		MASRAFID=@MASRAFID, OZELKOD=@OZELKOD, 
		KUR=@KUR,
		DOVIZ_TUTARI=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@DOVIZ_KURU,'TL')='TL' THEN ((@MIKTAR*@BIRIMFIYAT)/(100))*(100-@ISKONTO) ELSE ((@MIKTAR*@BIRIMFIYAT)/(100))*(100-@ISKONTO) END, 
		DOVIZ_KURU=@DOVIZ_KURU, ISKONTO2=@ISKONTO2,
		DOVIZ_BIRIMFIYAT=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@DOVIZ_KURU,'TL')='TL' THEN @BIRIMFIYAT ELSE @BIRIMFIYAT END, 
		DOVIZKURDEGERI=CASE WHEN ISNULL(@DOVIZ_KURU,'TL')='TL' THEN 1 ELSE (SELECT TOP 1 SATIS FROM DOVIZ D WHERE CONVERT(DATE,D.TARIH,102)=CONVERT(DATE,GETDATE(),102) AND CINSI=@DOVIZ_KURU) END, 
		EKLEYEN=@EKLEYEN,
		PROJEID=@PROJEID, TESLIMTARIHI=@TESLIMTARIHI, SUBEID=@SUBEID,
		URETIMPLANDETAYID=@URETIMPLANDETAYID, STOKDURUM=@STOKDURUM, EKIPMANID=@EKIPMANID,
		GIRISKAYNAK=@GIRISKAYNAK, OTVYUZDE=@OTVYUZDE, OTVMIKTAR=@OTVMIKTAR,
		OZELKOD2=@OZELKOD2 where ID=@ID
END

UPDATE S SET 
	S.SIPARIS_TUTARI=(select SUM(SD.KDVDAHILFIYAT) from SIPARISDETAY SD where S.ID=SD.SIPARISID),
	S.SIPARIS_MATRAHI=(select SUM(TUTAR) from SIPARISDETAY SD where S.ID=SD.SIPARISID),
	S.DOVIZ_TUTARI=(select SUM(SD.DOVIZ_TUTARI) from SIPARISDETAY SD where S.ID=SD.SIPARISID),
	S.KDV_TUTARI=(select SUM(SD.KDVDAHILFIYAT-SD.TUTAR) from SIPARISDETAY SD where S.ID=SD.SIPARISID)  
FROM SIPARIS S WHERE S.ID=@SIPARISID
select 'Kayýt baþarýlý.'
END TRY
			BEGIN CATCH
					SELECT 'Hata oluþtu....'+ERROR_MESSAGE()--+' - '+ISNULL(convert(datetime,(substring(@BasTar,7,4)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,4,2)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,1,2) +' '+@BasTarT)),'')
			END CATCH
END
