-- select * FROM SIPARIS WHERE ID='5544'
--select * FROM REHBER WHERE ID=1187


===== PROC: TM_SiparisDurumGuncelle =====
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[TM_SiparisDurumGuncelle](@SIPARISID INT)
AS
BEGIN
	DECLARE
		   @TUR INT,
		   @SATIRSAY INT,
		   @TAMAMLANDI INT,
		   @KISMI INT,
		   @YENI INT

	SET @TUR = (SELECT TUR FROM SIPARIS WHERE ID = @SIPARISID)

	SET @SATIRSAY = (SELECT COUNT(ID) FROM SIPARISDETAY WHERE SIPARISID = @SIPARISID)

	SET @TAMAMLANDI = ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(SD.ID) FROM SIPARISDETAY SD WHERE SD.SIPARISID = @SIPARISID AND SD.ADET - ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(ADET,0)) FROM FATURA WHERE YERI IN (409,410,429,473) AND YERID = SD.ID),0) <= 0),0)

	SET @KISMI = ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(SD.ID) FROM SIPARISDETAY SD WHERE SD.SIPARISID = 10224 AND SD.ADET - ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(ADET,0)) FROM FATURA WHERE YERI IN (409,410,429,473) AND YERID = SD.ID),0) > 0 AND SD.ADET <> SD.ADET - ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ADET) FROM FATURA WHERE YERI IN (409,410,429,473) AND YERID = SD.ID),0)),0)

	SET @YENI = ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(SD.ID) FROM SIPARISDETAY SD WHERE SD.SIPARISID = @SIPARISID AND  SD.ADET = SD.ADET - ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(ADET,0)) FROM FATURA WHERE YERI IN (409,410,429,473) AND YERID = SD.ID),0)),0)

	IF (@TUR = 19)
	BEGIN
		IF (@SATIRSAY = @TAMAMLANDI)
		BEGIN
			UPDATE SIPARIS SET DURUM = 9 WHERE ID = @SIPARISID
		END
		IF (@KISMI > 0 OR (@TAMAMLANDI <> 0 AND @SATIRSAY <> @TAMAMLANDI))
		BEGIN
			UPDATE SIPARIS SET DURUM = 1 WHERE ID = @SIPARISID
		END
		IF (@SATIRSAY = @YENI)
		BEGIN
			UPDATE SIPARIS SET DURUM = 0 WHERE ID = @SIPARISID
		END
	END
END


===== PROC: TM_SiparisGuncelle =====

--rapor dövizi boþ býrakýyoruz, ilk sipariþ kaleminin döviz tipi neyse onunla update edicez. 10092024

--exec [TM_SiparisGuncelle] '','2023-06-18','19','1','1874','-1','-1','2023-06-18 21:05','1901','','Anadepo','','','','','','','Hariç','','','','','2','Yapýlmadý','','2','Satýþ','','','','','-1','-1','TL'


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_SiparisGuncelle](@ID INT, @TARIH SMALLDATETIME, @TUR SMALLINT, @TIPI SMALLINT, @REHBERID INT, @PROJEID INT, @AKTIVITEID INT,
											@SIPARISTARIH DATETIME, @KOCANNO INT, @SIPARISNO NVARCHAR(10), @CIKISDEPO VARCHAR(50), @BASLIK NVARCHAR(100),
											@ADRES NVARCHAR(150), @ILCE NVARCHAR(50), @IL NVARCHAR(50), @VD NVARCHAR(50), @VNO NVARCHAR(50), @KDVDURUM NVARCHAR(5),
											@KUR NVARCHAR(10), @DOVIZ_TUTARI MONEY, @DOVIZ_CINSI NVARCHAR(6), @ACIKLAMA NVARCHAR(100), @SATICIKODU INT, @DURUM VARCHAR(50),
											@SIPARISSERI NVARCHAR(10), @EKLEYEN INT, @FIYAT_LISTESI VARCHAR(50), @DOVIZKUR MONEY, @TESLIM_SEKLI varchar(50), @ODEME varchar(50), @REHBERILETID INT,
											@SUBEID INT, @SERVISID INT, @RAPORDOVIZ NVARCHAR(5), @VADE INT, @BAYIID INT
											)
AS
BEGIN 
DECLARE @YENIID INT

if @SIPARISNO<>'Sil'
begin
BEGIN TRY

			IF @ID IN (SELECT ID FROM SIPARIS UO WHERE ID=@ID)
			BEGIN
					UPDATE S SET TARIH=@TARIH,
								TUR=@TUR, 
								TIPI=@TIPI,
								REHBERID=@REHBERID, PROJEID=@PROJEID, AKTIVITEID=@AKTIVITEID, SIPARISTARIH=@SIPARISTARIH, KOCANNO=@KOCANNO,
								SIPARISNO=@SIPARISNO, 
								CIKISDEPO=(SELECT ID FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.DEPOADI=@CIKISDEPO), 
								BASLIK=	ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=R.ID AND RB.YERI=2 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=10),R.FIRMA), 
								ADRES=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=Firma.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=2), 
								ILCE=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=Firma.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=6), 
								IL=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=Firma.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=8), 
								VD=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=R.ID AND RB.YERI=2 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=20), 
								VNO=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=R.ID AND RB.YERI=2 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=22), 
								KDVDURUM=@KDVDURUM, KUR=@KUR, DOVIZ_TUTARI=@DOVIZ_TUTARI, DOVIZ_CINSI=@DOVIZ_CINSI, 
								ACIKLAMA=@ACIKLAMA, SATICIKODU=@SATICIKODU, 
								DURUM=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -24020 AND ANAHTAR=@DURUM), 
								SIPARISSERI=@SIPARISSERI, EKLEYEN=@EKLEYEN, 
								FIYAT_LISTESI=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -1007 AND ANAHTAR=@FIYAT_LISTESI), 
								DOVIZKUR=@DOVIZKUR, 
					

								TESLIM_SEKLI=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2903 AND ANAHTAR=@TESLIM_SEKLI), 
								ODEME=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2904 AND ANAHTAR=@ODEME), 
								REHBERILETID=@REHBERILETID, SUBEID=@SUBEID, SERVISID=@SERVISID,
								VADE=@VADE
								, RAPORDOVIZ=@RAPORDOVIZ					
					FROM SIPARIS S INNER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID=S.REHBERID 
									LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERILETISIM Firma ON R.ID=Firma.REHBERID and Firma.VARSAYILAN=1 and Firma.AKTIF=1						

					WHERE
					R.ID=@REHBERID AND S.ID=@ID
					EXEC DBO.TM_SiparisAlinan '1900-01-01','1900-01-01',@ID,'1',@BAYIID
			END ELSE

			BEGIN
					INSERT INTO SIPARIS (
								TARIH,TUR,TIPI,REHBERID,PROJEID,AKTIVITEID,SIPARISTARIH,KOCANNO,
								SIPARISNO,
								CIKISDEPO,BASLIK,
								ADRES,
								ILCE,
								IL,
								VD,
								VNO,
								KDVDURUM,KUR,DOVIZ_TUTARI,DOVIZ_CINSI,
								ACIKLAMA,SATICIKODU,DURUM,SIPARISSERI,EKLEYEN,FIYAT_LISTESI,DOVIZKUR,
								TESLIM_SEKLI,ODEME,REHBERILETID,SUBEID,SERVISID,RAPORDOVIZ, VADE, GIRISKAYNAK) 
					SELECT @TARIH, @TUR, @TIPI, @REHBERID, @PROJEID, @AKTIVITEID, @SIPARISTARIH, @KOCANNO, 
								SIPARISNO=(SELECT Replicate('0',6-(len(MAX(SIPARISNO)+1)))+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),(MAX(SIPARISNO)+1)) FROM SIPARIS WHERE KOCANNO=1901), 
								CIKISDEPO=(SELECT ID FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.DEPOADI=@CIKISDEPO), 
								BASLIK=ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=R.ID AND RB.YERI=2 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=10),R.FIRMA), 
								ADRES=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=Firma.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=2), 
								ILCE=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=Firma.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=6), 
								IL=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=Firma.ID AND RB.YERI=1 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=8), 
								ID_VERGIDAI=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=R.ID AND RB.YERI=2 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=20), 
								ID_VERGINO=(SELECT TOP 1 BILGI FROM REHBERAYAR RA INNER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RA.SIRA=RB.SIRA AND RA.YERI=RB.YERI WHERE RB.YER_ID=R.ID AND RB.YERI=2 AND RA.VARSAYILAN=22), 
								@KDVDURUM, @KUR, @DOVIZ_TUTARI, @DOVIZ_CINSI, 
								@ACIKLAMA, @SATICIKODU, 
								DURUM=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -24020 AND ANAHTAR=@DURUM), 
								@SIPARISSERI, @EKLEYEN, 
					
								FIYAT_LISTESI=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -1007 AND ANAHTAR=@FIYAT_LISTESI), 
					
								@DOVIZKUR, 
								TESLIM_SEKLI=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2903 AND ANAHTAR=@TESLIM_SEKLI), 
								ODEME=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2904 AND ANAHTAR=@ODEME),
								@REHBERILETID, @SUBEID, @SERVISID, RAPORDOVIZ=@RAPORDOVIZ, @VADE, GIRISKAYNAK =1
					FROM REHBER R LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERILETISIM Firma ON R.ID=Firma.REHBERID and Firma.VARSAYILAN=1 and Firma.AKTIF=1
					WHERE
					R.ID=@REHBERID

					SET @YENIID = @@Identity
					EXEC DBO.TM_SiparisAlinan '1900-01-01','1900-01-01',@YENIID,'1',@BAYIID
			END



			END TRY
			BEGIN CATCH
					SELECT 'Hata oluþtu....'+ERROR_MESSAGE()--+' - '+ISNULL(convert(datetime,(substring(@BasTar,7,4)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,4,2)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,1,2) +' '+@BasTarT)),'')
			END CATCH
end 
if @SIPARISNO='Sil'
begin
	DELETE FROM SIPARISDETAY WHERE SIPARISID=@ID
	DELETE FROM SIPARIS WHERE ID=@ID
end
end

===== PROC: TM_StokAra =====

--SELECT * FROM KATEGORI
-- select * from stoklar where kod='105PP'
-- exec [TM_StokAra] 'STOK','','F','51709',''


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_StokAra](@AD VARCHAR(20), @KOD VARCHAR(30), @URUNID VARCHAR(20), @REHBERID varchar(50), @BARKOD VARCHAR(30))
AS
BEGIN 
	declare @FiyatAdi int 
	declare @Depo int = 0
	declare @RID int
	declare @BELGETUR int

	--set @FiyatAdi=(SELECT G.DEGER FROM REHBERBILGI RB INNER JOIN GENINI G ON BOLUM = -1007 AND ANAHTAR=BILGI WHERE RB.ETIKET='Fiyat Liste Adý' AND  RB.YER_ID=@REHBERID)			
	IF @URUNID='S'
	BEGIN
		set @FiyatAdi=(SELECT FIYAT_LISTESI FROM SIPARIS WHERE ID=@REHBERID)			
		set @Depo=1
		set @RID=(SELECT S.REHBERID FROM SIPARIS S WHERE ID=@REHBERID)			
	END ELSE
	IF @URUNID='F'
	BEGIN
		SELECT @FiyatAdi=CASE WHEN TUR<>'20' THEN FIYAT_LISTESI ELSE -2 END FROM FATBASLIK WHERE ID=@REHBERID	
		SELECT @Depo=CASE WHEN TUR='14' THEN CIKISDEPO WHEN TUR='10' THEN GIRISDEPO ELSE CIKISDEPO END, @BELGETUR=TUR FROM FATBASLIK WHERE ID=@REHBERID
		set @RID=(SELECT S.REHBERID FROM FATBASLIK S WHERE ID=@REHBERID)			
	END ELSE
	IF @URUNID='SD'	
	BEGIN
		set @FiyatAdi=''
		set @Depo=ISNULL((SELECT ID FROM DEPOLAR WHERE DEPOLAR.DEPOADI=@REHBERID),0)
		set @RID=''		 
	END ELSE	
	BEGIN
		set @FiyatAdi=(SELECT FIYAT_LISTESI FROM SIPARIS WHERE ID=@REHBERID)			
		set @Depo=1
		set @RID=(SELECT S.REHBERID FROM SIPARIS S WHERE ID=@REHBERID)	 		
	END


IF @URUNID<>'' AND @URUNID<>'F' AND @URUNID<>'S' AND @URUNID<>'SD'
BEGIN
	SELECT 
		*, 
		KALANRESIM=CASE WHEN ISNULL(KALAN,0)>0 THEN 18 ELSE 19 END,
		ISKONTO=ISNULL((Select TOP 1 KC.MIKTAR from KAMPANYACARI KC inner join GENINI G on G.BOLUM=-2221 and DIL=-1 and G.DEGER=KC.KAMPANYAID where REHBERID=@RID AND DURUM=1 AND (KOD=(case KC.KAMPANYAID
					when -1 THEN (select S.KOD from STOKLAR S where S.ID=KC.URUNID)
					when -2 THEN (select K.KOD from KATEGORI K where K.ID=KC.URUNID)
					when -11 THEN (select M.KOD from MASRAFGELIR M where M.ID=KC.URUNID)
					when -12 THEN (select M.KOD from MASRAFGELIR M where M.ID=KC.URUNID) end) 
					OR
					KATEGORI=(case KC.KAMPANYAID
					  when -1 THEN (select S.KOD from STOKLAR S where S.ID=KC.URUNID)
					  when -2 THEN (select K.KOD from KATEGORI K where K.ID=KC.URUNID)
					  when -11 THEN (select M.KOD from MASRAFGELIR M where M.ID=KC.URUNID)
					  when -12 THEN (select M.KOD from MASRAFGELIR M where M.ID=KC.URUNID)
					end) 
					OR 
					KAMPANYAID=-3) order by  KC.KAMPANYAID DESC),0)

	FROM (
				select TOP 200  
 					S.ID,                                
 					S.KOD,                         
 					AD=STOKADI,                          
 					TUR='Stok',                        
 					KALAN=isnull((select sum(KALAN) from STOKDURUM SD where SD.STOKID=S.ID and SD.DEPOID=@Depo ),0),
 					FIYAT=isnull(SF.FIYAT,-1),         
 					KUR=isnull(SF.KUR,'TL'),
 					STOKMARKA=StokMarka.ANAHTAR ,        
 					STOKMODEL=StokModel.ANAHTAR ,        
 					KDV=S.KDV, S.OTVYUZDE,S.OTVMIKTAR,   
 					KDVDURUM=SF.KDVDURUM,                
 					PAKET=isnull(S.PAKET,0),             
 					IZLEME=isnull(S.IZLEME,0),           
 					BIRIM=isnull(S.ANABIRIM,51),
					BIRIMAD=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE bolum=-2702 AND DEGER=isnull(S.ANABIRIM,51)),
					STOKGRUBU=StokGrubu.ANAHTAR,
					S.GELIRID AS MASRAFID,
					S.OZELKOD,
					S.OZELKOD2,
					FIYAT_LISTE=Fiyatadi.ANAHTAR,
					DEPOADI=isnull((select TOP 1  D.DEPOADI from DEPOLAR D where D.ID=@Depo ),0),
					S.URUNNO , 
					KATEGORI=(select TOP 1 KA.KOD from KATEGORI KA WHERE KA.ID=S.KATEGORI),
					KATEGORIAD=(select TOP 1 KA.AD from KATEGORI KA WHERE KA.ID=S.KATEGORI)	,
					DEPODETAYJSON=''
				 From                                  
 					STOKLAR S (nolock) LEFT OUTER JOIN STOKFIYAT SF (nolock) on S.ID = SF.STOKID and SF.BIRIM = ANABIRIM and SF.FIYATADI=@Fiyatadi and SF.PAKETID=0 and SF.SATIS=case when @FiyatAdi=-2 then 0 else 1 end    
									   LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokMarka ON StokMarka.DEGER = S.MARKA AND StokMarka.DIL=-1 AND StokMarka.BOLUM =-2701             
									   LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokModel ON StokModel.DEGER=S.MODEL AND StokModel.DIL=-1 AND StokModel.BOLUM=convert(int,'-2701'+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),S.MARKA))
									   LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokGrubu ON S.GRUBU = StokGrubu.DEGER AND StokGrubu.DIL=-1 AND StokGrubu.BOLUM=-2704
									   LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI Fiyatadi ON Fiyatadi.BOLUM = -1007 AND Fiyatadi.DEGER=SF.FIYATADI
				 where 
					S.DURUM=1 and 
					S.INTERNET_SATIS=1 AND
					S.ID=@URUNID
				 union all 
				 select TOP 200  
 					S.ID,                                
 					KOD=S.KOD+'#',                     
 					AD=STOKADI,                          
 					TUR='Stok',                        
 					KALAN=isnull((select ROUND(sum(KALAN) / S.BIRIM2MIKTAR,0,1 ) from STOKDURUM SD where SD.STOKID=S.ID and SD.DEPOID=@Depo ),0),
 					FIYAT=isnull(SF.FIYAT,-1),         
 					KUR=isnull(SF.KUR,'TL'),
 					STOKMARKA=StokMarka.ANAHTAR ,        
 					STOKMODEL=StokModel.ANAHTAR ,        
 					KDV=S.KDV, S.OTVYUZDE,S.OTVMIKTAR,   
 					KDVDURUM=SF.KDVDURUM,                
 					PAKET=isnull(S.PAKET,0),             
 					IZLEME=isnull(S.IZLEME,0),           
 					BIRIM=isnull(S.BIRIM2,51),
					BIRIMAD=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE bolum=-2702 AND DEGER=isnull(S.BIRIM2,51)),
					STOKGRUBU=StokGrubu.ANAHTAR,
					S.GELIRID AS MASRAFID,
					S.OZELKOD,
					S.OZELKOD2,
					FIYAT_LISTE=Fiyatadi.ANAHTAR,
					DEPOADI=isnull((select D.DEPOADI from DEPOLAR D where D.ID=@Depo ),0),
					S.URUNNO, 
					KATEGORI=(select TOP 1 KA.KOD from KATEGORI KA WHERE KA.ID=S.KATEGORI),
					KATEGORIAD=(select TOP 1 KA.AD from KATEGORI KA WHERE KA.ID=S.KATEGORI),
					DEPODETAYJSON=''	
				 From                                  
 					STOKLAR S (nolock) LEFT OUTER JOIN STOKFIYAT SF (nolock) on S.ID = SF.STOKID and SF.BIRIM = S.BIRIM2 and SF.FIYATADI=@Fiyatadi and SF.PAKETID=0 and S.DURUM=1 and SF.SATIS=case when @FiyatAdi=-2 then 0 else 1 end    													   
									   LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokMarka ON StokMarka.DEGER = S.MARKA AND StokMarka.DIL=-1 AND StokMarka.BOLUM =-2701
									   LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokModel ON StokModel.DEGER=S.MODEL AND StokModel.DIL=-1 AND StokModel.BOLUM=convert(int,'-2701'+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),S.MARKA))
									   LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokGrubu ON S.GRUBU = StokGrubu.DEGER AND StokGrubu.DIL=-1 AND StokGrubu.BOLUM=-2704              
									   LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI Fiyatadi ON Fiyatadi.BOLUM = -1007 AND Fiyatadi.DEGER=SF.FIYATADI
				 where 
					S.DURUM=1 and
					S.ANABIRIM<>isnull(S.BIRIM2,ANABIRIM) and	
					S.INTERNET_SATIS=1 AND
					S.ID=@URUNID) DD
				 order by 2 
END ELSE
IF @URUNID='SD'
BEGIN
			SELECT *, KALANRESIM=CASE WHEN ISNULL(KALAN,0)>0 THEN 18 ELSE 19 END,

		ISKONTO=ISNULL((Select TOP 1 KC.MIKTAR from KAMPANYACARI KC inner join GENINI G on G.BOLUM=-2221 and DIL=-1 and G.DEGER=KC.KAMPANYAID where REHBERID=@RID AND DURUM=1 AND (KOD=(case KC.KAMPANYAID
			when -1 THEN (select S.KOD from STOKLAR S where S.ID=KC.URUNID)
			when -2 THEN (select K.KOD from KATEGORI K where K.ID=KC.URUNID)
			when -11 THEN (select M.KOD from MASRAFGELIR M where M.ID=KC.URUNID)
			when -12 THEN (select M.KOD from MASRAFGELIR M where M.ID=KC.URUNID)
		end) OR
		KATEGORI=(case KC.KAMPANYAID
			when -1 THEN (select S.KOD from STOKLAR S where S.ID=KC.URUNID)
			when -2 THEN (select K.KOD from KATEGORI K where K.ID=KC.URUNID)
			when -11 THEN (select M.KOD from MASRAFGELIR M where M.ID=KC.URUNID)
			when -12 THEN (select M.KOD from MASRAFGELIR M where M.ID=KC.URUNID)
		end) OR KAMPANYAID=-3 ) order by  KC.KAMPANYAID DESC),0) FROM (
			select TOP 200  
 			S.ID,                                
 			S.KOD,                         
 			AD=STOKADI,                          
 			TUR='Stok',                        
 			KALAN=case when @depo=0 then isnull((select sum(KALAN) from STOKDURUM SD where SD.STOKID=S.ID),0) else isnull((select sum(KALAN) from STOKDURUM SD where SD.STOKID=S.ID and SD.DEPOID=@Depo ),0) end,
 			FIYAT=isnull(SF.FIYAT,-1),         
 			KUR=isnull(SF.KUR,'TL'),
 			STOKMARKA=StokMarka.ANAHTAR ,        
 			STOKMODEL=StokModel.ANAHTAR ,        
 			KDV=S.KDV, S.OTVYUZDE,S.OTVMIKTAR,   
 			KDVDURUM=SF.KDVDURUM,                
 			PAKET=isnull(S.PAKET,0),             
 			IZLEME=isnull(S.IZLEME,0),           
 			BIRIM=isnull(S.ANABIRIM,51),
			BIRIMAD=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE bolum=-2702 AND DEGER=isnull(S.ANABIRIM,51)),
			STOKGRUBU=StokGrubu.ANAHTAR,
			S.GELIRID AS MASRAFID,
			S.OZELKOD,
			S.OZELKOD2,
			FIYAT_LISTE=Fiyatadi.ANAHTAR,
			DEPOADI=case when @depo=0 then 'Tüm Depolar Toplam' else isnull((select D.DEPOADI from DEPOLAR D where D.ID=@Depo ),0) end,
			S.URUNNO, 
			KATEGORI=(select TOP 1 KA.KOD from KATEGORI KA WHERE KA.ID=S.KATEGORI),
			KATEGORIAD=(select TOP 1 KA.AD from KATEGORI KA WHERE KA.ID=S.KATEGORI),
			DEPODETAYJSON=(
							SELECT 
								D.DEPOADI,
								SD.KALAN AS Kalan
							FROM STOKDURUM SD (nolock)
							INNER JOIN DEPOLAR D (nolock) ON D.ID = SD.DEPOID
							WHERE SD.STOKID = S.ID AND SD.KALAN > 0
							FOR JSON PATH, root('results')
						)
			From                                  
 			STOKLAR S (nolock) LEFT OUTER JOIN STOKFIYAT SF (nolock) on S.ID = SF.STOKID and SF.BIRIM = ANABIRIM and SF.FIYATADI=@Fiyatadi and SF.PAKETID=0 and SF.SATIS=case when @FiyatAdi=-2 then 0 else 1 end
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokMarka ON StokMarka.DEGER = S.MARKA AND StokMarka.DIL=-1 AND StokMarka.BOLUM =-2701             
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokModel ON StokModel.DEGER=S.MODEL AND StokModel.DIL=-1 AND StokModel.BOLUM=convert(int,'-2701'+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),S.MARKA))
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokGrubu ON S.GRUBU = StokGrubu.DEGER AND StokGrubu.DIL=-1 AND StokGrubu.BOLUM=-2704
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI Fiyatadi ON Fiyatadi.BOLUM = -1007 AND Fiyatadi.DEGER=SF.FIYATADI

			where 
			S.DURUM=1 and 
			S.INTERNET_SATIS=1 AND
			(
			CASE @AD
				WHEN 'STOKADI'  THEN S.STOKADI
				WHEN 'KOD'      THEN S.KOD 
				WHEN 'KATEGORI'      THEN (select TOP 1 KA.AD from KATEGORI KA WHERE KA.ID=S.KATEGORI)	
				
			END LIKE '%'+@KOD+'%'
			or
			CASE @AD WHEN 'KOD' THEN S.URUNNO END LIKE '%'+@KOD+'%'			 
			) AND
			@AD<>'BARKOD'


		UNION ALL
		select TOP 200  
 			S.ID,                                
 			S.KOD,                         
 			AD=STOKADI,                          
 			TUR='Stok',                        
 			KALAN=case when @depo=0 then isnull((select sum(KALAN) from STOKDURUM SD where SD.STOKID=S.ID),0) else isnull((select sum(KALAN) from STOKDURUM SD where SD.STOKID=S.ID and SD.DEPOID=@Depo ),0) end,
 			FIYAT=isnull(SF.FIYAT,-1),         
 			KUR=isnull(SF.KUR,'TL'),
 			STOKMARKA=StokMarka.ANAHTAR ,        
 			STOKMODEL=StokModel.ANAHTAR ,        
 			KDV=S.KDV, S.OTVYUZDE,S.OTVMIKTAR,   
 			KDVDURUM=SF.KDVDURUM,                
 			PAKET=isnull(S.PAKET,0),             
 			IZLEME=isnull(S.IZLEME,0),           
 			BIRIM=isnull(S.ANABIRIM,51),
			BIRIMAD=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE bolum=-2702 AND DEGER=isnull(S.ANABIRIM,51)),
			STOKGRUBU=StokGrubu.ANAHTAR,
			S.GELIRID AS MASRAFID,
			S.OZELKOD,
			S.OZELKOD2,
			FIYAT_LISTE=Fiyatadi.ANAHTAR,
			DEPOADI=case when @depo=0 then 'Tüm Depolar Toplam' else isnull((select D.DEPOADI from DEPOLAR D where D.ID=@Depo ),0) end,
			S.URUNNO, 
			KATEGORI=(select TOP 1 KA.KOD from KATEGORI KA WHERE KA.ID=S.KATEGORI),
			KATEGORIAD=(select TOP 1 KA.AD from KATEGORI KA WHERE KA.ID=S.KATEGORI),
			DEPODETAYJSON=(SELECT D.DEPOADI, SD.KALAN AS Kalan FROM STOKDURUM SD (nolock) INNER JOIN DEPOLAR D (nolock) ON D.ID = SD.DEPOID WHERE SD.STOKID = S.ID AND SD.KALAN > 0 FOR JSON PATH , root('results'))						 
			From                                  
 			STOKLAR S (nolock) LEFT OUTER JOIN STOKFIYAT SF (nolock) on S.ID = SF.STOKID and SF.BIRIM = ANABIRIM and SF.FIYATADI=@Fiyatadi and SF.PAKETID=0 and SF.SATIS=case when @FiyatAdi=-2 then 0 else 1 end
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokMarka ON StokMarka.DEGER = S.MARKA AND StokMarka.DIL=-1 AND StokMarka.BOLUM =-2701             
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokModel ON StokModel.DEGER=S.MODEL AND StokModel.DIL=-1 AND StokModel.BOLUM=convert(int,'-2701'+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),S.MARKA))
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokGrubu ON S.GRUBU = StokGrubu.DEGER AND StokGrubu.DIL=-1 AND StokGrubu.BOLUM=-2704
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI Fiyatadi ON Fiyatadi.BOLUM = -1007 AND Fiyatadi.DEGER=SF.FIYATADI

			where 
			S.DURUM=1 and 
			S.INTERNET_SATIS=1 AND
			CASE  @AD WHEN 'BARKOD' THEN  S.ID ELSE 1 END IN (SELECT STOKID FROM STOKBARKOD SB WHERE SB.BARKOD LIKE '%'+@KOD+'%')
			 
	) DD
	where ISNULL(KALAN,0)>0  		
 order by 2 

 END ELSE
 BEGIN
			SELECT *, KALANRESIM=CASE WHEN ISNULL(KALAN,0)>0 THEN 18 ELSE 19 END,

		ISKONTO=ISNULL((Select TOP 1 KC.MIKTAR from KAMPANYACARI KC inner join GENINI G on G.BOLUM=-2221 and DIL=-1 and G.DEGER=KC.KAMPANYAID where REHBERID=@RID AND DURUM=1 AND (KOD=(case KC.KAMPANYAID
			when -1 THEN (select S.KOD from STOKLAR S where S.ID=KC.URUNID)
			when -2 THEN (select K.KOD from KATEGORI K where K.ID=KC.URUNID)
			when -11 THEN (select M.KOD from MASRAFGELIR M where M.ID=KC.URUNID)
			when -12 THEN (select M.KOD from MASRAFGELIR M where M.ID=KC.URUNID)
		end) OR
		KATEGORI=(case KC.KAMPANYAID
			when -1 THEN (select S.KOD from STOKLAR S where S.ID=KC.URUNID)
			when -2 THEN (select K.KOD from KATEGORI K where K.ID=KC.URUNID)
			when -11 THEN (select M.KOD from MASRAFGELIR M where M.ID=KC.URUNID)
			when -12 THEN (select M.KOD from MASRAFGELIR M where M.ID=KC.URUNID)
		end) OR KAMPANYAID=-3 ) order by  KC.KAMPANYAID DESC),0) FROM (
			select TOP 200  
 			S.ID,                                
 			S.KOD,                         
 			AD=STOKADI,                          
 			TUR='Stok',                        
 			KALAN=isnull((select sum(KALAN) from STOKDURUM SD where SD.STOKID=S.ID and SD.DEPOID=@Depo ),0),
 			FIYAT=isnull(SF.FIYAT,-1),         
 			KUR=isnull(SF.KUR,'TL'),
 			STOKMARKA=StokMarka.ANAHTAR ,        
 			STOKMODEL=StokModel.ANAHTAR ,        
 			KDV=S.KDV, S.OTVYUZDE,S.OTVMIKTAR,   
 			KDVDURUM=SF.KDVDURUM,                
 			PAKET=isnull(S.PAKET,0),             
 			IZLEME=isnull(S.IZLEME,0),           
 			BIRIM=isnull(S.ANABIRIM,51),
			BIRIMAD=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE bolum=-2702 AND DEGER=isnull(S.ANABIRIM,51)),
			STOKGRUBU=StokGrubu.ANAHTAR,
			S.GELIRID AS MASRAFID,
			S.OZELKOD,
			S.OZELKOD2,
			FIYAT_LISTE=Fiyatadi.ANAHTAR,
			DEPOADI=isnull((select D.DEPOADI from DEPOLAR D where D.ID=@Depo ),0),
			S.URUNNO, 
			KATEGORI=(select TOP 1 KA.KOD from KATEGORI KA WHERE KA.ID=S.KATEGORI),
			KATEGORIAD=(select TOP 1 KA.AD from KATEGORI KA WHERE KA.ID=S.KATEGORI)	,
					DEPODETAYJSON=''
			From                                  
 			STOKLAR S (nolock) LEFT OUTER JOIN STOKFIYAT SF (nolock) on S.ID = SF.STOKID and SF.BIRIM = ANABIRIM and SF.FIYATADI=@Fiyatadi and SF.PAKETID=0 and SF.SATIS=case when @FiyatAdi=-2 then 0 else 1 end
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokMarka ON StokMarka.DEGER = S.MARKA AND StokMarka.DIL=-1 AND StokMarka.BOLUM =-2701             
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokModel ON StokModel.DEGER=S.MODEL AND StokModel.DIL=-1 AND StokModel.BOLUM=convert(int,'-2701'+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),S.MARKA))
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokGrubu ON S.GRUBU = StokGrubu.DEGER AND StokGrubu.DIL=-1 AND StokGrubu.BOLUM=-2704
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI Fiyatadi ON Fiyatadi.BOLUM = -1007 AND Fiyatadi.DEGER=SF.FIYATADI

			where 
			S.DURUM=1 and 
			S.INTERNET_SATIS=1 AND
			(
			CASE @AD
				WHEN 'STOKADI'  THEN S.STOKADI
				WHEN 'KOD'      THEN S.KOD 
				WHEN 'KATEGORI'      THEN (select TOP 1 KA.AD from KATEGORI KA WHERE KA.ID=S.KATEGORI)	
				
			END LIKE '%'+@KOD+'%'
			or
			CASE @AD WHEN 'KOD' THEN S.URUNNO END LIKE '%'+@KOD+'%'			 
			) AND
			@AD<>'BARKOD'


		UNION ALL
		select TOP 200  
 			S.ID,                                
 			S.KOD,                         
 			AD=STOKADI,                          
 			TUR='Stok',                        
 			KALAN=isnull((select sum(KALAN) from STOKDURUM SD where SD.STOKID=S.ID and SD.DEPOID=@Depo ),0),
 			FIYAT=isnull(SF.FIYAT,-1),         
 			KUR=isnull(SF.KUR,'TL'),
 			STOKMARKA=StokMarka.ANAHTAR ,        
 			STOKMODEL=StokModel.ANAHTAR ,        
 			KDV=S.KDV, S.OTVYUZDE,S.OTVMIKTAR,   
 			KDVDURUM=SF.KDVDURUM,                
 			PAKET=isnull(S.PAKET,0),             
 			IZLEME=isnull(S.IZLEME,0),           
 			BIRIM=isnull(S.ANABIRIM,51),
			BIRIMAD=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE bolum=-2702 AND DEGER=isnull(S.ANABIRIM,51)),
			STOKGRUBU=StokGrubu.ANAHTAR,
			S.GELIRID AS MASRAFID,
			S.OZELKOD,
			S.OZELKOD2,
			FIYAT_LISTE=Fiyatadi.ANAHTAR,
			DEPOADI=isnull((select D.DEPOADI from DEPOLAR D where D.ID=@Depo ),0),
			S.URUNNO, 
			KATEGORI=(select TOP 1 KA.KOD from KATEGORI KA WHERE KA.ID=S.KATEGORI),
			KATEGORIAD=(select TOP 1 KA.AD from KATEGORI KA WHERE KA.ID=S.KATEGORI)	,
					DEPODETAYJSON=''
			From                                  
 			STOKLAR S (nolock) LEFT OUTER JOIN STOKFIYAT SF (nolock) on S.ID = SF.STOKID and SF.BIRIM = ANABIRIM and SF.FIYATADI=@Fiyatadi and SF.PAKETID=0 and SF.SATIS=case when @FiyatAdi=-2 then 0 else 1 end
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokMarka ON StokMarka.DEGER = S.MARKA AND StokMarka.DIL=-1 AND StokMarka.BOLUM =-2701             
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokModel ON StokModel.DEGER=S.MODEL AND StokModel.DIL=-1 AND StokModel.BOLUM=convert(int,'-2701'+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),S.MARKA))
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokGrubu ON S.GRUBU = StokGrubu.DEGER AND StokGrubu.DIL=-1 AND StokGrubu.BOLUM=-2704
								LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI Fiyatadi ON Fiyatadi.BOLUM = -1007 AND Fiyatadi.DEGER=SF.FIYATADI

			where 
			S.DURUM=1 and 
			S.INTERNET_SATIS=1 AND
			CASE  @AD WHEN 'BARKOD' THEN  S.ID ELSE 1 END IN (SELECT STOKID FROM STOKBARKOD SB WHERE SB.BARKOD LIKE '%'+@KOD+'%')
			 
	) DD
	where 1=CASE WHEN @URUNID='F' THEN 
			CASE WHEN @BELGETUR=10 THEN 1 ELSE
		    CASE WHEN ISNULL(KALAN,0)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0  END END ELSE 1 END 			
 order by 2 

 END

end


===== PROC: TM_StokAraDetay =====



-- EXEC TM_StokAraDetay '437','' 


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_StokAraDetay](@URUNID VARCHAR(20), @REHBERID INT)
AS
BEGIN 
IF @REHBERID='0'
BEGIN
SELECT * FROM (
select  top 10  TIP='Alýþ',
				  FATURATARIH=FB.FATURATARIH,
				  BASLIK=(select R.FIRMA from REHBER R where R.ID=FB.REHBERID),
				  BIRIMTUTAR=ROUND(F.TUTAR/F.MIKTAR,2),
				  BIRIMTUTARDOVIZ=ROUND(F.DOVIZ_TUTARI/F.MIKTAR,2),
				  F.DOVIZ_KURU,
				  F.MIKTAR ,
				  RESIM=30
	from FATBASLIK FB inner join FATURA F on FB.ID=F.FATBASID					
	where
		FB.TUR IN ( 11,12) and		
		F.MIKTAR > 0 and
		F.URUNID = @URUNID
	order by FB.FATURATARIH desc) D1

UNION ALL
SELECT * FROM (select  top 10  TIP='Satýþ',
				FATURATARIH=FB.FATURATARIH,
				  BASLIK=(select R.FIRMA from REHBER R where R.ID=FB.REHBERID),
				  BIRIMTUTAR=ROUND(F.TUTAR/F.MIKTAR,2),
				  BIRIMTUTARDOVIZ=ROUND(F.DOVIZ_TUTARI/F.MIKTAR,2),
				  F.DOVIZ_KURU,
				  F.MIKTAR 	,
				  RESIM=31
	from FATBASLIK FB inner join FATURA F on FB.ID=F.FATBASID
	where
		FB.TUR IN (15,16) and		
		F.MIKTAR > 0 and
		F.URUNID = @URUNID
	order by FATURATARIH desc
	) D order by 2 desc
END ELSE
BEGIN 
		SELECT * FROM (
		select  top 10  TIP='Alýþ',
						  FATURATARIH=FB.FATURATARIH,
						  BASLIK=(select R.FIRMA from REHBER R where R.ID=FB.REHBERID),
						  BIRIMTUTAR=ROUND(F.TUTAR/F.MIKTAR,2),
						  BIRIMTUTARDOVIZ=ROUND(F.DOVIZ_TUTARI/F.MIKTAR,2),
						  F.DOVIZ_KURU,
						  F.MIKTAR ,
						  RESIM=30
			from FATBASLIK FB inner join FATURA F on FB.ID=F.FATBASID
							 INNER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID=FB.REHBERID AND R.ID=@REHBERID
			where
				FB.TUR IN ( 11,12) and		
				F.MIKTAR > 0 and
				F.URUNID = @URUNID
			order by FB.FATURATARIH desc) D1

		UNION ALL
		SELECT * FROM (select  top 10  TIP='Satýþ',
						FATURATARIH=FB.FATURATARIH,
						  BASLIK=(select R.FIRMA from REHBER R where R.ID=FB.REHBERID),
						  BIRIMTUTAR=ROUND(F.TUTAR/F.MIKTAR,2),
						  BIRIMTUTARDOVIZ=ROUND(F.DOVIZ_TUTARI/F.MIKTAR,2),
						  F.DOVIZ_KURU,
						  F.MIKTAR 	,
						  RESIM=31
			from FATBASLIK FB inner join FATURA F on FB.ID=F.FATBASID
								INNER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID=FB.REHBERID AND R.ID=@REHBERID
			where
				FB.TUR IN (15,16) and		
				F.MIKTAR > 0 and
				F.URUNID = @URUNID
			order by FATURATARIH desc
			) D order by 2 desc
END

end


===== PROC: TM_StokAraSayim =====



-- EXEC TM_StokAraSayim 'a','','',8,''


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_StokAraSayim](@AD VARCHAR(20), @KOD VARCHAR(20), @BARKOD VARCHAR(20), @DEPO INT, @KATEGORI varchar(50))
AS
BEGIN 
	declare @FiyatAdi int 
     	

	--set @FiyatAdi=(SELECT G.DEGER FROM REHBERBILGI RB INNER JOIN GENINI G ON BOLUM = -1007 AND ANAHTAR=BILGI WHERE RB.ETIKET='Fiyat Liste Adý' AND  RB.YER_ID=@REHBERID)			
	--set @FiyatAdi=(SELECT R.FIYAT FROM REHBER R WHERE ID=@REHBERID)				 
	set @FiyatAdi=10
 
	 


 select TOP 500  
 	S.ID,                                
 	S.KOD, S.URUNNO,                     
 	AD=STOKADI,                          
 	TUR='Stok',                        
 	KALAN=isnull((select sum(KALAN) from STOKDURUM SD where SD.STOKID=S.ID and SD.DEPOID=@Depo ),0),
 	FIYAT=isnull(SF.FIYAT,-1),         
 	KUR=isnull(SF.KUR,'TL'),
 	STOKMARKA=StokMarka.ANAHTAR ,        
 	STOKMODEL=StokModel.ANAHTAR ,        
 	KDV=S.KDV, S.OTVYUZDE,S.OTVMIKTAR,   
 	KDVDURUM=SF.KDVDURUM,                
 	PAKET=isnull(S.PAKET,0),             
 	IZLEME=isnull(S.IZLEME,0),           
 	BIRIM=isnull(S.ANABIRIM,51),
	STOKGRUBU=StokGrubu.ANAHTAR 
	,S.MASRAFID AS MASRAFID
	,S.OZELKOD 
 From                                  
 	STOKLAR S (nolock) LEFT OUTER JOIN STOKFIYAT SF (nolock) on S.ID = SF.STOKID and SF.BIRIM = ANABIRIM and SF.FIYATADI=@Fiyatadi and SF.PAKETID=0 and SF.SATIS=0
					   LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokMarka ON StokMarka.DEGER = S.MARKA AND StokMarka.DIL=-1 AND StokMarka.BOLUM =-2701
					   LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokModel ON StokModel.DEGER=S.MODEL AND StokModel.DIL=-1 AND StokModel.BOLUM=convert(int,'-2701'+CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),S.MARKA))
					   LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI StokGrubu ON S.GRUBU = StokGrubu.DEGER AND StokGrubu.DIL=-1 AND StokGrubu.BOLUM=-2704
					   LEFT OUTER JOIN KATEGORI KG ON KG.ID=S.KATEGORI
					   INNER JOIN STOKBARKOD STB ON S.ID = STB.STOKID AND S.ANABIRIM = STB.BARKODBIRIMI
where 
	S.DURUM=1
	and STOKADI LIKE '%'+@AD+'%'
	and S.KOD LIKE '%'+@KOD+'%'
	and ((STB.BARKOD like '%'+@BARKOD+'%' ) or ( @BARKOD like replace(replace(replace(STB.BARKOD,'O','_'),'P','_'),'Q','_')))
	and S.KULLANIM=1 
	and ISNULL(KG.AD,'') LIKE CASE WHEN ISNULL(@BARKOD,'')<>'' THEN '%' ELSE @KATEGORI+'%' END
	--and S.ID not in (select SSK.STOKID from STOKSAYIMKALEMLERI SSK where SSK.SAYIMID=3) 
 order by 2 
 end


===== PROC: TM_StokSayim =====

--EXEC [TM_StokSayim] '2024-01-01','2025-12-31'
--select * from yetký order by ID DESC
CREATE PROC [dbo].[TM_StokSayim](@BASTARIH DATETIME, @BITTARIH DATETIME) 
AS
begin
SELECT 
	S.ID,
	D.DEPOADI,
	DEPOID=D.ID,
	S.SAYIMTARIHI,
	SAYIMONAY,
	FIYATADI=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE DEGER=S.FIYATADI AND BOLUM=-1008),
	SAYIMYAPAN=(SELECT TOP 1 FIRMA FROM REHBER R1 WHERE R1.ID=S.SAYIMYAPAN)
FROM STOKSAYIM S INNER JOIN DEPOLAR D ON S.SAYIMDEPO=D.ID
WHERE
   SAYIMTARIHI BETWEEN @BASTARIH+' 00:00:00' AND @BITTARIH+' 23:59:00' and ISNULL(SAYIMONAY,0)<>1
ORDER BY SAYIMTARIHI DESC

end






===== PROC: TM_StokSayimKalemleri =====

--EXEC [TM_StokSayimKalemleri] 'Listele',3 ,'','','','','','','','','',8
--select * from yetký order by ID DESC
CREATE PROC [dbo].[TM_StokSayimKalemleri](@TIP VARCHAR(50), @SAYIMID INT, @STOKID INT, @SISTEMDEKIMIKTAR INT, @SAYIMMIKTAR INT, @EKLEYEN INT, @IZLEME INT, @SERINO VARCHAR(50), @LOTNO VARCHAR(50), @SKT DATE, @KALAN FLOAT, @DEPOID INT) 
AS
begin
	IF @TIP='Listele'
	begin
			SELECT DISTINCT
				S.ID,
				SAYIMID,
				S.STOKID,
				ST.URUNNO,
				SISTEMDEKIMIKTAR,
				SAYIMMIKTAR=SI.ADET,
				FARK=SISTEMDEKIMIKTAR-SAYIMMIKTAR,
				BIRIMFIYAT,
				TUTAR,
				EKLEYEN=ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 FIRMA FROM REHBER R1 WHERE R1.ID=S.EKLEYEN),''),
				S.EKLEMETARIHI,
				IZLEME=CASE WHEN S.IZLEME=0 THEN 'Yok'
							WHEN S.IZLEME=1 THEN 'Seri No'
							WHEN S.IZLEME=2 THEN 'Lot No'
							WHEN S.IZLEME=3 THEN 'SKT'
							WHEN S.IZLEME=4 THEN 'Karekod'
							WHEN S.IZLEME=5 THEN 'Lot No + SKT'
							WHEN S.IZLEME=6 THEN 'Seri No + Lot No' end,
				KUR,
				KOD,
				STOKADI,
				SERINO,
				LOTNO,
				SKT,
				SILRESIM=19

			FROM STOKSAYIMKALEMLERI S INNER JOIN STOKLAR ST ON S.STOKID=ST.ID
										LEFT OUTER JOIN STOKIZLEME SI ON  SI.STOKID=S.STOKID AND SI.BASLIKID=S.SAYIMID AND BELGETUR=99 AND S.ID=SI.SATIRID
										LEFT OUTER JOIN STOKSERILOT SL ON SL.ID=SI.SERILOTID
			WHERE
			   SAYIMID = @SAYIMID
	end
	IF @TIP='Giris'
	begin
		DECLARE @SAYIMKALEMLERIID INT, @STOKIZLEMEID INT, @STOKSERILOTID INT
		SET @STOKSERILOTID=0
		SET @SAYIMKALEMLERIID=0
		
		SELECT TOP 1 @SAYIMKALEMLERIID=ID FROM STOKSAYIMKALEMLERI S WHERE S.SAYIMID=@SAYIMID AND S.STOKID=@STOKID
		IF @SAYIMKALEMLERIID=0 
		BEGIN
		INSERT INTO STOKSAYIMKALEMLERI (SAYIMID,STOKID,SISTEMDEKIMIKTAR,SAYIMMIKTAR,EKLEYEN,IZLEME, BIRIMFIYAT, TUTAR, EKLEMETARIHI, DEGISTIRMETARIHI) VALUES (@SAYIMID, @STOKID, @SISTEMDEKIMIKTAR, @SAYIMMIKTAR, @EKLEYEN, @IZLEME,0,0, GETDATE(),GETDATE()) 
		SELECT @SAYIMKALEMLERIID=SCOPE_IDENTITY() 
		END ELSE UPDATE STOKSAYIMKALEMLERI SET SAYIMMIKTAR=SAYIMMIKTAR+@SAYIMMIKTAR WHERE ID=@SAYIMKALEMLERIID

		--delete from STOKIZLEME where STOKID=@STOKID and BASLIKID=@SAYIMID and SATIRID=@SAYIMKALEMLERIID
		
		select top 1 @STOKSERILOTID=ID from STOKSERILOT where STOKID=@STOKID and SERINO=@SERINO and LOTNO=@LOTNO AND SKT=@SKT --VARSA GETÝR YOKSA ALTTAKÝNÝ ÇALIÞTIR
		IF @STOKSERILOTID=0
		BEGIN
			INSERT INTO STOKSERILOT (STOKID,SERINO,LOTNO,URT,SKT) values(@STOKID,@SERINO,@LOTNO,'1990-01-01',@SKT) 
			select @STOKSERILOTID=scope_identity()
				print '1'
		END
		IF not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM STOKIZLEME WHERE STOKID=@STOKID AND BELGETUR=99 AND BASLIKID=@SAYIMID AND SERILOTID=@STOKSERILOTID AND  SATIRID=@SAYIMKALEMLERIID)
		begin
			insert into STOKIZLEME(STOKID,BELGETUR,BASLIKID,SATIRID,IZLEMTUR,KALAN,ADET, EKLEYEN, DONUSID, SERILOTID) values(@STOKID,99,@SAYIMID,@SAYIMKALEMLERIID, @IZLEME, @SAYIMMIKTAR , @SAYIMMIKTAR, @EKLEYEN, 0,@STOKSERILOTID) 
			select @STOKIZLEMEID=scope_identity()
		end else
		begin
		print '2'
				update STOKIZLEME SET KALAN=KALAN+@SAYIMMIKTAR, ADET=ADET+@SAYIMMIKTAR WHERE STOKID=@STOKID AND BELGETUR=99 AND BASLIKID=@SAYIMID AND SERILOTID=@STOKSERILOTID AND  SATIRID=@SAYIMKALEMLERIID
				SELECT @STOKIZLEMEID=ID FROM STOKIZLEME WHERE STOKID=@STOKID AND BELGETUR=99 AND BASLIKID=@SAYIMID AND SERILOTID=@STOKSERILOTID AND  SATIRID=@SAYIMKALEMLERIID
		end
		print '3'


		IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM STOKIZLEMEDEPO WHERE IZLEMID=@STOKIZLEMEID AND DEPOID=@DEPOID)
		BEGIN
			UPDATE STOKIZLEMEDEPO SET ADET=ADET+@SAYIMMIKTAR WHERE IZLEMID=@STOKIZLEMEID AND DEPOID=@DEPOID
			print '4'
		END ELSE
		BEGIN
			print '5'
			insert into STOKIZLEMEDEPO (IZLEMID, DEPOID, ADET) values(@STOKIZLEMEID,@DEPOID, @SAYIMMIKTAR)
			print '6'
		END

		
		EXEC [TM_StokSayimKalemleri] 'Listele',@SAYIMID ,'','','','','','','','','',@DEPOID
	end
	IF @TIP='Sil'
	begin
		DELETE FROM STOKSAYIMKALEMLERI WHERE ID=@IZLEME
		delete from STOKIZLEME where BELGETUR='99' and STOKID=@STOKID and BASLIKID=@SAYIMID and SATIRID=@IZLEME
		delete from STOKLOKASYON where DURUM=0 and BELGETUR='99' and BASLIKID=@SAYIMID and SATIRID=@IZLEME
		EXEC [TM_StokSayimKalemleri] 'Listele',@SAYIMID ,'','','','','','','','','',@DEPOID
	end
end
--SELECT * FROM STOKSERILOT WHERE STOKID=3981
--SELECT * FROM STOKIZLEME WHERE STOKID=3981 AND BASLIKID=3

===== PROC: TM_StokSeriLotListele =====



-- EXEC TM_StokSeriLotListele 'Listele','436','Anadepo','1319654','51465'
--exec [TM_StokSeriLotListele] 'Listele','136','SATICI A','1319887','51675'
 


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_StokSeriLotListele](@TIP VARCHAR(20), @STOKID INT, @DEPOID VARCHAR(50), @STOKSERILOTID VARCHAR(200), @SIPARISID INT)
AS
BEGIN 
 
	IF @TIP='Sil'
	begin
		DELETE from SI FROM STOKIZLEME SI INNER JOIN FATURA F ON F.FATBASID=SI.BASLIKID AND F.ID=SI.SATIRID WHERE F.ID=@STOKSERILOTID 
		DELETE from SI FROM STOKLOKASYON SI INNER JOIN FATURA F ON F.FATBASID=SI.BASLIKID AND F.ID=SI.SATIRID WHERE F.ID=@STOKSERILOTID 
		DELETE FROM FATURA WHERE ID=@STOKSERILOTID		
	end else
	BEGIN
		declare @DEPO INT, @BELGETUR INT
		SELECT @DEPO=ID FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.DEPOADI=@DEPOID
		SELECT @BELGETUR=TUR FROM FATBASLIK D WHERE D.ID=@SIPARISID
		IF @BELGETUR=10
		BEGIN
			select 
				SEC=ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1  'True' from STOKIZLEME SI WHERE SI.BELGETUR=@BELGETUR AND SI.IZLEMTUR='2' AND SI.STOKID=SD.STOKID AND SI.BASLIKID=@SIPARISID AND SL.ID=SI.SERILOTID AND SATIRID=@STOKSERILOTID),'False'),
				SD.STOKID,
				DEPOID,
				SD.SERILOTID,
				SD.KALAN,
				SERINO,
				LOTNO,
				URT,
				SKT ,
				MIKTAR=ISNULL((SELECT  top 1 ADET from STOKIZLEME SI WHERE SI.BELGETUR=@BELGETUR AND SI.IZLEMTUR='2' AND SI.STOKID=SD.STOKID AND SI.BASLIKID=@SIPARISID AND SL.ID=SI.SERILOTID AND SATIRID=@STOKSERILOTID),0) 
	 
			from STOKDURUMIZLEME SD INNER JOIN STOKSERILOT SL ON SD.SERILOTID=SL.ID --and SL.ID=@STOKSERILOTID
									--LEFT OUTER JOIN STOKIZLEME SI ON  SI.STOKID=SD.STOKID AND BELGETUR=@BELGETUR AND BASLIKID=@SIPARISID  
										 INNER JOIN STOKIZLEME SI ON SI.SERILOTID=SL.ID AND SI.STOKID=SL.STOKID AND SATIRID=@STOKSERILOTID
			WHERE 
				SD.STOKID=@STOKID AND 
				SD.KALAN>0 AND 
				DEPOID=@DEPO 
		
			ORDER BY SL.ID
		END ELSE
		IF @BELGETUR IN (14,20)
		BEGIN
			select 
				SEC=ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1  'True' from STOKIZLEME SI WHERE SI.BELGETUR=@BELGETUR AND SI.IZLEMTUR='2' AND SI.STOKID=SD.STOKID AND SI.BASLIKID=@SIPARISID AND SL.ID=SI.SERILOTID AND SATIRID=@STOKSERILOTID),'False'),
				SD.STOKID,
				DEPOID,
				SD.SERILOTID,
				SD.KALAN,
				SERINO,
				LOTNO,
				URT,
				SKT ,
				MIKTAR=ISNULL((SELECT  top 1 ADET from STOKIZLEME SI WHERE SI.BELGETUR=@BELGETUR AND SI.IZLEMTUR='2' AND SI.STOKID=SD.STOKID AND SI.BASLIKID=@SIPARISID AND SL.ID=SI.SERILOTID AND SATIRID=@STOKSERILOTID),0) 
	 
			from STOKDURUMIZLEME SD INNER JOIN STOKSERILOT SL ON SD.SERILOTID=SL.ID --and SL.ID=@STOKSERILOTID
									--LEFT OUTER JOIN STOKIZLEME SI ON  SI.STOKID=SD.STOKID AND BELGETUR=@BELGETUR AND BASLIKID=@SIPARISID  
										-- LEFT OUTER JOIN STOKIZLEME SI ON SI.SERILOTID=SL.ID AND SI.STOKID=SL.STOKID AND SATIRID=@STOKSERILOTID
			WHERE 
				SD.STOKID=@STOKID AND 
				SD.KALAN>0 AND 
				DEPOID=@DEPO 
		
			ORDER BY SL.ID
		END
	end

END
--SELECT * FROM STOKIZLEME ORDER BY ID DESC

===== PROC: TM_STOKTRANSFER =====
--exec [TM_STOKTRANSFER] '2026-01-01','2026-09-11','',1,-1,'3584'
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[TM_STOKTRANSFER]
    @BasTarihi VARCHAR(10),
    @BitTarihi VARCHAR(10),
    @ID INT,
    @TAMAMLANAN INT,
    @BAYIID INT,
    @KULLANICIKODU VARCHAR(20)
AS
BEGIN

    DECLARE @GORMEYETKISI INT, @SUBEID INT

    SET @GORMEYETKISI = 0
    SET @SUBEID = 0

    SELECT 
        @GORMEYETKISI = CASE WHEN ISNULL(RO.TY, 0) = 0 THEN BILGI ELSE '100' END,
        @SUBEID = R.SUBEID
    FROM REHBER R 
        INNER JOIN KULLANICI K ON K.REHBERID = R.ID 
        INNER JOIN YETKIEK YE ON YE.ROLID = K.ROLID AND R.ID = @KULLANICIKODU AND MODULID = '2201'
        LEFT OUTER JOIN ROLLER RO ON RO.ID = K.ROLID

    IF @BAYIID = -1
    BEGIN
        SELECT DISTINCT
            FB.ID,
			TARIH=CONVERT(DATE,TARIH,102),
			TARIHT=CONVERT(VARCHAR(5),TARIH,108),
            FATURATARIH=CONVERT(DATE,FATURATARIH,102),
			FATURATARIHT=CONVERT(VARCHAR(5),FATURATARIH,108),
            FATURANO     = FB.FATURANO,
			R3.FIRMA,
            FB.TUR,
            CIKISSUBE=FB.SUBEID,
            FB.CIKISDEPO,
            FB.GIRISDEPO,
            FB.GIRISSUBE,
            GIRISDEPOSU  = Giris.DEPOADI,
            CIKISDEPOSU  = Cikis.DEPOADI,
            TESLIMALAN   = R1.FIRMA,
            TESLIMEDEN   = R2.FIRMA,
			TESLIMALANID=REHBERID,
			TESLIMEDENID=SATICIKODU,
            FB.OZELKOD,
            FB.YETKIKODU,
            FB.ACIKLAMA,
            KAYNAK       = CASE 
                             WHEN EXISTS (
                                 SELECT F2.ID FROM SIPARISDETAY F2 
                                 WHERE F2.ID IN (
                                     SELECT F1.YERID FROM FATURA F1 
                                     WHERE F1.YERI = 435 AND F1.FATBASID = FB.ID
                                 )
                             ) THEN 'Talepten' 
                           END,
						   ONAY=ISNULL(ONAY,0),
			DETAYVAR=ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM FATURA F WHERE F.FATBASID=FB.ID),0)
        FROM FATBASLIK FB (NOLOCK)
            INNER JOIN DEPOLAR Giris ON Giris.ID = FB.GIRISDEPO
            INNER JOIN DEPOLAR Cikis ON Cikis.ID = FB.CIKISDEPO
            LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R1 ON FB.REHBERID = R1.ID
            LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R2 ON FB.SATICIKODU = R2.ID
			LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R3 ON FB.SUBEID = R3.ID
        WHERE
            (
                (
                    FB.TUR = 20 AND
                    CONVERT(DATE, FB.TARIH, 102) BETWEEN CONVERT(DATE, @BasTarihi, 102) AND CONVERT(DATE, @BitTarihi, 102)
                )
                OR FB.ID = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ID, '') <> '' THEN @ID ELSE 0 END
            )
			         --AND ISNULL(FB.ONAY, 0) > CASE WHEN @TAMAMLANAN = 1 THEN 0 ELSE -1 END
            --AND ISNULL(FB.SATICIKODU, '') LIKE CASE WHEN @GORMEYETKISI = 1 THEN @KULLANICIKODU ELSE '%' END
            --AND ISNULL(R1.SUBEID, -1)     LIKE CASE WHEN @GORMEYETKISI = 10 THEN @SUBEID ELSE -1 END
        ORDER BY 2 DESC, 3 DESC
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT DISTINCT
            FB.ID,
			FB.TARIH,
            FATURATARIH=CONVERT(DATE,FATURATARIH,102),
			FATURATARIHT=CONVERT(VARCHAR(5),FATURATARIH,108),
            FATURANO     = FB.FATURANO,
			R3.FIRMA,
            FB.TUR,
            CIKISSUBE=FB.SUBEID,
            FB.CIKISDEPO,
            FB.GIRISDEPO,
            FB.GIRISSUBE,
            GIRISDEPOSU  = Giris.DEPOADI,
            CIKISDEPOSU  = Cikis.DEPOADI,
            TESLIMALAN   = R1.FIRMA,
            TESLIMEDEN   = R2.FIRMA,
			TESLIMALANID=REHBERID,
			TESLIMEDENID=SATICIKODU,
            FB.OZELKOD,
            FB.YETKIKODU,
            FB.ACIKLAMA,
            KAYNAK       = CASE 
                             WHEN EXISTS (
                                 SELECT F2.ID FROM SIPARISDETAY F2 
                                 WHERE F2.ID IN (
                                     SELECT F1.YERID FROM FATURA F1 
                                     WHERE F1.YERI = 435 AND F1.FATBASID = FB.ID
                                 )
                             ) THEN 'Talepten' 
                           END,
						   ONAY=ISNULL(ONAY,0),
			DETAYVAR=ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 1 FROM FATURA F WHERE F.FATBASID=FB.ID),0)
        FROM FATBASLIK FB (NOLOCK)
            INNER JOIN DEPOLAR Giris ON Giris.ID = FB.GIRISDEPO
            INNER JOIN DEPOLAR Cikis ON Cikis.ID = FB.CIKISDEPO
            LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R1 ON FB.REHBERID = R1.ID
            LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R2 ON FB.SATICIKODU = R2.ID
			LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R3 ON FB.SUBEID = R3.ID
        WHERE
            R1.ID = @BAYIID AND
            (
                (
                    FB.TUR = 20 AND
                    CONVERT(DATE, FB.TARIH, 102) BETWEEN CONVERT(DATE, @BasTarihi, 102) AND CONVERT(DATE, @BitTarihi, 102)
                )
                OR FB.ID = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ID, '') <> '' THEN @ID ELSE 0 END
            )
            --AND ISNULL(FB.ONAY, 0) > CASE WHEN @TAMAMLANAN = 1 THEN 0 ELSE -1 END
            --AND ISNULL(FB.SATICIKODU, '') LIKE CASE WHEN @GORMEYETKISI = 1 THEN @KULLANICIKODU ELSE '%' END
            --AND ISNULL(R1.SUBEID, -1)     LIKE CASE WHEN @GORMEYETKISI = 10 THEN @SUBEID ELSE -1 END
       ORDER BY 2 DESC, 3 DESC
    END

END

===== PROC: TM_STOKTRANSFERGuncelle =====

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[TM_STOKTRANSFERGuncelle]
	@TUR VARCHAR(20),
    @ID INT,
    @TARIH DATETIME,        
    @REHBERID INT,        -- TESLIMALAN (R1 = FB.REHBERID)
    @SATICIKODU INT,      -- TESLIMEDEN (R2 = FB.SATICIKODU)
    @CIKISDEPO VARCHAR(50),
    @GIRISDEPO VARCHAR(50),        
    @ACIKLAMA NVARCHAR(100),       
    @DURUM VARCHAR(50),
    @EKLEYEN VARCHAR(20) ,
	@BAYIID INT
AS
BEGIN

    DECLARE @YENIID INT

    IF @TUR <> 'Sil'
    BEGIN
        BEGIN TRY

            IF @ID IN (SELECT ID FROM FATBASLIK WHERE ID = @ID)
            BEGIN
                UPDATE S SET
                    TARIH          = @TARIH,
					FATURATARIH    = @TARIH,
                    TUR            = 20,
                    REHBERID       = @REHBERID,
                    SATICIKODU     = @SATICIKODU,
                    CIKISDEPO      = (SELECT ID FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.DEPOADI = @CIKISDEPO),
                    GIRISDEPO      = (SELECT ID FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.DEPOADI = @GIRISDEPO),
                    ACIKLAMA       = @ACIKLAMA,
                    DURUM          = 1,
                    DEGISTIREN        = @EKLEYEN,
					DEGISTIRMETARIHI=GETDATE(),
					ONAY=@DURUM
                FROM FATBASLIK S
                WHERE S.ID = @ID

                EXEC DBO.TM_STOKTRANSFER '1900-01-01', '1900-01-01', @ID, 1, @BAYIID, @EKLEYEN
            END
            ELSE
            BEGIN
                DECLARE @GelenBelgeNo NVARCHAR(50), @an DATETIME, @TUR01 VARCHAR(10)
                SET @an = GETDATE()
                SET @TUR01 = '2001'  -- TUR=20 için belge tipi

                DECLARE @TempTable TABLE (KocanNo NVARCHAR(50), BelgeSeri NVARCHAR(50), BelgeNo NVARCHAR(50))
                INSERT INTO @TempTable (KocanNo, BelgeSeri, BelgeNo)
                EXEC [dbo].[sp_BelgeNoGetir] 20, -1, @TUR01, @an
                SELECT TOP 1 @GelenBelgeNo = BelgeNo FROM @TempTable

                INSERT INTO FATBASLIK (
                    TARIH, 
					TUR, 
					REHBERID, 
					SATICIKODU,
                    KOCANNO,
                    FATURANO,
                    CIKISDEPO,
                    GIRISDEPO,
                    ACIKLAMA,
                    DURUM,
                    EKLEYEN,
					EKLEMETARIHI,
                    GIRISKAYNAK,
                    SANAL, 
					TIPI, 
					PROJEID, 
					AKTIVITEID,
					FATURATARIH,
					ONAY, 
					R, 
					KUR
                )
                SELECT 
                    @TARIH, 
					TUR=20, 
					@REHBERID, 
					@SATICIKODU,
                    KOCANNO=(SELECT TOP 1 KOCANNO FROM KOCANAYARLARI WHERE TUR = 20 AND SUBEID = -1),
                    FATURANO=@GelenBelgeNo,
                    CIKISDEPO=(SELECT ID FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.DEPOADI = @CIKISDEPO),
                    GIRISDEPO=(SELECT ID FROM DEPOLAR D WHERE D.DEPOADI = @GIRISDEPO),
                    @ACIKLAMA,
                    DURUM=1,
                    @EKLEYEN,
					GETDATE(),
                    1,
                    1,
					TIPI=1,
					PROJEID=-1,
					AKTIVITEID=-1,
					FATURATARIH=@TARIH,
					@DURUM, 
					R=0,
					KUR='TL'
                

                SET @YENIID = @@IDENTITY
                EXEC DBO.TM_STOKTRANSFER '1900-01-01', '1900-01-01', @YENIID, 1, @BAYIID, @EKLEYEN
            END

        END TRY
        BEGIN CATCH
            SELECT 'Hata oluþtu....' + ERROR_MESSAGE()
        END CATCH
    END

    IF @TUR = 'Sil'
    BEGIN
        DELETE FROM FATURA WHERE FATBASID = @ID
        DELETE FROM FATBASLIK WHERE ID = @ID
    END

END


===== PROC: TM_UretimEkAlanOzellikler =====

--  exec [TM_UretimEkAlanOzellikler] 'FaturaWizardDlg'


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_UretimEkAlanOzellikler](@KONUM VARCHAR(50))
AS
BEGIN 

Select 
	*,
	ICERIK=ISNULL((select 
				STRING_AGG(G2.ANAHTAR,CHAR(13)) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY G2.ANAHTAR) 
			from GENINI G1 INNER JOIN GENINI G2 ON G1.DEGER=G2.BOLUM 
			where G1.BOLUM='0' and G1.ANAHTAR=ALANLAR.ALANADI	AND G2.DIL=-1 ) ,'')
from ALANLAR Where EKRANADI IN ('UretimEmriWizardDlg','IsEmriPersonelZamanDlg','FaturaWizardDlg')  AND KONUM=@KONUM AND TUR IN ('3','4','1','11','7')

end

--select ID,FIRMA FROM REHBER WHERE GRUP IN (120) and FIRMA like '%<ara>%'

===== PROC: TM_UretimFason =====

--SELECT * FROM GOREVYORUM

-- exec [TM_UretimFason] '',25413,'','','','','','','','','','','','ListeleUel'
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_UretimFason](@URETIMEMRIID int,
	@URETIMOPERASYONID int,
	@TARIH datetime,
	@OLAY VARCHAR(10),
	@REHBERID int,
	@GIREN float,
	@CIKAN float,
	@BIRIM VARCHAR(50),
	@EKLEYEN smallint,
	@TUR VARCHAR(50),
	@BELGENO varchar(30),
	@ACIKLAMA VARCHAR(150),
	@ID int,
	@TIP VARCHAR(50),
	@FASONTIPI VARCHAR(50))
AS
BEGIN

IF @TIP='ListeleUel'
begin
	SELECT
		UF.ID,
		URETIMEMRIID,
		URETIMOPERASYONID,
		TARIH,
		OLAY=CASE WHEN OLAY= 0 THEN 'Çýkýþ' ELSE 'Giriþ' end,
		REHBERID,
		FIRMA=ISNULL(R.FIRMA,''),
		TUR=CASE WHEN ISNULL(TUR,0) = 1 THEN 'Ýrsaliye' WHEN ISNULL(TUR,0) = 2 THEN 'Diðer' end,
		BELGENO,
		ACIKLAMA,
		GIREN,
		CIKAN,
		BIRIM=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2702 AND DEGER=UF.BIRIM),
		EKLEYEN=(SELECT TOP 1 R.FIRMA FROM REHBER R WHERE R.ID = UF.EKLEYEN),
		FASONTIPI=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2799 AND DEGER=UF.FASONTIPI)
	FROM URETIMOPERASYONFASON UF LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID=UF.REHBERID
	WHERE
	UF.URETIMOPERASYONID=@URETIMOPERASYONID
	--URETIMEMRIID=@URETIMEMRIID
	order by TARIH, UF.ID
end







IF @TIP='GuncelleUel'
begin
--SELECT * FROM MOBILLOG DELETE FROM MOBILLOG 
--INSERT INTO MOBILLOG(ACIKLAMA) VALUES(@TARIH)
	if @ID=0 
	begin
		INSERT INTO URETIMOPERASYONFASON (URETIMEMRIID,URETIMOPERASYONID,TARIH,OLAY,REHBERID,TUR,BELGENO,ACIKLAMA,GIREN,CIKAN,BIRIM,EKLEYEN, FASONTIPI) 
		VALUES( 
			@URETIMEMRIID,
			@URETIMOPERASYONID,
			@TARIH,
			CASE WHEN @OLAY='Çýkýþ' THEN 0 ELSE 1 end,
			@REHBERID,
			@TUR,
			@BELGENO,
			@ACIKLAMA,
			@GIREN,
			@CIKAN,
			(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2702 AND ANAHTAR=@BIRIM),
			@EKLEYEN,
			(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2799 AND ANAHTAR=@FASONTIPI))
	end ELSE
	begin
		UPDATE URETIMOPERASYONFASON SET 
		TARIH=@TARIH,
		OLAY=CASE WHEN @OLAY='Çýkýþ' THEN 0 ELSE 1 end,
		REHBERID=@REHBERID,
		TUR=@TUR,
		BELGENO=@BELGENO,
		ACIKLAMA=@ACIKLAMA,
		GIREN=@GIREN,
		CIKAN=@CIKAN,
		BIRIM=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2702 AND ANAHTAR=@BIRIM),
		EKLEYEN=@EKLEYEN,
		FASONTIPI=(SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2799 AND ANAHTAR=@FASONTIPI)
		WHERE ID=@ID
	end
	exec TM_UretimFason '',@URETIMOPERASYONID,'','','','','','','','','','','','ListeleUel',''
end


end
-- exec [TM_UretimFason] '23833',25407,'2024-04-17','','','','','','','','','','24','GuncelleUel'

--select * from URETIMOPERASYONFASON WHERE URETIMOPERASYONID=25413

===== PROC: TM_UretimImajListele =====




--select * from DOKUMAN ORDER BY ID DESC
--SELECT * FROM IMAJ WHERE YER_ID=15309

-- exec [TM_UretimImajListele] 34815
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_UretimImajListele](@ID INT)
AS
BEGIN
	SELECT TUR='Resim',I.ID,I.BELGEADI FROM URETIMOPERASYON T0 INNER JOIN STOKLAR T2 ON T2.ID = T0.STOKID
										INNER JOIN IMAJ I ON T2.ID=I.YER_ID AND I.YERI=88
	WHERE T0.ID=@ID
	UNION ALL
	SELECT TUR='Döküman',I.ID,D.AD FROM URETIMOPERASYON T0 INNER JOIN STOKLAR T2 ON T2.ID = T0.STOKID
										INNER JOIN GOREVYORUM GI ON GI.GOREVID=T2.ID INNER JOIN DOKUMAN D ON D.MODULID=GI.ID AND D.KLASOR=-88 INNER JOIN IMAJ I ON I.YER_ID=D.ID and I.YERI=1 AND GI.TUR=88
	WHERE T0.ID=@ID
end





===== PROC: TM_UretimOlcum =====





--select * from URETIMEMRI where ID=20843
--SELECT * FROM URETIMOPERASYON where URETIMEMRIID=20843
--select * from URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL where OPERASYONID=21968
--SELECT * FROM URETIMOLCUM order by 1 DESC

 -- exec [TM_UretimOlcum] 0,105425,'Guncelle','2023-12-21 07:00:00.000','1 nci Numune','0','0','0','0','Adet','','2','Bekliyor'
	--exec [TM_UretimOlcum] 0,105425,'Listele'
	--exec [TM_UretimOlcum] 0,105413,'Listele'
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_UretimOlcum](@ID INT,@OPID INT, @TIP VARCHAR(50),@TARIH DATETIME=NULL, @KONUSU VARCHAR(100)=NULL, @PERSONEL VARCHAR(100)=NULL, @LOKASYON VARCHAR(100)=NULL, @KAYNAK VARCHAR(100)=NULL, @ADET FLOAT=NULL, @BIRIM VARCHAR(100)=NULL, @ACIKLAMA VARCHAR(120)=NULL, @EKLEYEN VARCHAR(100)=NULL, @DURUM VARCHAR(30)=NULL


)
AS
BEGIN 
declare @URETIMOLCUMID INT,
		@YERID INT,
		@DEGER INT


BEGIN TRY
if @TIP='Listele'
begin
		SELECT 
			UO.ID,
			OPERASYONID,
			OPERASYONPERSONELID,
			TARIH,
			SAAT=CONVERT(VARCHAR(5),TARIH,108),
			KONUSU,
			PERSONEL,
			SORUMLUADI=(select R.FIRMA from REHBER R where UO.EKLEYEN=R.ID),
			LOKASYON,
			LOKASYONADI=(select L1.ACIKLAMA from LOKASYON L1 where UO.LOKASYON=L1.ID),
			KAYNAK,
			KAYNAKADI=(select L1.ACIKLAMA from LOKASYON L1 where UO.KAYNAK=L1.ID),
			ADET,
			BIRIM=BRM.ACIKLAMA,
			UO.ACIKLAMA,
			EKLEYEN,
			EKLEMETARIHI,
			DURUM= CASE WHEN  ISNULL(UO.DURUM,0)=0 THEN 'Bekliyor' 
				WHEN  ISNULL(UO.DURUM,0)=1 THEN 'Çalýþýlýyor' 
				WHEN  ISNULL(UO.DURUM,0)=9 THEN 'Tamamlandý' END,
				DURUMINT=DURUM,
			ALARM = (select
						case when exists (select ALARM from URETIMOLCUMDETAY UOD where URETIMOLCUMID=UO.ID and isnull(ALARM,0)=0 )  then 2
						 when exists (select ALARM from URETIMOLCUMDETAY UOD where URETIMOLCUMID=UO.ID and ALARM=2 )  then 4
						else 3 end)
		FROM URETIMOLCUM UO LEFT OUTER JOIN (SELECT FILTRE='BIRIM', ROOTKOD='', KOD=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),DEGER), ID=SIRA, ACIKLAMA=ANAHTAR, TUR='00', REHBERID=-1 FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2702) BRM ON BRM.KOD=UO.BIRIM
		WHERE 
			OPERASYONPERSONELID=@OPID
		ORDER BY 4 , 3 DESC
end
if @TIP='Guncelle'
begin
	IF ISNULL(@ID,0)<>0
	BEGIN
		UPDATE 
			URETIMOLCUM
		SET 
			TARIH=		@TARIH,
			KONUSU=		@KONUSU,
			PERSONEL=	@PERSONEL,
			LOKASYON=	@LOKASYON,
			KAYNAK=		@KAYNAK,
			ADET=		@ADET,
			BIRIM=		(SELECT top 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2702 AND ANAHTAR=@BIRIM),
			ACIKLAMA=	@ACIKLAMA,
			EKLEYEN=	@EKLEYEN,
			DURUM=		CASE --WHEN ISNULL(@BitTar,'1900-01-01')<>'' or ISNULL(@BitTarT,'00:00')<>'' THEN 9 
								WHEN  ISNULL(@durum,0)='Bekliyor' THEN  '0'
								WHEN  ISNULL(@durum,0)='Çalýþýlýyor'  THEN '1'
								WHEN  ISNULL(@durum,0)='Tamamlandý' THEN '9' else '8' END  
		WHERE 
			ID=@ID

	END ELSE
	BEGIN
		INSERT INTO URETIMOLCUM (OPERASYONID,OPERASYONPERSONELID,TARIH,KONUSU,PERSONEL,LOKASYON,KAYNAK,ADET,BIRIM,ACIKLAMA,EKLEYEN,DURUM) 
		VALUES (
			(SELECT OPERASYONID FROM URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL WHERE ID=@OPID),
			@OPID,
			@TARIH,
			@KONUSU,
			@PERSONEL,
			@LOKASYON,
			@KAYNAK,
			@ADET,
			(SELECT top 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2702 AND ANAHTAR=@BIRIM),
			@ACIKLAMA,
			@EKLEYEN,
			CASE --WHEN ISNULL(@BitTar,'1900-01-01')<>'' or ISNULL(@BitTarT,'00:00')<>'' THEN 9 
								WHEN  ISNULL(@durum,0)='Bekliyor' THEN  '0'
								WHEN  ISNULL(@durum,0)='Çalýþýlýyor'  THEN '1'
								WHEN  ISNULL(@durum,0)='Tamamlandý' THEN '9' else '8' END)

		SET @URETIMOLCUMID = (SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY())
		PRINT @URETIMOLCUMID

 	 SET @YERID = (SELECT YERID FROM URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL WHERE ID = @OPID)

	SET @DEGER = 10

		INSERT INTO URETIMOLCUMDETAY(URETIMOLCUMID, KALITESABLONDETAYID,OLCUALETI,DEGERI,SAPMA,EKLEYEN,ALARM) 

		SELECT 
			URETIMOLCUMID=@URETIMOLCUMID, 
			KALITESABLONDETAYID=KSD.ID, 
			OLCUALETI=KSD.OLCUALETI,
			DEGERI=CASE WHEN KSD.GIRIS=13 THEN NULL ELSE '' END,
			SAPMA= CASE WHEN KSD.GIRIS=13 THEN (0-CONVERT(FLOAT,KSD.NOMINAL)) ELSE '' END,
			@EKLEYEN,
			ALARM= --null
			CASE WHEN KSD.GIRIS=13 THEN
				  CASE WHEN @DEGER IS NULL THEN 0 
					   WHEN KSD.LIMITALT <> '0' AND KSD.LIMITUST <> '0' AND @DEGER BETWEEN CONVERT(FLOAT,KSD.NOMINAL)-CONVERT(FLOAT,KSD.LIMITALT) AND CONVERT(FLOAT,KSD.NOMINAL)-CONVERT(FLOAT,KSD.LIMITUST) THEN 1 
					   WHEN KSD.LIMITUST = '0' AND @DEGER BETWEEN CONVERT(FLOAT,KSD.NOMINAL)-CONVERT(FLOAT,KSD.LIMITALT) AND KSD.NOMINAL THEN 1 
					   WHEN KSD.LIMITALT = '0' AND @DEGER BETWEEN CONVERT(FLOAT,KSD.NOMINAL) AND CONVERT(FLOAT,KSD.NOMINAL)+CONVERT(FLOAT,LIMITUST) THEN 1 ELSE 2 END 
					ELSE NULL END
		FROM KALITESABLONDETAY KSD WHERE YER=20 AND KSD.YERID=@YERID ORDER BY ID




	END
	EXEC TM_UretimOlcum 0 ,@OPID ,'Listele',@TARIH , @KONUSU, @PERSONEL, @LOKASYON, @KAYNAK, @ADET, @BIRIM, @ACIKLAMA, @EKLEYEN, @DURUM		
end
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
		SELECT 'Hata oluþtu....'+ERROR_MESSAGE()--+' - '+ISNULL(convert(datetime,(substring(@BasTar,7,4)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,4,2)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,1,2) +' '+@BasTarT)),'')
END CATCH
end











===== PROC: TM_UretimOlcumDetay =====





--select * from URETIMEMRI where ID=23851
--SELECT * FROM URETIMOPERASYON where URETIMEMRIID=23851
--select * from URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL where OPERASYONID=25426
--SELECT * FROM URETIMOLCUM WHERE OPERASYONPERSONELID=176332
--SELECT * FROM URETIMOLCUMDETAY WHERE URETIMOLCUMID=580
--  exec [TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaDetay] 
-- EXEC TM_UretimOlcumDetay 0,632,'Listele','0'
 
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_UretimOlcumDetay](@ID INT, @OLCUMID INT,@TIP VARCHAR(50), @JSON nVARCHAR(max))
AS
BEGIN 
IF @TIP='Listele'
begin

	IF OBJECT_ID('tempdb..#URETIMDETAY') IS NOT NULL
	BEGIN
		DROP TABLE #URETIMDETAY
	END


	SELECT  
		KT.ADI,
		UOD.ID,
		KALITESABLONDETAYID,
		UOD.OLCUALETI,
		OLCUALETIADI=(SELECT ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE  DEGER=UOD.OLCUALETI and BOLUM = -32052),
		DEGERI,
		SAPMA,
		UOD.EKLEYEN,
		UOD.EKLEMETARIHI,
		ALARM=case WHEN ISNULL(ALARM,0)=2 THEN 4 WHEN ISNULL(ALARM,0)=1 THEN 3 ELSE 2 END,
		URETIMOLCUMID ,
		BIRIM=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2702 AND DEGER=KSD.BIRIM),
		KSD.TOLERANSTIPI, 
		KSD.TOLERANSDEGERI,
		NOMINAL=KSD.NOMINAL,--ROUND(KSD.NOMINAL,4),
		KSD.LIMITALT,
		KSD.LIMITUST,
		TOLERANS = case when isnull(GIRIS,2)=13 THEN 
           case when CONVERT(DECIMAL(10,4),isnull(KSD.LIMITALT,0.0))>=0.0 then '-'+cast(KSD.LIMITALT as varchar(20)) else '' end + ' '+
           case when CONVERT(DECIMAL(10,4),isnull(KSD.LIMITUST,0.0))>=0.0 then '+'+cast(KSD.LIMITUST as varchar(20)) else '' end 	   
	 ELSE '' END,
		KSD.GIRIS,
		KSD.KAYNAK,
		KAYNAKSONUC=convert(varchar(2000),'')
	INTO #URETIMDETAY
	FROM URETIMOLCUMDETAY  UOD
             inner join KALITESABLONDETAY KSD on KSD.ID=UOD.KALITESABLONDETAYID
             inner join KALITETEST KT on KT.ID = KSD.TESTID
	WHERE 
		URETIMOLCUMID=@OLCUMID
							-----------------------------kaynak sütunundaki sqlleri çalýþtýrýp sonuçkaynak alanýnda deðerleri döndürüyoruz---------------------------------------
								DECLARE @sqlQuery NVARCHAR(MAX)
								DECLARE @result NVARCHAR(MAX)
								DECLARE @id INT
								DECLARE @tempTable TABLE (Result NVARCHAR(MAX))

								-- Cursor kullanarak her satýrdaki KAYNAK sütunundaki SQL'i çalýþtýr
								DECLARE cursor_sql CURSOR FOR
								SELECT ID, KAYNAK FROM #URETIMDETAY WHERE KAYNAK IS NOT NULL

								OPEN cursor_sql
								FETCH NEXT FROM cursor_sql INTO @id, @sqlQuery

								WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
								BEGIN
									-- Geçici tabloyu temizle
									DELETE FROM @tempTable

									-- Dinamik SQL'i çalýþtýr ve sonuçlarý geçici tabloya ekle
									INSERT INTO @tempTable
									EXEC sp_executesql @sqlQuery

									-- Sonuçlarý satýr sonu (Enter) ile ayýrarak birleþtir
									SET @result = (SELECT STRING_AGG(Result, CHAR(13) + CHAR(10)) FROM @tempTable)

									-- Sonucu KAYNAKSONUC sütununa güncelle
									UPDATE #URETIMDETAY
									SET KAYNAKSONUC = @result
									WHERE ID = @id

									FETCH NEXT FROM cursor_sql INTO @id, @sqlQuery
								END

								CLOSE cursor_sql
								DEALLOCATE cursor_sql
							-----------------------------kaynak sütunundaki sqlleri çalýþtýrýp sonuçkaynak alanýnda deðerleri döndürüyoruz---------------------------------------
	SELECT * FROM #URETIMDETAY
	ORDER BY 1 , 3 DESC
end 
if @TIP='Guncelle'
begin
BEGIN TRY
		UPDATE UOD SET 
			UOD.DEGERI=D.DEGERI,--CASE WHEN D.GIRIS=13 and ISNULL(D.DEGERI,'')='' THEN NULL ELSE D.DEGERI END, 
			UOD.OLCUALETI=(SELECT DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE  ANAHTAR=D.OLCUALETI and BOLUM = -32052),
			SAPMA=CASE WHEN D.GIRIS=13 AND ISNULL(D.DEGERI,'')<>'' THEN CONVERT(FLOAT,D.DEGERI)-CONVERT(FLOAT,D.NOMINAL) ELSE '' END,
			ALARM=CASE WHEN D.GIRIS=13 AND ISNULL(D.DEGERI,'')<>'' THEN 
							CASE WHEN (CONVERT(DECIMAL(10,4),D.DEGERI)-CONVERT(DECIMAL(10,4),D.NOMINAL) >= (CONVERT(DECIMAL(10,4),LIMITALT)*(-1))  AND CONVERT(DECIMAL(10,4),D.DEGERI)-CONVERT(DECIMAL(10,4),D.NOMINAL) <= CONVERT(DECIMAL(10,4),LIMITUST)) AND (D.DEGERI IS NOT NULL) THEN 1 WHEN ISNULL(D.DEGERI,'')=''  THEN NULL ELSE 2 END
					ELSE case WHEN D.DEGERI=D.NOMINAL THEN 1 ELSE 2 END END
		FROM 
		--SELECT * FROM 
								OPENJSON(@json,'$."FDMemOlcumDetay"') 
															WITH (
																ID VARCHAR(15) '$.ID',
																DEGERI VARCHAR(40) '$.DEGERI',
																OLCUALETI VARCHAR(50) '$.OLCUALETIADI',
																NOMINAL VARCHAR(40) '$.NOMINAL',
																LIMITALT VARCHAR(40) '$.LIMITALT',
																LIMITUST VARCHAR(40) '$.LIMITUST',
																GIRIS FLOAT '$.GIRIS'
							) D INNER JOIN URETIMOLCUMDETAY UOD ON UOD.ID=D.ID --where UOD.ID=@ID
	select DURUM=1,ACIKLAMA='Baþarýyla Kaydedildi.'	
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
select DURUM=0,ACIKLAMA='Hata oluþtu. Transfer gerçekleþmedi. ' 	+ ERROR_MESSAGE()
END CATCH
end
end


===== PROC: TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanla =====








--  exec [TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanla] '2024-05-01', '2024-05-28',''


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanla](@BasTarihi VARCHAR(10), @BitTarihi VARCHAR(10), @kalmayan int, @EMIRNO VARCHAR(50)='')
AS
BEGIN 

DECLARE @SQL VARCHAR(MAX)

IF @EMIRNO<>''
BEGIN
	SET @BasTarihi='1900-01-01'
	SET @BÝTTarihi='2099-01-01'
END

--if CHARINDEX('.',@BasTarihi,1)=3

--set @BasTarihi=case when substring(@BasTarihi,1,2) like '%.%' or substring(@BasTarihi,1,2) like '%-%' or substring(@BasTarihi,1,2) like '%/%' then '0'+@BasTarihi end
--set @BitTarihi=case when substring(@BitTarihi,1,2) like '%.%' or substring(@BitTarihi,1,2) like '%-%' or substring(@BitTarihi,1,2) like '%/%' then '0'+@BitTarihi end

--DECLARE @BasTarihi1 DATETIME, @BitTarihi1 DATETIME

--set @BasTarihi=SUBSTRING(@BasTarihi,1,4)+'/'+SUBSTRING(@BasTarihi,4,2)+'/'+SUBSTRING(@BasTarihi,1,2)
--set @BitTarihi=SUBSTRING(@BitTarihi,1,4)+'/'+SUBSTRING(@BitTarihi,4,2)+'/'+SUBSTRING(@BitTarihi,1,2)
set @sql='
 select U.ID, 
		BASTAR=CONVERT(DATE,U.BASTAR,102),
		 STOKKODU=(select KOD from STOKLAR where ID=U.STOKID), 
		 STOKADI=(select STOKADI from STOKLAR where ID=U.STOKID), 
		 PROJEKODU=ISNULL(R.FIRMA,''),--ISNULL((select PROJEKODU from PROJELER P where P.ID=U.PROJEID),''),  
		 ANAKAYNAKAD = (SELECT ACIKLAMA FROM LOKASYON WHERE ID=U.ANAKAYNAK),
		 EMIRNO,
		 EMIRTURU=G.ANAHTAR,
		 TALEPTARIHI=CONVERT(DATE,TALEPTARIHI,102),
		 ADET,
		 DURUM	= G1.ANAHTAR, --CASE WHEN ISNULL(DURUM,1)=1 THEN ''Aktif'' ELSE ''Pasif'' END	   
		 DISKAYNAKOLAY=CASE WHEN UF.OLAY=0 THEN ''Çýkýþ'' when UF.OLAY=1 THEN ''Giriþ'' else '''' end,		 
		 DISKAYNAKTARIH=UF.TARIH,
		 DISKAYNAKCARI=R1.FIRMA,
		 DISKAYNAKMIKTAR=CASE WHEN ISNULL(UF.OLAY,0)=0 THEN UF.CIKAN else UF.GIREN end,
		 FASONBUTON=23,
		 SP.SIPARISTARIH,
		 FASONSAY=(SELECT COUNT(ID) FROM URETIMOPERASYONFASON UF1 WHERE UF1.URETIMEMRIID=U.ID ),
		 FASONTIPI=(SELECT TOP 1 ANAHTAR FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2799 AND DEGER=UF.FASONTIPI),
		 S.URUNNO,
		 
		 YORUMMEDYATARIH=GY.EKLEMETARIHI,
		 YORUMMEDYAYORUM=TRIM(char(10) from GY.YORUM),
		 YORUMMEDYAEKLEYEN=(SELECT TOP 1 R.FIRMA FROM REHBER R WHERE R.ID = GY.EKLEYEN),
		 DETAYBUTON=23,
		 YORUMSAY=(SELECT COUNT(GY1.ID) FROM GOREVYORUM GY1 LEFT OUTER JOIN URETIMOPERASYON UOP on GY1.GOREVID=UOP.ID WHERE UOP.URETIMEMRIID=U.ID AND GY1.TUR=142)
 from URETIMEMRI U left outer JOIN GENINI G ON G.DEGER=U.EMIRTURU AND BOLUM=-2488
					LEFT OUTER JOIN GENINI G1 ON  G1.DEGER=U.DURUM AND G1.BOLUM<>0 and G1.BOLUM = -1001 and  G1.DIL = -1
					LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID=U.REHBERID
					LEFT OUTER JOIN URETIMOPERASYONFASON UF ON UF.URETIMEMRIID=U.ID AND UF.ID=(SELECT TOP 1 ID FROM URETIMOPERASYONFASON UF1 WHERE UF1.URETIMEMRIID=U.ID ORDER BY TARIH DESC)
					LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBER R1 ON R1.ID = UF.REHBERID	
					left OUTER join STOKLAR S on S.ID=U.STOKID
					LEFT OUTER JOIN GOREVYORUM GY ON GY.TUR=142 and GY.ID=(SELECT TOP 1 GY1.ID FROM GOREVYORUM GY1 LEFT OUTER JOIN URETIMOPERASYON UOP on GY1.GOREVID=UOP.ID WHERE UOP.URETIMEMRIID=U.ID AND GY1.TUR=142   ORDER BY GY1.EKLEMETARIHI DESC)	
					left join SIPARIS SP ON SP.SIPARISNO = U.SIPARIS_NO										

 where 1=1 
 and convert(date,U.BASTAR,102) between convert(date,'''+@BasTarihi+''',102) and convert(date,'''+@BitTarihi+''',102)'
 
 if @EMIRNO<>'' begin set @sql=@sql+'and EMIRNO = '''+@EMIRNO+'''' end 
 if @kalmayan=0 begin set @sql=@sql+'and U.DURUM > 0' end 
 
 set @sql=@sql+' Order By EMIRNO DESC'

 EXEC (@SQL)

end








===== PROC: TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaDetay =====







--select * from URETIMEMRI where ID=20843
--SELECT * FROM URETIMOPERASYON where URETIMEMRIID=20843
--select * from URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL where OPERASYONID=21968
--SELECT * FROM URETIMOLCUM WHERE OPERASYONPERSONELID=105346

--  exec [TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaDetay] 11069

CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaDetay](@ID INT)
AS
BEGIN 
select 
	BASLAMA=CONVERT(DATE,UO.BASLAMA,102),
	BITIS=CONVERT(DATE,UO.BITIS,102),
	BASLAMAT=CONVERT(VARCHAR(5),UO.BASLAMA,108),
	BITIST=CONVERT(VARCHAR(5),UO.BITIS,108),
	MOLA=CONVERT(VARCHAR(5),UO.MOLA,108),
	SURE=FORMAT(DATEADD(minute, DATEDIFF(MINUTE,CONVERT(VARCHAR(5),UO.BASLAMA,108),CONVERT(VARCHAR(5),UO.BITIS,108)), ''), 'HH:mm'),
	UO.KONUSU,
	UO.PERSONEL,
	UO.LOKASYON,
	UO.KAYNAK,
	UO.ADET,
	BIRIM=BRM.ACIKLAMA,
	DURUM=CASE WHEN  ISNULL(UO.DURUM,0)=0 THEN 'Bekliyor' 
				WHEN  ISNULL(UO.DURUM,0)=1 THEN 'Çalýþýlýyor' 
				WHEN  ISNULL(UO.DURUM,0)=9 THEN 'Tamamlandý' END,
	UO.ACIKLAMA,
	UO.ID,
	----SURE=[dbo].[fn_TarihFarkiFormatli]( BASLAMA, BITIS ),
	SORUMLUADI=(select R.FIRMA from REHBER R where UO.PERSONEL=R.ID),
	LOKASYONADI=(select L1.ACIKLAMA from LOKASYON L1 where UO.LOKASYON=L1.ID),
	KAYNAKADI=(select L1.ACIKLAMA from LOKASYON L1 where UO.KAYNAK=L1.ID),
	OLCUMADEDI=(SELECT COUNT(ID) FROM URETIMOLCUM WHERE OPERASYONPERSONELID=UO.ID),
	LOTU,
	SICAKLIGI,
	VOLTAJ,
	RECETESURE,
	SIRA,
	ISKARTA,
	URETILEN=UO.ADET,
	BIRIM2
from URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL UO INNER JOIN URETIMOPERASYON T0 ON UO.OPERASYONID=T0.ID
			LEFT OUTER JOIN (SELECT FILTRE='BIRIM', ROOTKOD='', KOD=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),DEGER), ID=SIRA, ACIKLAMA=ANAHTAR, TUR='00', REHBERID=-1 FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2702) BRM ON BRM.KOD=UO.BIRIM
where 
T0.ID=@ID
ORDER BY SIRA, 1 DESC, 3 DESC
end










===== PROC: TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaDetayGuncelle =====








--  exec TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaDetayGuncelle '{OPID}','{BASLAMA}','{BITIS}','{MOLA}','{KONUSU}','{PERSONEL}','{LOKASYON}','{KAYNAK}','{ADET}','{BIRIM}','{DURUM}','{ACIKLAMA}','23'
--  exec TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaDetay 5

--exec TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaDetayGuncelle '{OPID}','{BASLAMA}','{BITIS}','{MOLA}','{KONUSU}','{PERSONEL}','{LOKASYON}','{KAYNAK}','{ADET}','{BIRIM}','{DURUM}','{ACIKLAMA}','{ID}','{BASLAMAT}','{BITIST}','{SORUMLUADI}'

CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaDetayGuncelle](@OPID INT, @BasTar varchar(10), @BitTar varchar(10), @mola varchar(5), @konusu varchar(200), @sorumluid varchar(200), 
				@lokasyon varchar(250), @kaynak varchar(250), @adet float, @birim varchar(20), @durum varchar(20), @aciklama varchar(250), @ID INT, @BasTarT varchar(5), @BitTarT varchar(5), @SORUMLUADI VARCHAR(100), 
					@LOTU VARCHAR(100),
					@SICAKLIGI	VARCHAR(100),
					@VOLTAJ	VARCHAR(100),
					@RECETESURE VARCHAR(100),
				@ISKARTA VARCHAR(100),
				@URETILEN float, @BIRIM2 varchar(20),
				@SIRA FLOAT
				
				)
AS
BEGIN 

set @LOTU = case when @LOTU='' THEN NULL ELSE @LOTU END
set @SICAKLIGI = case when @SICAKLIGI='' THEN NULL ELSE @SICAKLIGI END
set @VOLTAJ = case when @VOLTAJ='' THEN NULL ELSE @VOLTAJ END
set @RECETESURE = case when @RECETESURE='' THEN NULL ELSE @RECETESURE END

declare @YERID INT
		
BEGIN TRY

--SET @MOLA='1899-12-30 '+@MOLA 

IF @ID IN (SELECT ID FROM URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL UO WHERE UO.ID=@ID)
begin
		UPDATE UO SET 
			BASLAMA		=@BasTar +' '+@BasTarT,--convert(datetime,(substring(@BasTar,7,4)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,4,2)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,1,2) +' '+@BasTarT)), 
			BITIS		=@BitTar+' '+@BitTarT,--convert(datetime,(substring(@BitTar,7,4)+'-'+substring(@BitTar,4,2)+'-'+substring(@BitTar,1,2) +' '+@BitTarT)),  
			MOLA		=('1899-12-30 '+@mola), 
			KONUSU		=@konusu , 
			PERSONEL	=@sorumluid , 
			LOKASYON	=@lokasyon , 
			KAYNAK		=@kaynak , 
			ADET		=@URETILEN , 
			BIRIM		=(SELECT top 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2702 AND ANAHTAR=@BIRIM2) , 
			DURUM		=  CASE --WHEN ISNULL(@BitTar,'1900-01-01')<>'' or ISNULL(@BitTarT,'00:00')<>'' THEN 9 
								WHEN  ISNULL(@durum,0)='Bekliyor' THEN  '0'
								WHEN  ISNULL(@durum,0)='Çalýþýlýyor'  THEN '1'
								WHEN  ISNULL(@durum,0)='Tamamlandý' THEN '9' else '8' END,  
								
			ACIKLAMA	=@aciklama,
			LOTU=@LOTU,			
			RECETESURE=@RECETESURE,
			ISKARTA=@ISKARTA 
		from URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL UO 
		where 
		UO.ID=@ID
		
		exec TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaDetay @OPID
		--select SONUC='Güncelleme Baþarýlý.'--CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@ID)
end else
begin
		SET @YERID = (SELECT top 1 UO.ID FROM URETIMOPERASYON U INNER JOIN URETIMRECETEOPR UO ON UO.URETIMRECETEID=U.RECETEID WHERE U.ID = @OPID AND UO.KONUSU=@konusu)

		INSERT INTO URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL(BASLAMA,BITIS,MOLA, KONUSU, PERSONEL, LOKASYON, KAYNAK, ADET, BIRIM, DURUM, ACIKLAMA, OPERASYONID, YER, YERID, LOTU, RECETESURE, ISKARTA, SIRA)					 							
		VALUES(
				@BasTar +' '+@BasTarT,--convert(datetime,(substring(@BasTar,7,4)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,4,2)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,1,2) +' '+@BasTarT)), 
				@BitTar+' '+@BitTarT,--convert(datetime,(substring(@BitTar,7,4)+'-'+substring(@BitTar,4,2)+'-'+substring(@BitTar,1,2) +' '+@BitTarT)), 
				convert(time,@mola), 
				@konusu, 
				@sorumluid, 
				@lokasyon, 
				@kaynak, 
				@URETILEN, 
				(SELECT top 1 DEGER FROM GENINI WHERE BOLUM = -2702 AND ANAHTAR=@BIRIM2), 
				CASE WHEN  ISNULL(@durum,0)='Bekliyor' THEN  0
								WHEN  ISNULL(@durum,0)='Çalýþýlýyor'  THEN 1
								WHEN  ISNULL(@durum,0)='Tamamlandý' THEN 9 END, 
				@aciklama, @OPID,1,@YERID,@LOTU,@RECETESURE, @ISKARTA,  
				@SIRA
				)
		exec TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaDetay @OPID 
		--select 'Kayýt Giriþi Baþarýlý.'
end

END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
		SELECT 'Hata oluþtu....'+ERROR_MESSAGE()--+' - '+ISNULL(convert(datetime,(substring(@BasTar,7,4)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,4,2)+'-'+substring(@BasTar,1,2) +' '+@BasTarT)),'')
END CATCH
end






--set @bastar=case when substring(@BasTar,1,2) like '%.%' or substring(@BasTar,1,2) like '%-%' then '0'+@BasTar end
--set @BitTar=case when substring(@BitTar,1,2) like '%.%' or substring(@BitTar,1,2) like '%-%' then '0'+@BitTar end
