===== PROC: TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaDurumGuncelle =====








--  exec [TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaDurumGuncelle] '237969'


CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaDurumGuncelle](@UID VARCHAR(20), @DURUM VARCHAR(100))
AS
BEGIN 
 UPDATE  URETIMEMRI SET DURUM= (SELECT TOP 1 DEGER FROM GENINI G1 where   G1.BOLUM<>0 and G1.BOLUM = -1001 and  G1.DIL = -1 AND ANAHTAR=@DURUM)   WHERE EMIRNO=@UID
end


--select * from URETIMEMRI where EMIRNO='237969'







===== PROC: TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaEkalanlar =====


--  exec [TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaEkalanlar] '237058','Listele',''
--select ISNULL(UrunMiktari1,''),* from URETIMEMRI U where EMIRNO='237058'

CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaEkalanlar](@EMIRNO VARCHAR(50), @TIP VARCHAR(20), @JSON VARCHAR(8000) )
AS
BEGIN 
DECLARE @SQL VARCHAR(MAX), @alanadlari varchar(8000)
IF @TIP= 'Listele'
begin

set @alanadlari=''

		select 
			@alanadlari=@alanadlari +SC.NAME+',' 
		from SYS.OBJECTS SO INNER JOIN SYS.COLUMNS SC  ON SO.object_id=SC.object_id AND SO.NAME='URETIMEMRI'
						 
						 
						order by sc.name
		set @alanadlari=substring(@alanadlari,1,len(@alanadlari)-1)
		print @alanadlari

		set @sql='SELECT '
		--select @SQL=@SQL+SC.NAME+'=ISNULL('+SC.NAME+',''''),' from SYSOBJECTS SO INNER JOIN SYSCOLUMNS SC ON SO.ID=SC.ID AND SO.NAME='URETIMEMRI'
		select @SQL=@SQL+SC.NAME+'='''',' from SYSOBJECTS SO INNER JOIN SYSCOLUMNS SC ON SO.ID=SC.ID AND SO.NAME='URETIMEMRI' order by sc.name
		set @sql=substring(@sql,1,len(@sql)-1)
		print @sql

		--SET @SQL = @SQL +' from URETIMEMRI U where EMIRNO='''+@EMIRNO+''''
		SET @SQL = @SQL +'UNION ALL select '+@alanadlari+' from URETIMEMRI U where EMIRNO='''+@EMIRNO+''' order by ID  '

		EXEC (@sql)
end
IF @TIP= 'Guncelle'
begin
BEGIN TRY

set @sql=''
select 
	@SQL=@SQL+'['+SC.NAME+'] nvarchar'/*+t.name*/+case when t.precision=0 then '('+convert(varchar(10),sc.max_length)+')' else '(4)' end +' ''$.'+SC.NAME+''',' 
from SYS.OBJECTS SO INNER JOIN SYS.COLUMNS SC  ON SO.object_id=SC.object_id AND SO.NAME='URETIMEMRI'
					INNER JOIN sys.types t ON t.user_type_id = SC.user_type_id
					INNER JOIN ALANLAR ON EKRANADI= 'UretimEmriWizardDlg'  AND TABLO=SO.NAME AND ALANADI=SC.name AND TUR IN ('4','1')
set @sql=substring(@sql,1,len(@sql)-1)

set @alanadlari=''

select 
	@alanadlari=@alanadlari+'U.['+SC.NAME+']=case WHEN D.['+SC.NAME+']='''' THEN NULL ELSE D.['+SC.NAME+'] END,' 
from SYS.OBJECTS SO INNER JOIN SYS.COLUMNS SC  ON SO.object_id=SC.object_id AND SO.NAME='URETIMEMRI'
					INNER JOIN sys.types t ON t.user_type_id = SC.user_type_id
					INNER JOIN ALANLAR ON EKRANADI= 'UretimEmriWizardDlg'  AND TABLO=SO.NAME AND ALANADI=SC.name AND TUR IN ('4','1')
set @alanadlari=substring(@alanadlari,1,len(@alanadlari)-1)


	--set @sql='INSERT INTO URETIMEMRITEST('+@alanadlari+') 
	SET @sql='update U SET '+@alanadlari+' FROM OPENJSON('''+@json+''',''$."FDMemUretimEkAlanlar"'') WITH ('+@sql+') D , URETIMEMRI U where U.EMIRNO='''+@EMIRNO+''''
	
	print @sql	
	EXEC (@sql)

	select DURUM=1,ACIKLAMA='Baþarýyla Kaydedildi.'


END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
		select DURUM=0,ACIKLAMA='Hata oluþtu. Transfer gerçekleþmedi.'	
END CATCH


END
end








===== PROC: TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaEkalanlarSatir =====

--SELECT * FROM URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL WHERE OPERASYONID=25417
--  exec [TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaEkalanlarSatir] '176169','Listele',''
--select ISNULL(UrunMiktari1,''),* from URETIMEMRI U where EMIRNO='237058'

CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaEkalanlarSatir](@ID VARCHAR(50), @TIP VARCHAR(20), @JSON VARCHAR(8000) )
AS
BEGIN 
DECLARE @SQL VARCHAR(MAX), @alanadlari varchar(8000)
IF @TIP= 'Listele'
begin

		set @alanadlari=''

		select @alanadlari=@alanadlari + SC.NAME+',' from SYS.OBJECTS SO INNER JOIN SYS.COLUMNS SC  ON SO.object_id=SC.object_id AND SO.NAME='URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL'  order by sc.name
			
		set @alanadlari=substring(@alanadlari,1,len(@alanadlari)-1)	

		set @sql='SELECT '
	
		select @SQL=@SQL+case when sc.system_type_id IN (48,52,56,62,106) then SC.NAME+'=0,' ELSE SC.NAME+'='''',' END from SYS.OBJECTS SO INNER JOIN SYS.COLUMNS SC ON SO.object_id=SC.object_id AND SO.NAME='URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL'  order by sc.name
	PRINT @SQL
		set @sql=substring(@sql,1,len(@sql)-1)
		
		SET @SQL = @SQL +'UNION ALL select '+@alanadlari+' from URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL U where ID='''+@ID+''' order by ID  '		

		EXEC (@sql)
end
IF @TIP= 'Guncelle'
begin
BEGIN TRY
INSERT INTO TMLOG(ISLEM, JSONT) VALUES('URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL',@JSON)
set @sql=''
select 
	@SQL=@SQL+'['+SC.NAME+'] nvarchar'/*+t.name*/+case when t.precision=0 then '('+convert(varchar(10),sc.max_length)+')' else '(4)' end +' ''$.'+SC.NAME+''',' 
from SYS.OBJECTS SO INNER JOIN SYS.COLUMNS SC  ON SO.object_id=SC.object_id AND SO.NAME='URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL'
					INNER JOIN sys.types t ON t.user_type_id = SC.user_type_id
					INNER JOIN ALANLAR ON EKRANADI= 'IsEmriPersonelZamanDlg'  AND TABLO=SO.NAME AND ALANADI=SC.name AND TUR IN ('4','1')
set @sql=substring(@sql,1,len(@sql)-1)

set @alanadlari=''

select 
	@alanadlari=@alanadlari+'U.['+SC.NAME+']=case WHEN D.['+SC.NAME+']='''' THEN NULL ELSE D.['+SC.NAME+'] END,' 
from SYS.OBJECTS SO INNER JOIN SYS.COLUMNS SC  ON SO.object_id=SC.object_id AND SO.NAME='URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL'
					INNER JOIN sys.types t ON t.user_type_id = SC.user_type_id
					INNER JOIN ALANLAR ON EKRANADI= 'IsEmriPersonelZamanDlg'  AND TABLO=SO.NAME AND ALANADI=SC.name AND TUR IN ('4','1')
set @alanadlari=substring(@alanadlari,1,len(@alanadlari)-1)


	--set @sql='INSERT INTO URETIMEMRITEST('+@alanadlari+') 
	SET @sql='update U SET '+@alanadlari+' FROM OPENJSON('''+@json+''',''$."FDMemUretimEkAlanlar"'') WITH ('+@sql+') D , URETIMOPERASYONPERSONEL U where U.ID='''+@ID+''''
	
	print @sql	
	EXEC (@sql)

	select DURUM=1,ACIKLAMA='Baþarýyla Kaydedildi.'


END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
		select DURUM=0,ACIKLAMA='Hata oluþtu. Transfer gerçekleþmedi.'	
END CATCH


END
end








===== PROC: TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaOpr =====



--  exec TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaOpr 23880

CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_UretimPersonelZamanPlanlaOpr](@ID INT)
AS
BEGIN 
 SELECT
	   T0.ID,
       T1.EMIRNO AS EMIRNO,
       T5.KOD AS UrunKodu,
	   T2.URUNNO AS URUNNO,
       T0.URETIMEMRIID,
       T2.KOD AS StokKodu,
       T2.STOKADI AS UretilecekStok,
       T3.FIRMA AS Personel,
       CAST(T0.BASTAR AS DATE) AS BaslamaPlan,
       CAST(T0.BITTAR AS DATE) AS BitisPlan,
       T0.ADET AS Adet,
       T4.ANAHTAR AS Birim,
       T6.DEPOADI AS GirisDepo,
       T7.DEPOADI AS CikisDepo,
       T0.RECETEID,
       T0.RECETEDETAYID,
	   IMAJ=(SELECT TOP 1 23 FROM IMAJ I WHERE t2.ID=I.YER_ID AND YERI=88),
	   DOKUMAN=(SELECT TOP 1 25 FROM GOREVYORUM GI INNER JOIN DOKUMAN D ON D.MODULID=GI.ID AND D.KLASOR=-88 INNER JOIN IMAJ I ON I.YER_ID=D.ID and I.YERI=1 WHERE GI.GOREVID=T2.ID AND GI.TUR=88),
	   RECETELISTE ='{"success":true,"results":'+
	   (SELECT KONUSU, SIRA FROM [dbo].[URETIMRECETEOPR] UO WHERE UO.URETIMRECETEID = T0.RECETEID ORDER BY UO.SIRA FOR JSON PATH)+'}'
FROM
       URETIMOPERASYON T0
              INNER JOIN URETIMEMRI T1 ON T1.ID = T0.URETIMEMRIID
              INNER JOIN STOKLAR T2 ON T2.ID = T0.STOKID
              INNER JOIN REHBER T3 ON T3.ID = T0.PERSONEL
              INNER JOIN GENINI T4 ON T4.BOLUM = -2702 AND T4.DEGER = T0.BIRIM
              INNER JOIN STOKLAR T5 ON T5.ID = T1.STOKID
              INNER JOIN DEPOLAR T6 ON T6.ID = T0.GIRISDEPO
              INNER JOIN DEPOLAR T7 ON T7.ID = T0.CIKISDEPO
			  
 WHERE
	T1.DURUM > 0 AND
	T1.ID=@ID


end







===== PROC: TM_UserGiris =====



-- exec TM_UserGiris 'erhan@granit.com.tr',''
CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_UserGiris](@KADI VARCHAR(50), @CIHAZID VARCHAR(250)=NULL)
AS
BEGIN 
INSERT INTO TMLOG(ISLEM, KULLANICIADI, CIHAZID) VALUES('LOGIN',@KADI, @CIHAZID)

SELECT        KL.ID, KL.REHBERID, SIFRE, KL.KOD, ROLID, KL.DURUM, KL.SUBEID, DIL, TOKEN='', KULLANICIADI=@KADI, GRUP='-1' , R.FIRMA 
FROM            KULLANICI KL INNER JOIN REHBER R ON R.ID=KL.REHBERID
							 LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERILETISIM RI ON RI.REHBERID=R.ID
							 LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RB.YER_ID=RI.ID AND RB.ETIKET='Eposta'
where KL.DURUM=1 AND (R.FIRMA = @KADI or ISNULL(RB.BILGI,'')=@KADI)


UNION ALL
SELECT        ID=0, REHBERID=R1.ID, SIFRE=ISNULL(RB.BILGI,''), KOD='0', ROLID=0, DURUM=1, SUBEID=-1, DIL=-1, TOKEN='', KULLANICIADI=@KADI, R1.GRUP , R1.FIRMA
FROM            REHBER R INNER JOIN REHBER R1 ON R.BAGID=R1.ID 
							 LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERILETISIM RI ON RI.REHBERID=R.ID
							 LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB ON RB.YER_ID=RI.ID AND RB.ETIKET='Eposta'
							 LEFT OUTER JOIN REHBERBILGI RB1 ON RB1.YER_ID=RI.ID AND RB1.ETIKET='Web'
where  (R.FIRMA = @KADI or ISNULL(RB.BILGI,'')=@KADI) AND R1.GRUP=951

end
 

===== PROC: TM_Whatsappcontacts =====





CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_Whatsappcontacts](@JSON nvarchar(max))
AS
BEGIN 
set @json=replace(@json,'''','''''''')
--exec TM_Whatsappmesaj @json
TRUNCATE TABLE TMWHATSAPPCONTACTS
INSERT INTO TMWHATSAPPCONTACTS(number,isBusiness,isEnterprise,namex,pushname,shortName,typex,isMe,isUser,isGroup,isWAContact,isMyContact,isBlocked,serverx,userx,serialized)
select * from OPENJSON(@json,'$."contacts"') 
				WITH (
					number VARCHAR(150) '$.number',
					isBusiness VARCHAR(150) '$.isBusiness',
					isEnterprise VARCHAR(8000) '$.isEnterprise',
					namex nVARCHAR(150) '$.name',
					pushname nVARCHAR(150) '$.pushname',
					shortName nVARCHAR(150) '$.shortName',
					typex VARCHAR(150) '$.type',
					isMe VARCHAR(150) '$.isMe',
					isUser VARCHAR(150) '$.isUser',
					isGroup VARCHAR(150) '$.isGroup',
					isWAContact VARCHAR(150) '$.isWAContact',
					isMyContact VARCHAR(150) '$.isMyContact',
					isBlocked VARCHAR(150) '$.isBlocked',					
					serverx VARCHAR(150) '$.id.server',
					userx VARCHAR(150) '$.id.user',
					serialized VARCHAR(150) '$.id._serialized'
) D 
where 
serverx<>'lid' --and namex like '%seçil%'
end


===== PROC: TM_Whatsappmesaj =====




CREATE procedure [dbo].[TM_Whatsappmesaj](@JSON NVARCHAR(MAX))
AS
BEGIN 


INSERT INTO TMLOG(JSONM) VALUES(@JSON)

INSERT INTO TMWHATSAPPMESAJCHAT(ack,hasMedia,body,typex,timestampx,fromx,tox,author,deviceType,isForwarded,forwardingScore,isStatus,isStarred,fromMe,hasQuotedMsg,hasReaction,vCards,mesajid,serialized,notifyName)
select * from OPENJSON(@json,'$."body"') 
				WITH (
					ack VARCHAR(150) '$.ack',
					hasMedia VARCHAR(150) '$.hasMedia',
					body nVARCHAR(max) '$.body',
					[typex] VARCHAR(150) '$.type',
					[timestampx] VARCHAR(150) '$.timestamp',
					[fromx] VARCHAR(150) '$.from',
					[tox] VARCHAR(150) '$.to',
					author VARCHAR(150) '$.author',
					deviceType VARCHAR(150) '$.deviceType',
					isForwarded VARCHAR(150) '$.isForwarded',
					forwardingScore VARCHAR(150) '$.forwardingScore',
					isStatus VARCHAR(150) '$.isStatus',
					isStarred VARCHAR(150) '$.isStarred',
					fromMe VARCHAR(150) '$.fromMe',
					hasQuotedMsg VARCHAR(150) '$.hasQuotedMsg',
					hasReaction VARCHAR(150) '$.hasReaction',
					vCards VARCHAR(150) '$.vCards',
					id VARCHAR(150) '$.id.id',
					serialized VARCHAR(150) '$.id._serialized',
					notifyName VARCHAR(150) '$._data.notifyName'
) D 

select * from OPENJSON(@json,'$."body"') 
				WITH (
					body nVARCHAR(max) '$.body',
					fromMe VARCHAR(150) '$.fromMe',
					[fromx] VARCHAR(150) '$.from',
					[typex] VARCHAR(150) '$.type'
) D where
typex NOT IN ('e2e_notification','notification_template')

end
